Result: 研究表明，PtMP IPoWDM方案在多种地理类型场景中显著优化了效率。与传统基准方案相比，该架构在十年周期内实现了CAPEX降低92.0%、功耗降低99.2%的显著成效。

Conclusion: 基于DSCM的点对多点IPoWDM架构在接入-城域网络中具有显著优势，能够大幅降低资本支出和运营功耗，是一种高效、经济、节能的网络解决方案。

Abstract: This study compares transponder-based, Point-to-Point, and DSCM-based Point-to-Multipoint (PtMP) access-metro architectures. Findings demonstrate that PtMP IPoWDM significantly optimizes efficiency across diverse geotypes, slashing CAPEX by 92.0% and power by 99.2% compared to the traditional benchmark over a ten-year horizon.

</details>


### [36] [Comparative Analysis of Direct-to-Cell (D2C) and 3GPP Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN) for Global Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05843)
*Donglin Wang,Anjie Qiu,Qiuheng Zhou,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了D2C和3GPP NTN两种卫星通信架构，指出NTN在性能、安全性和扩展性方面更具优势，是6G卫星-地面融合的基础框架，而结合两者优势的混合模式是实现全球连接的最优路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现无处不在的移动覆盖，业界出现了两种截然不同的架构范式：D2C（直接到手机）和标准化的3GPP非地面网络（NTN）。本文旨在对这两种方法进行全面的技术比较，分析它们在标准化进程、网络架构、物理层创新、安全态势和运营权衡等方面的差异，并探讨其对新兴6G用例的影响。

Method: 本文从多个技术维度对D2C和3GPP NTN进行了系统性比较分析，包括：标准化轨迹对比（D2C的市场驱动模式 vs NTN在3GPP R17-19中的系统化框架）、网络架构分析、物理层创新研究、安全态势评估以及运营权衡分析。此外，还研究了两种架构在6G应用场景（特别是自动驾驶）中的具体应用，提出了混合三链路架构方案。

Result: 分析结果表明：D2C通过兼容传统设备实现了快速市场准入，适合作为紧急连接的叠加层；而NTN在性能、安全性和可扩展性方面表现更优，更适合长期发展。在自动驾驶等安全关键型应用中，结合地面5G、NTN宽带和D2C紧急回退的混合三链路架构能够提供必要的冗余保障。

Conclusion: NTN凭借其卓越的性能、安全性和可扩展性，被定位为6G卫星-地面融合的基础框架。然而，结合两种范式优势的混合模型被认为是最具实用性的全球连接实现路径，能够在保证技术先进性的同时兼顾市场部署的现实需求。

Abstract: The quest for ubiquitous mobile coverage has catalyzed two fundamentally distinct architectural paradigms: Direct-to-Cell (D2C) and standardized 3GPP Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN). D2C, pioneered by SpaceX Starlink and AST SpaceMobile, leverages existing terrestrial spectrum and unmodified consumer handsets to provide emergency connectivity as a market-driven overlay. In contrast, 3GPP NTN, standardized across Releases 17-19, offers a systematic satellite-native framework designed for long-term scalability, high-throughput broadband, and deep integration with terrestrial 5G/6G networks. This paper presents a comprehensive technical comparison of these approaches, analyzing their standardization trajectories, network architectures, physical-layer innovations, security postures, and operational trade-offs. We further examine their implications for emerging 6G use cases, particularly autonomous driving, where safety-critical redundancy motivates a hybrid tri-link architecture combining terrestrial 5G, NTN broadband, and D2C emergency fallback. Our analysis shows that, although D2C enables rapid market entry through legacy-device compatibility, NTN provides superior performance, security, and scalability, positioning it as the foundational framework for 6G satellite-terrestrial convergence. A hybrid model that combines the strengths of both paradigms is identified as the most practical path toward truly global connectivity.

</details>


### [37] [A Disaster-Aware Integrated TN-NTN System-Level Simulator for Resilient 6G Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05852)
*Donglin Wang,Anjie Qiu,Qiuheng Zhou,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个轻量级的系统级仿真器，用于评估在灾难导致的局部故障模型下，地面网络（TN）与非地面网络（NTN）融合系统的故障后回退行为与韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着3GPP将非地面网络（NTN，如LEO卫星、HAPS、无人机）标准化为未来6G系统的关键组成部分，亟需研究在极端事件和灾难场景下，NTN如何辅助地面网络（TN）以增强网络覆盖与韧性。目前缺乏符合3GPP标准的轻量化工具来评估融合网络在部分故障下的性能。

Method: 提出了一种遵循3GPP Rel-17/18建模原则的轻量级系统级仿真器。该仿真器支持概率性地面基站（gNB）故障建模，以及服务向NTN的迁移。通过改变用户负载、灾难严重程度和NTN资源配置水平，对比分析网络的吞吐量、分组接收率（PRR）和延迟。

Result: 仿真结果揭示了地面运行模式下容量与延迟的权衡关系，非地面服务模式的高可靠性与稳定性，以及混合TN-NTN运行模式下平衡的韧性表现。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究未来集成6G移动系统的无线网络韧性和流量管理提供了一个易于处理的工具，验证了TN-NTN融合网络在灾难场景下的优势。

Abstract: Non-terrestrial networks (NTN) have been standardized by the 3rd generation partnership project (3GPP) as a key component of future 6G systems to enhance coverage and resilience. In particular, NTN technologies such as low-earth orbit (LEO) satellites, high-altitude platform stations (HAPS), and unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are expected to support terrestrial networks (TN) during extreme events and disasters. In this paper, we present a lightweight system-level simulator for evaluating post-failure fallback behavior in integrated TN-NTN wireless networks under a partial-failure disaster model. The simulator follows 3GPP Rel-17/18 modeling principles, supports probabilistic terrestrial next-generation node B (gNB) failures, and service migration to NTN. The simulator supports comparative analysis of throughput, packet reception ratio (PRR), and latency under different user loads, disaster severities, and NTN provisioning levels. Results show the expected capacity-delay tradeoff of terrestrial operation, the reliability and stability of non-terrestrial service, and the balanced resilience behavior of hybrid TN-NTN operation. The proposed framework provides a tractable tool for studying wireless network resilience and traffic management in future integrated 6G mobile systems.

</details>


### [38] [FluxShard: Motion-Aware Feature Cache Reuse for Collaborative Video Analytics in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06027)
*Xiuxian Guan,Zongyuan Zhang,Zheng Lin,Zekai Sun,Tianyang Duan,Zihan Fang,Rui Wang,Heming Cui,Wei Ni,Jun Luo,Yuanwei Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出FluxShard系统，利用编解码器块级运动向量(MV)对特征缓存进行运动感知的细粒度管理，解决非均匀运动场景下整场景粒度缓存失效问题，显著降低边缘云视频分析的延迟与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘云视频分析方法在固定或全局坐标系下以整场景粒度管理特征缓存。在移动场景的非均匀运动模式下，即使大部分内容仅发生空间位移，这种粗粒度管理也会导致大部分缓存失效，造成计算与带宽浪费。根本原因在于粒度不匹配：缓存以场景为单位管理，而运动却因区域而异。

Method: 提出FluxShard系统，利用编解码器块级运动向量(MV)实现基于运动区域的细粒度特征缓存复用。核心方法包括：(1)沿块级MV重新索引缓存特征，将空间位移与内容变化分离；(2)提出感受野对齐原则(RFAP)，仅根据输入级MV场识别因感受野内空间组成不一致而必须重计算的位置；(3)采用MV引导的缓存重映射，每帧将特征缓存扭曲到当前坐标系以维持缓存一致性；(4)基于性能分析的调度器将稀疏工作负载分配至边缘或云端以降低延迟。

Result: 在多种视觉任务、动态视频基准和网络条件下评估，相比所有基线方法，FluxShard在规定精度预算下延迟降低32.6-83.8%，能耗降低14.9-64.0%。

Conclusion: FluxShard通过运动感知的细粒度缓存管理有效解决了非均匀运动场景下的缓存失效问题，在保证精度的前提下显著降低了边缘云视频分析的延迟与能耗，证明了基于运动区域管理缓存的有效性。

Abstract: Caching and reusing intermediate features across consecutive frames is a common technique to reduce redundant computation and transmission for edge-cloud video analytics in mobile edge computation. Existing methods manage the cache in a fixed or globally shifted coordinate system, treating it as an indivisible whole. Under the non-uniform motion patterns of mobile scenes, this whole-scene granularity invalidates large portions of the cache even when most content has merely shifted spatially, wasting computation and bandwidth. The root cause is a granularity mismatch: the cache is managed per scene, yet motion varies per region. In this paper, we present FluxShard, a motion-aware edge-cloud video analytics system that uses codec-level block motion vectors (MVs) to manage feature cache reuse and recomputation at the granularity of individual motion regions. By re-indexing cached features along per-block MVs, FluxShard separates spatial displacement from content changes, recovering reusable content that whole-scene methods would otherwise discard. To ensure correct reuse under heterogeneous motion, the Receptive Field Alignment Principle (RFAP) identifies, from the input-level MV field alone, the positions that must be recomputed due to inconsistent spatial composition within receptive fields. To maintain cache coherence across frames, MV-guided cache remapping warps the entire feature cache to the current coordinate system each frame, sustaining a high reuse ratio over time. A profiling-driven dispatcher routes the remaining sparse workload between edge and cloud for lower latency. Evaluation across multiple vision tasks, dynamic video benchmarks, and network conditions shows that FluxShard reduces latency by 32.6-83.8% and energy by 14.9-64.0% over all baselines under the prescribed accuracy budget.

</details>


### [39] [Temporal Spectral Noise-Floor Adaptation for Error-Intolerant Trigger Integrity in IoT Mesh Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06338)
*Sergii Makovetskyi,Lars Thomsen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种适用于IoT传感器节点的轻量级嵌入式自主边缘事件触发算法，通过在固件中执行确定性FFT频谱特征提取并维护时变频谱噪声底限，在动态环境中有效抑制干扰触发，实现了无需云端推理或共享噪声模型的自适应可靠检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网传感器节点常部署在动态户外环境中，易受雨、风、机械振动等非平稳环境激励干扰，导致误触发频发。现有自适应阈值方案多关注通信负载管理，缺乏对环境非平稳性下的触发可靠性保障。本文旨在解决在无需中心化云端推理、共享噪声模型或频繁校准的前提下，实现节点本地自适应噪声底限维护，以确保事件触发完整性。

Method: 在传感器节点固件中实现确定性FFT（快速傅里叶变换）频谱特征提取；构建并持续维护一个时变频谱噪声底限基线，以吸收非平稳环境激励；基于该动态基线进行本地自主事件决策，无需外部模型或云端参与。

Result: 在单节点网状传感器（采用雷达类近接传感器）的户外动态环境实测中，该方法显著抑制了干扰引起的误触发，降低了网状网络中的虚假事件流量放大，嵌入式执行开销有界，且对真实频谱特征保持了可靠的检测灵敏度。

Conclusion: 所提算法成功实现了在动态非平稳环境下的校准免部署、本地自主决策与可靠事件触发，有效保障了连接间歇性下的节点响应性并缓解了中心化系统的安全风险，为物联网边缘智能提供了一种高效、安全的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a lightweight, embedded algorithm for autonomous edge event triggering in IoT sensor nodes suitable for operating in mesh networks. The device acquires local sensor data, performs deterministic FFT spectral feature extraction in firmware, and maintains a temporal spectral noise-floor baseline that absorbs non-stationary environmental excitations such as rain, wind, and mechanical vibration. While adaptive thresholds in IoT sensor nodes are often applied to manage communication load or stabilize long-term metrics, this work focuses on maintaining a time-evolving spectral noise floor to preserve event trigger reliability in dynamic environments. Our method targets trigger integrity under environmental non-stationary conditions, enabling calibration-free deployment of autonomous nodes; without shared noise models or cloud-side inference. Local decision authority preserves node responsiveness when connectivity is intermittent and mitigates security risks inherent in centralized remote-analysis systems. We validate the algorithm in a single node mesh sensor deployed in a dynamic outdoor environment using a radar-class proximity sensor as one example sensor modality. Results demonstrate substantial suppression of nuisance-induced triggers, reduced false-event traffic amplification in the mesh, bounded embedded execution, and reliable detection sensitivity to true spectral signatures.

</details>

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模蒙特卡洛仿真，证明了TSNFA方法在物联网网状传感器网络中实现实时事件检测的优越性，其核心在于三种防御机制的协同作用。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网网状传感器网络中的实时事件检测面临时变噪声条件、边缘节点计算资源受限以及需要在无中心协调下自主运行等重大挑战，亟需一种高效且鲁棒的检测方法。

Result: TSNFA实现了100%的检测率和零误报率，其独特之处在于结合了三种相互锁定的防御机制：频谱频带选择、时间持久性滤波和自适应噪声底跟踪。所有竞争算法都至少缺少这三种防御机制之一，导致相应失效，误报率范围从0（Send-on-Delta算法，但也无法检测到任何事件）到13,387,930（宽带能量比算法）。

Conclusion: 研究结果确定了这三种防御机制的组合是在资源受限的物联网部署中实现自主边缘触发的必要且充分条件。

Abstract: Real-time event detection in Internet of Things (IoT) mesh sensor networks presents significant challenges due to time-varying noise conditions, limited computational resources at edge nodes, and the need for autonomous operation without centralised coordination. This paper presents a comprehensive Monte Carlo simulation study comparing the Temporal Spectral Noise-Floor Adaptation (TSNFA) method against six alternative detection algorithms, evaluated across a 200-node mesh network over 24 hours with realistic noise models including 60 Hz electromagnetic interference (EMI), sinusoidally drifting noise power (+/- 6 dB), and intermittent digital switching bursts. TSNFA achieves 100% detection rate with zero false positives, uniquely combining three interlocking defences: spectral band selection, temporal persistence filtering, and adaptive noise-floor tracking. Every competing algorithm omits at least one of these three defences and fails correspondingly, with false-positive rates ranging from 0 (Send-on-Delta, which also detects nothing) to 13,387,930 (broadband energy ratio). These results identify the three-defence combination as necessary and sufficient for autonomous edge triggering in resource-constrained IoT deployments.

</details>

*Huaisheng Ye,Xiaowen Ye,Liqun Fu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MobiU-MAC，一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）的水声网络MAC协议。通过引入无需测距且抗延迟的CHILL-STER算法，解决了节点移动性带来的时空不确定性问题，显著提升了网络吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水声网络中存在长传播延迟，导致时空不确定性，限制了MAC协议的信道利用率。而在自主水下航行器（AUV）场景中，节点移动性进一步加剧了这一挑战，引入了动态传播延迟和变化的拓扑结构，使得传统方法难以适用。

Method: 提出了一种基于深度强化学习的MAC协议MobiU-MAC，核心包含名为CHILL-STER的新型DRL算法。该算法具备两大关键机制：一是信用视野受限λ回报（CHILL-Return）机制，用于在异步延迟奖励下实现稳定学习；二是时空经验回放（STER）机制，用于应对节点移动引起的拓扑变化。此外，该方法无需测距（ranging-free），且对延迟具有鲁棒性。

Result: 理论证明了DRL在无需测距的条件下，能够达到与标准马尔可夫决策过程等效的最优策略学习。性能评估表明，MobiU-MAC在无需测距开销的情况下利用最大系统延迟边界，其性能优于现有的基于DRL的水声网络MAC协议。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论与算法在复杂的水下动态环境中具有显著的有效性，成功解决了长传播延迟和节点移动性带来的双重挑战，为移动水声网络提供了高效的接入控制解决方案。

Abstract: Long propagation delays in underwater acoustic networks (UWANs) cause spatio-temporal uncertainty, constraining channel utilization in medium access control (MAC) protocols. Node mobility within autonomous underwater vehicle scenarios exacerbates these challenges by introducing dynamic propagation delays and varying spatial topologies. We present MobiU-MAC, a deep reinforcement learning (DRL)-based MAC protocol for mobile node access in UWANs that maximizes throughput via autonomous learning. MobiU-MAC incorporates CHILL-STER, a novel DRL algorithm optimized for UWANs that is both ranging-free and delay-robust. CHILL-STER employs a credit horizon-limited $λ$-return (CHILL-Return) mechanism to achieve stable learning under asynchronous delayed rewards, while the companion spatio-temporal experience replay (STER) mechanism addresses topological changes arising from node mobility. This work also demonstrates theoretically that DRL attains optimal policy learning equivalent to a standard Markov decision process under long propagation delays without requiring ranging. Performance evaluations indicate that MobiU-MAC outperforms existing DRL-based MAC protocols for UWANs by leveraging the maximum system delay boundary without ranging overhead, supporting the effectiveness of the proposed theory and algorithm in complex underwater dynamic environments.

</details>

### [42] [Are Flat Minima an Illusion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05209)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"平坦极小值改善泛化"的传统观点，指出平坦性可被重参数化人为操控，并非泛化的真正原因。作者提出"弱度"（weakness）作为新的解释框架，证明了其重参数化不变性，并在实验中证实弱度能有效预测泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明位于损失景观平坦区域的神经网络泛化性能更好，Sharpness-Aware Minimisation等算法基于此假设。然而，保持函数不变的重参数化可以在不改变网络预测的情况下，使Hessian矩阵特征值增大两个数量级。如果权重空间的几何形状可以被人为制造，那么它就不可能是泛化性能的真正原因。这促使作者寻找一个重参数化不变且能真正解释泛化现象的量。

Method: 作者提出了"弱度"(weakness)这一新概念，定义为在学习的具身语言中与学习函数兼容的补全体积。理论方面：(1)证明弱度在重参数化下保持不变，因为它定义在网络"做什么"而非"如何参数化"；(2)证明弱度在可交换需求下具有极小极大最优性；(3)证明PAC-Bayes界有效是因为它与弱度相关。实验方面，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST数据集上对比100个相同架构和训练过程的网络，分析弱度、平坦度和简单性对泛化性能的预测能力，并研究数据量对平坦度预测能力的影响。

Result: 实验结果：(1)MNIST数据集上，弱度能有效预测泛化性能(ρ=+0.374, p=0.00012)，平坦度呈负相关(ρ=-0.226)，而简单性无预测能力(p=0.848)；(2)Fashion-MNIST数据集上，弱度同样有效(ρ=+0.384, p=8.15×10^-5)，简单性有一定预测能力；(3)关键发现：大批次泛化优势随训练数据增加而消失，从n=2000时的+1.6%降至n=60000时的+0.02%。这表明平坦度的预测能力依赖于数据量，属于混杂因素而非因果因素。简单性具有数据集依赖性，而弱度保持不变。

Conclusion: 平坦极小值从来不是泛化问题的答案。弱度作为重参数化不变量，是驱动泛化性能的真正原因，而非混杂因素。平坦度可以通过重参数化人为制造，因此不能作为泛化的因果解释。简单性具有数据集依赖性，不具备普适性。这一发现为理解神经网络泛化机制提供了新视角，对现有基于平坦性的优化算法(如SAM)的理论基础提出了根本性质疑。

Abstract: Neural networks that land in flat regions of the loss landscape tend to generalise better than those in sharp regions. Sharpness-Aware Minimisation exploits this to improve generalisation. But function-preserving reparameterisation can inflate the Hessian of any minimum by two orders of magnitude without changing a single prediction. If the geometry of weight space can be manufactured from nothing, it cannot be the cause of anything. In other words, flat is simple and simplicity depends on encoding. Here I show that the actual driver is weakness, the volume of completions compatible with the learned function in the learner's embodied language. Weakness is reparameterisation-invariant because it is defined over what the network \emph{does}, not how it is parameterised. I prove weakness is minimax-optimal under exchangeable demands, and that PAC-Bayes bounds work because they correlate with it. On MNIST, the large-batch generalisation advantage \emph{vanishes} as training data grows, from $+1.6\%$ at $n = 2{,}000$ to $+0.02\%$ at $n = 60{,}000$. A quantity whose predictive power depends on how much data you have is not a cause but a confounder. I run head-to-heads on 100 networks with identical architecture and training. For MNIST weakness predicts generalisation ($ρ= +0.374$, $p = 0.00012$), sharpness anticorrelates ($ρ= -0.226$) and simplicity predicts nothing ($p = 0.848$). For Fashion-MNIST ($ρ= +0.384$, $p = 8.15 \times 10^{-5}$), though simplicity is at least somewhat predictive there. Simplicity is dataset dependent, whereas weakness is invariant. Flat minima were never the answer.

</details>

*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）是一种常见的异质性炎症性疾病，早期识别困难，因为其症状与过敏性鼻炎等常见疾病重叠，且异质性表型模糊了风险模式。此外，现有预测研究多依赖单机构队列，降低了人群层面的泛化能力。

Method: 利用All of Us研究计划的全美纵向电子健康记录（EHR）数据，使用诊断前两年的病史进行预测。针对EHR数据的极端稀疏性和高维性，实施了混合特征选择流程（结合基于患病率的统计筛选与基于模型的特征重要性排序），将约11万个候选代码压缩为100个可解释特征。为捕捉人口统计学异质性，训练了六个成人性别和生命阶段分组的分层模型，并进行了分组特定的超参数调优。

Result: 该框架总体AUC达到0.8461，相比最佳基线模型，判别能力提高了0.0168。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据可支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，有助于初级医疗保健中的早期分诊和转诊优先级排序。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [44] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出顺序智能体调优（SAT）框架，通过去中心化训练实现多个小模型协作，在数学推理基准上超越大模型并支持即插即用升级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署成本高昂，而使用多个小模型团队协作虽能降低成本，但联合更新多个智能体时会引入复合分布偏移，导致训练协调困难和稳定性问题。现有方法需要中心化控制器，难以扩展。

Method: 提出顺序智能体调优（SAT）范式：1）将团队表示为分解策略，采用块坐标更新实现去中心化训练；2）开发序列感知的在策略优势估计器，条件化于演化的团队策略；3）引入每智能体KL信任区域隔离占用漂移。无需中心控制器即可实现可扩展训练。

Result: 理论上保证单调改进稳定训练，并提供可证明的即插即用不变性——可独立升级任一智能体且性能边界提升。实验中，三个4B智能体团队（共12B）在AIME24/25基准上平均超越Qwen3-32B达3.9%；替换两个8B智能体后综合得分提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT成功解决了多智能体协作训练的稳定性和可扩展性问题，为部署高效的小模型团队提供了新范式，其即插即用特性支持灵活升级，在数学推理任务上证明了小团队协作可以超越单一超大模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [45] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Learnable Loss Balancing and Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05217)
*Reza Pirayeshshirazinezhad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督物理信息神经网络框架，通过自适应平衡物理约束与数据驱动损失，在仅用87个CFD数据点的情况下实现了液态金属微型散热器传热预测误差<8%，解决了数据稀缺条件下的科学机器学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理信息神经网络（PINN）在数据稀缺场景下，通常依赖固定或启发式的物理残差与数据损失的加权方式，这导致训练不稳定、泛化能力差且需要繁琐的人工调参，限制了其在科学计算领域的应用效果。

Method: 提出了一种包含可学习混合神经元的自监督PINN框架，该神经元能根据不确定性动态调整物理损失与数据损失的相对贡献；同时集成了迁移学习策略，通过复用相关领域的表征并适应到新的物理系统，以进一步提升在有限数据下的训练效率。

Result: 在液态金属微型散热器传热预测任务中，仅使用87个CFD数据点进行验证，该自适应PINN模型的预测误差低于8%，性能优于浅层神经网络、核方法以及纯物理基线模型。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种将物理知识自适应嵌入神经网络的通用范式，为流体动力学、材料建模等多个科学领域的数据稀缺问题，提供了一种稳健、可复现且无需人工调参的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a self-supervised physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework that adaptively balances physics-based and data-driven supervision for scientific machine learning under data scarcity. Unlike prior PINNs that rely on fixed or heuristic weighting of physics residuals and data loss, our approach introduces a learnable blending neuron that dynamically adjusts the relative contribution of each term based on their uncertainties. This mechanism enables stable training and improved generalization without manual tuning. To further enhance efficiency, we integrate a transfer learning strategy that reuses representations from related domains and adapts them to new physical systems with limited data. We validate the framework for the prediction of heat transfer in liquid-metal miniature heat sinks using only 87 CFD datapoints, where the adaptive PINN achieves an error <8%, outperforming shallow neural networks, kernel methods, and physics-only baselines. Our framework provides a general recipe for embedding physics adaptively into neural networks, offering a robust and reproducible approach for data-scarce problems across various scientific domains, including fluid dynamics and material modeling.

</details>


### [46] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的严格联系，提出了视界约束Rashomon集框架，证明有效Rashomon集以最大Lyapunov指数决定的速率指数级收缩，并开发了决策对齐的选择算法，在混沌系统预测任务中将决策质量提升了18-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性和混沌动力学是机器学习中两个独立发展但概念上存在联系的基础性挑战。现有研究将两者分离看待，而在混沌系统中，模型多重性会随预测时间视界演化，传统静态预测任务中固定不变的Rashomon集假设不再适用，需要建立统一的理论框架来理解混沌如何改变预测等价性的本质。

Method: 引入视界约束Rashomon集理论框架刻画混沌系统中模型多重性随预测视界的演化；证明有效Rashomon集以最大Lyapunov指数确定的速率指数级收缩；引入Lyapunov加权度量提供更紧的预测分歧边界；基于此开发决策对齐的选择算法，根据下游效用而非仅凭预测准确率在近优模型中进行选择。

Result: 在合成混沌系统（Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky）和真实应用（风电、交通、天气）上的大量实验表明，该框架在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，将决策质量提升了18-34%。

Conclusion: 本研究首次建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的严格联系，为在安全关键的混沌领域部署机器学习提供了原则性指导。框架揭示了混沌系统中预测等价性的根本性转变，证明了Rashomon集的指数收缩特性，并通过决策对齐的模型选择策略实现了决策质量的显著提升。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>

*Mikhail Shirokikh,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对状态空间模型（SSM）提出了一种稀疏前缀缓存策略。该方法通过在稀疏位置存储检查点并在缓存命中时从最近的检查点恢复计算，在低检查点预算下显著优于现有启发式方法，且不改变模型输出的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM前缀缓存系统主要针对Transformer架构的密集键值（KV）复用设计。然而，状态空间模型（SSM）具有不同的计算结构：其循环层可以从单个存储状态恢复，而无需整个token历史。这种不对称性揭示了在“不复用”和“密集缓存”之间存在一个新的设计空间，即稀疏检查点缓存，以优化延迟和存储效率。

Result: 在QuALITY和System Prompts数据集上的实验表明，该方法在层-组帕累托前沿上优于所有固定预算基线。与最强启发式方法（块缓存 block caching）相比，该方法通常使用更少的检查点即可达到匹配或更优的性能，特别是在检查点预算较低且重叠分布非均匀的情况下，优势最为显著。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布感知检查点放置方法成功利用了SSM的循环特性，在请求共享大量但不完全相同前缀的场景下显著提升了服务效率。该方法保持精确输出，无需修改循环计算或引入新的内核，具有实用性和良好的兼容性。

Abstract: Prefix caching is a key latency optimization for autoregressive LLM serving, yet existing systems assume dense per-token key/value reuse. State-space models change the structure of the problem: a recurrent layer can resume from a single stored state rather than requiring the entire token history. This asymmetry opens a new design point between no reuse and dense caching: store exact recurrent states at a sparse set of checkpoint positions and, on a cache hit, resume from the deepest stored checkpoint and recompute the remaining suffix exactly.
  We formalize sparse prefix caching as checkpoint placement under a distribution over overlap depths, yielding an exact O(NM) dynamic program. For use cases where requests share a non-trivial prefix (e.g. asking different questions about a single long document), we show that our method consistently improves the Pareto frontier traced by standard heuristics on real-world data. Across QuALITY and System Prompts, distribution-aware placement dominates every fixed-budget baseline on the measured layer-group Pareto frontier and matches or outperforms the strongest heuristic (block caching) while typically using substantially fewer checkpoints, with the largest gains at low checkpoint budgets where the overlap distribution is most non-uniform. The method is most relevant when many requests share a substantial but not identical prefix within a retained cache entry. It preserves exact outputs, does not change the recurrent computation itself or require new recurrent update kernels, applies to recurrent/SSM layers whose hidden state can be extracted and restored exactly, and for hybrid models can be combined with existing KV-cache compression techniques.

</details>


### [48] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了概念引导的理论框架，证明了标准引导方法是LEACE仿射擦除方法的特例，并提出了MidSteer方法，一种通用的仿射概念操纵框架，在多种任务和架构中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管引导中间表示在控制生成模型方面取得了实证成功，特别是在部署后的对齐和安全设置中，但目前缺乏一个全面的理论框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，形式化概念引导的理论。

Method: 首先建立了引导与仿射概念擦除之间的联系，证明去除不期望行为的标准方法是LEACE（一种闭式仿射擦除方法）的特例。然后提出了概念切换的原则性理论框架LEACE-Switch，并刻画了其提供最优仿射解的假设条件。在此基础上，引入了MidSteer（最小扰动概念引导），这是一种更通用的仿射概念操纵框架，放宽了这些假设，并实现了有向的、最小扰动的变换。

Result: MidSteer在包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型在内的一系列任务、模态和架构中表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将概念引导理论化，建立了其与仿射概念擦除的联系，并提出了一种新的、更通用的框架MidSteer，为概念引导提供了坚实的理论基础并展示了其有效性。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [49] [Data-Driven Variational Basis Learning Beyond Neural Networks: A Non-Neural Framework for Adaptive Basis Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05221)
*Andrew Kiruluta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了数据驱动变分基学习（DVBL）框架，作为一种非神经网络的替代方案，通过变分优化直接从数据中学习基函数，在保持显式性、可解释性和数学透明度的同时实现数据自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典表示系统（如傅里叶级数、小波）缺乏对现代高维数据经验结构的内在适应性，而神经网络虽能从数据中学习特征，但通过分层非线性参数化牺牲了可解释性、对基结构的显式控制以及数学透明度。研究旨在开发一种既具备数据自适应性，又保持数学严谨性和可解释性的表示学习方法。

Method: 将基原子作为主要优化变量，通过变分优化直接从数据中学习基函数，并与样本特定系数及潜在线性演化算子（适用时）联合优化。采用交替最小化算法进行求解，并集成了流形和动力学正则化方法，但不依赖神经网络架构。

Result: 建立了极小化子的存在性，证明了交替最小化算法的块下降性质，给出了系数恢复和基可识别性的条件，展示了如何在避免神经网络架构的情况下集成流形和动力学正则化。

Conclusion: DVBL框架成功实现了数据自适应的基展开，保持了显式性、可解释性和可严格分析性，为经典字典学习、谱方法、Koopman算子方法和深度表示学习提供了一个概念上新颖的非神经网络替代方案。

Abstract: Classical representation systems such as Fourier series, wavelets, and fixed dictionaries provide analytically tractable basis expansions, but they are not intrinsically adapted to the empirical structure of modern high-dimensional data. Neural networks overcome this limitation by learning features from data, yet they do so through layered nonlinear parameterizations that often sacrifice interpretability, explicit control over basis structure, and mathematical transparency. In this manuscript we develop a non-neural alternative that learns basis functions directly from data through variational optimization. The proposed framework, termed Data Driven Variational Basis Learning (DVBL), treats basis atoms as primary optimization variables and learns them jointly with sample-specific coefficients and, when appropriate, a latent linear evolution operator. This yields a data-adaptive basis expansion that remains explicit, interpretable, and amenable to rigorous analysis. We formulate the model, establish existence of minimizers, prove blockwise descent properties for an alternating minimization algorithm, give conditions for coefficient recovery and basis identifiability, and show how manifold and dynamical regularization can be integrated without invoking neural architectures. We also discuss the conceptual novelty of the framework relative to classical dictionary learning, spectral methods, Koopman operator methods, and deep representation learning.

</details>


### [50] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，通过轻量级MLP为每个token学习一个连续的门控概率，动态决定是否跳过transformer层，在几乎不增加参数和损失性能的情况下节省14-23%的计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer架构对所有token应用相同数量的层，忽略了不同token的处理难度差异。这种"一刀切"的方式导致计算资源浪费，因为简单token可能不需要经过所有层就能得到充分处理。

Method: TSA在每个transformer块的残差连接处引入一个学习的per-token门控机制。该门控由一个轻量级两层MLP实现，输出连续的停止概率，使机制端到端可微分。该方法仅增加1.7%的参数开销，无需修改基础架构。值得注意的是，即使没有显式的深度正则化(λ=0)，仅任务损失梯度就能驱动路由器跳过20%的token-layer操作。

Result: 在字符级语言建模任务上，TSA在Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8数据集上节省了14-23%的token-layer操作(TLOps)，质量损失小于0.5%。在匹配效率的条件下，TSA的验证损失比早退(early exit)方法低0.7%。学习到的路由策略可直接应用于推理时的稀疏执行，实现实际的wall-clock加速。

Conclusion: TSA成功实现了token级别的动态深度路由，能够根据任务难度自适应分配计算资源。该方法证明了无需显式深度正则化也能学习到难度比例路由，且学到的路由策略具有良好的迁移性，可直接用于推理加速，为构建自适应计算的Transformer提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [51] [A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18555)
*Ran Ben-Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Gal Mendelson,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文澄清了TurboQuant与早期EDEN算法的关系，证明TurboQuant是EDEN的特例或次优变体，实验表明EDEN在所有设置下均优于TurboQuant。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清近期TurboQuant工作与更早的DRIVE（NeurIPS 2021）和EDEN（ICML 2022）方案之间的关系，指出TurboQuant并非创新方法而是EDEN的特殊或次优情况。

Method: 通过理论分析对比两种算法：(1) 证明TurboQuant_mse是EDEN在标量参数S=1时的特例；(2) 分析TurboQuant_prod的三个次优之处；(3) 指出两者在数学分析上的相似性（随机旋转与Beta分布的联系、Lloyd-Max算法、随机Hadamard变换）。通过重复TurboQuant论文中的所有精度实验进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明：使用优化S参数的有偏EDEN比TurboQuant_mse更精确；无偏EDEN明显优于TurboQuant_prod，优势常超过1比特（例如2比特EDEN优于3比特TurboQuant_prod）；EDEN在所有测试设置下均优于TurboQuant。

Conclusion: TurboQuant并非独立创新，而是EDEN算法的次优实现；EDEN在理论完备性和实验性能上均占优势，研究者应直接采用EDEN而非TurboQuant。

Abstract: This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN.
  First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$.
  Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN.
  Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations.
  Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.

</details>


### [52] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架来分析稀疏自编码器中特征联合（feature unions）的不稳定性，推导出了组合塌陷阈值，并揭示了ReLU整流在组合过程中会产生类似棘轮效应的干扰累积，从而指出了基于联合的引导策略在可扩展性方面存在的几何约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管稀疏自编码器（SAEs）在解耦Transformer架构中的特征叠加方面表现出色，但关于组合引导（即同时激活不同语义潜在特征）的理论基础仍探索不足。现有的线性表示假设往往忽略了在过完备字典中出现的非线性干扰效应，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出了一个几何分析框架：将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，在球形字典模型下推导渐近组合塌陷阈值（由信号锥的高斯平均宽度表征）；分析了高偏置状态下ReLU整流如何将微观相关诱导的方差波动转化为系统性漂移。在CLEVR数据集上提取结构化语义特征进行验证。

Result: 推导出了组合塌缩阈值的解析表达式；发现ReLU整流会产生类似棘轮效应的干扰累积，导致干扰在组合过程中持续增长；在CLEVR实验中验证了预测的缩放趋势，显示层次化相关性相比随机基线会加速相变过程。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了基于联合的引导策略在可扩展性方面存在的几何约束，强调了超越朴素线性叠加、显式管理干扰的组合机制的必要性，为设计更稳健的特征组合方法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [53] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习样本（UEs）在预训练-微调范式下的表现，发现预训练权重会削弱现有UEs的有效性，并提出了一种新的层级欺骗策略SSC来保持数据的不可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不可学习样本研究主要评估从头训练的设置，而其在广泛采用的预训练-微调（PF）范式下的行为尚未被探索。由于未经授权使用个人数据进行模型训练已成为日益严重的隐私威胁，研究UEs在不同训练范式下的有效性至关重要。

Method: 提出了一种层级欺骗策略——浅层语义伪装（SSC），该方法将生成过程限制在语义有效的子空间内，旨在绕过预训练权重引入的语义抑制。通过语义过滤的视角解释发现：UEs倾向于诱导模型过拟合非语义噪声，而在PF范式下，冻结的浅层保留数据语义，有效过滤掉干扰信息。

Result: 实验表明，SSC方法在具有挑战性的训练范式下（如浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练SF-Pretrain）能够一致地保持数据的不可学习性，弥合了基于预训练的不可学习学习中的关键差距。分析揭示了加载和冻结预训练权重会显著削弱现有UEs方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了不可学习样本在预训练-微调范式下研究的空白，提出的SSC方法成功解决了预训练权重对UEs有效性的削弱问题，为保护个人数据隐私提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [54] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对随机化Hadamard变换（RHT）替代均匀随机旋转（URR）在量化等场景中的最坏情况性能问题，证明了两次RHT组合可使边缘分布高斯逼近，三次RHT可保证向量量化的性能，并提出一种线性时间的自适应方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机旋转（URR）广泛用于梯度压缩、推理加速等现代量化方法的预处理，实际中常用随机化Hadamard变换（RHT）替代URR以保持正交性并支持快速实现。然而，单次RHT在最坏情况输入下性能不佳，因为单个坐标的分布可能远偏离理想分布。现有工作缺乏对RHT在最坏情况下与URR性能匹配的理论保证。

Method: 1. 证明两次RHT组合作用于任意d维输入向量后，归一化旋转向量中每个固定坐标的边缘分布在Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离下均与标准高斯分布相差$O(d^{-1/2})$。2. 将该界限应用于现代压缩方案DRIVE和QUIC-FL的分析。3. 针对向量量化（VQ）需要坐标块间弱相关性的特点，证明三次RHT组合可实现衰减的坐标协方差。4. 提出基于输入矩的线性时间$O(d)$检查方法，动态调整运行时使用的RHT次数。

Result: 1. 两次RHT组合在边缘分布收敛性上渐近匹配URR的性能，适用于DRIVE和QUIC-FL等压缩方案。2. 三次RHT组合能确保任意为URR优化的有界多维VQ码本在使用三次RHT时具有相同的期望误差（随维度增加误差项趋于零）。3. 提出的自适应方法可在实际非对抗性输入场景下提升性能。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上严格证明了有限次RHT组合可实现与URR渐近匹配的性能：两次RHT足以满足边缘分布要求的压缩方案，三次RHT满足向量量化的相关性要求。这为RHT在实际量化系统中的可靠应用提供了理论基础，所提出的自适应策略进一步提升了实用性能。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


### [55] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了递归智能体优化（RAO），一种训练递归智能体的强化学习方法，使智能体能够通过自我递归调用和任务委托实现更好的训练效率、上下文扩展和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单智能体系统在处理长上下文和复杂任务时存在局限性。研究人员希望开发一种能够通过分治策略自然扩展到更长上下文、并泛化到更困难问题的智能体架构。

Method: 提出递归智能体优化（RAO）方法，这是一种强化学习训练框架。该方法训练递归智能体，使其能够递归地生成新的自我实例并将子任务委托给这些实例。RAO教导智能体何时以及如何进行委托和通信，从而优化递归推理过程。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过RAO训练的递归智能体具有以下优势：（1）更好的训练效率；（2）能够处理超出模型上下文窗口的任务；（3）能够泛化到比训练任务更难的场景；（4）相比单智能体系统减少了实际运行时间。

Conclusion: RAO成功实现了递归智能体的有效训练，证明了递归架构在扩展推理能力方面的潜力，为构建可扩展、高效且具有强泛化能力的智能体系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [56] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MACS框架，一种免训练的推理方法，通过感知模态的容量缩放解决MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中因拖尾效应导致的效率瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理过程中存在严重的效率瓶颈，主要源于拖尾效应。现有基于token数量的负载均衡方法无法应对多模态场景下的两大挑战：(1) 信息异质性，即大量冗余视觉token与语义关键token被同等对待；(2) 模态动态性，即不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源分配不当。

Method: 提出MACS（Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling）免训练推理框架。包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵加权负载机制，通过量化视觉token的语义价值来解决信息异质性问题；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制，根据输入的实时模态组成分配专家资源，应对模态动态性挑战。

Result: 在多个多模态基准测试上的大量实验表明，MACS显著优于现有方法，证明了其在解决MoE MLLMs专家并行推理效率问题上的有效性。

Conclusion: MACS为MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中的高效部署提供了一个新颖且鲁棒的解决方案，成功解决了多模态场景下特有的负载均衡挑战。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [57] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“监督内化”方法，通过识别、纠正和重用失败的推理轨迹，自动将稀疏的结果级监督转化为细粒度的过程级信号，从而在仅有结果监督的情况下实现更精细的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习推理的核心挑战在于如何将序列末端提供的稀疏结果反馈转化为能够指导中间推理步骤的细粒度学习信号。现有方法要么依赖序列级优化的结果奖励导致信用分配困难，要么依赖昂贵且难以扩展的外部过程监督。

Method: 提出“监督内化”视角，将推理强化学习理解为将结果监督内化为过程监督的过程。通过识别、纠正和重用失败的推理轨迹，自动提取过程级学习信号，实现更精细的策略优化。

Result: 该方法建立了一种新的训练范式，使模型能够在强化学习过程中持续生成和完善自身的过程监督，为推理任务的细粒度信用分配开辟了新路径。

Conclusion: 提出的监督内化方法成功解决了结果监督稀疏性与过程监督需求之间的矛盾，为仅依赖结果监督的强化学习推理提供了一种可扩展、自监督的细粒度学习信号生成方案。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [58] [CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06571)
*Iason Ofeidis,Nikos Papadis,Randeep Bhatia,Leandros Tassiulas,TV Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CLAD框架，通过结合聚类联邦学习与双模式微架构，同时解决IoT安全中的设备异构性和标签稀缺问题，在80%客户端无标签场景下检测性能相对提升30%，通信成本减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT/IIoT的快速扩张带来了大规模、异构的攻击面，传统网络安全机制难以应对。虽然联邦学习为集中式入侵检测系统提供了隐私保护的替代方案，但现有方法难以在多样化设备行为上泛化，且无法充分利用边缘环境中大量存在的无标签数据。

Method: 提出CLAD框架，整合聚类联邦学习（CFL）与新型双模式微架构（DM²A）。DM²A包含共享编码器和两个分支，实现联合无监督异常检测与监督攻击分类，从而利用有标签和无标签客户端的数据。聚类组件动态将具有相似流量模式的设备分组，防止全局模型发散。

Result: 在80%客户端无标签的场景下，检测性能相对基线提升30%，通信成本仅为基线的一半。

Conclusion: CLAD框架通过有机结合聚类联邦学习与双模式架构，有效解决了IoT环境下的设备异构性和标签稀缺两大核心瓶颈，实现了对有标签和无标签数据的充分利用，显著优于现有最先进方法。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.

</details>


### [59] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT框架，将数据治理重新定义为在线重加权问题，通过动态调整样本重要性来替代离线静态预处理，在指令微调和大规预训练中均取得优于现有离线/在线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据治理方法（如数据选择和混合）采用离线范式，与训练过程解耦，导致工程开销大且鲁棒性差；离线方法通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模，损害数据多样性并影响泛化能力。

Method: 提出ADAPT（自适应数据重加权）在线框架，在训练过程中通过基于相似性的质量信号引导，为每个样本动态分配自适应学习率进行损失加权，保持训练样本数量不变，实现隐式课程学习。

Result: 在指令微调和大规预训练实验中，ADAPT持续优于离线选择/混合方法及先前的在线方法，在同等FLOPs下实现了更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将数据治理视为在线重加权问题优于传统离线方法，ADAPT通过动态样本权重调整实现隐式课程学习，能够随模型演化从粗粒度模式逐步过渡到细粒度语义区分，有效提升模型性能。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [60] [OpenG2G: A Simulation Platform for AI Datacenter-Grid Runtime Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05519)
*Jae-Won Chung,Zhirui Liang,Yanyong Mao,Jiasi Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Vladimir Dvorkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenG2G，一个用于AI数据中心与电网实时协调的仿真平台，旨在解决AI计算需求增长带来的电网容量和可靠性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI日益增长的计算需求和数据中心建设给电网带来了巨大的容量和可靠性挑战，导致新数据中心面临数年的并网延迟，成为AI增长的瓶颈。为缓解这一压力，数据中心开始提供快速功率灵活性响应电网信号，因此需要理解大型数据中心对电网的影响并设计有效的协调策略。

Method: 构建了OpenG2G仿真平台，采用模块化和可扩展架构：包括由生产级AI服务实际测量驱动的数据中心后端、基于高保真电网模拟器的电网后端，以及连接两者的通用控制器接口。平台允许用户实现和比较多种控制范式（包括经典控制、优化控制和基于学习的控制器），并量化AI模型和部署选择对数据中心灵活性和协调结果的影响。

Result: OpenG2G能够回答广泛的协调问题，通过真实的电网场景和AI工作负载验证了其实用性。平台成功实现了对不同控制范式的比较分析，并能够量化AI模型和部署决策对数据中心灵活性的具体影响。

Conclusion: OpenG2G为研究AI数据中心与电网的协调提供了一个强大而灵活的仿真工具，有助于理解大型数据中心对电网的影响，促进有效协调策略的设计，从而缓解AI增长对电网的压力并减少并网延迟。

Abstract: AI's growing compute demand and new datacenter buildouts present major capacity and reliability challenges for the electricity grid, leading to multi-year interconnection delays for new datacenters and bottlenecking AI growth. To ease this strain, datacenters increasingly offer rapid power flexibility in response to grid signals, where the datacenter can increase or decrease its power consumption by adapting its workload in real time.
  In order to understand the impact of large datacenters on the grid and to facilitate the design of effective coordination strategies, we build OpenG2G, a simulation platform for AI datacenter-grid runtime coordination. We show that OpenG2G is capable of answering a wide range of coordination questions by allowing users to implement and compare various control paradigms (including classic, optimization, and learning-based controllers), and quantify how AI model and deployment choices affect datacenter flexibility and coordination outcomes. This versatility is enabled by OpenG2G's modular and extensible architecture: a datacenter backend driven by real measurements of production-grade AI services, a grid backend built on high-fidelity grid simulators, and a generic controller interface that closes the loop between them. We describe the design of OpenG2G and demonstrate its usefulness through realistic grid scenarios and AI workloads.

</details>


### [61] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于进化策略的深度学习模型量化优化方法，通过迭代调整部分权重的量化状态来提升量化后模型的精度，在VGG、ResNet及自编码器等架构上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在IoT、移动设备及实时系统等场景中受限于计算复杂度和内存占用，量化是主流压缩技术，但传统基于最近邻舍入的量化方法无法保证最优精度，因此需要更优的量化状态优化方法。

Method: 提出使用进化策略作为优化方法，在每次迭代中对小比例的权重进行微调，将其值转移到不同的量化状态，通过适当的算子和参数组合快速提升量化模型的精度。

Result: 在VGG和ResNet等流行架构上进行了图像分类和目标检测实验，同时测试了自编码器架构。实验表明该方法能够快速提升量化模型的准确率。

Conclusion: 基于进化策略的量化状态优化方法能够有效提升深度学习模型在量化后的性能表现，为模型压缩和边缘部署提供了新的优化思路。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [62] [Expert Routing for Communication-Efficient MoE via Finite Expert Banks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05278)
*Mohammad Reza Deylam Salehi,Ali Khalesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将稀疏MoE中的门控机制视为随机信道，通过互信息量化路由信息，提出了实用的信息量估计方法，发现经验互信息与泛化差距单调相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE架构中的门控既是学习组件又是路由接口，控制计算、通信和精度。现有研究缺乏对MoE门控信息论特性的深入理解，特别是如何量化路由信息及其与泛化性能的关系。

Method: 将门控视为随机信道，使用I(X;T)量化路由信息；构建有限库MNIST实验，使用预训练CNN专家和离散数据依赖选择规则；提出基于经验后验的离散熵估计器计算I(S;W)；引入I(X;T)的经验估计器和Blahut-Arimoto过程来追踪精度-速率曲线。

Result: 随数据依赖参数α变化，经验互信息估计$\hat{I}(S;W)$与泛化差距呈单调跟踪关系；Xu-Raginsky界表现出预期的宽松性；提出的方法为资源感知MoE推理系统分析提供了实用工具。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为分析资源感知MoE推理系统提供了实用工具，I(X;T)和D(R_g)可作为高效专家路由的设计代理指标，为MoE系统的信息论分析与设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Resource-efficient machine learning increasingly uses sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures, where the gate acts as both a learning component and a routing interface controlling computation, communication, and accuracy. Motivated by finite-rate interpretations of MoE gating, we treat the gate as a stochastic channel and use $I(X;T)$ to quantify the routing information available to the selected expert. To make the associated information quantities tractable beyond synthetic examples, we develop a finite-bank MNIST construction using pretrained CNN experts and a discrete, data-dependent selection rule. Since the selected model belongs to a finite candidate set, the algorithmic mutual information $I(S;W)$ admits a closed-form discrete-entropy estimator from the empirical posterior $q(W|S)$. Sweeping a data-dependence parameter $α$, we observe that $\widehat I(S;W)$ monotonically tracks the generalization gap, while the Xu-Raginsky bound exhibits the expected looseness. We also compare with a uniform union-bound baseline and introduce an empirical estimator of $I(X;T)$ together with a Blahut-Arimoto procedure for tracing an accuracy-rate curve over the expert bank. The proposed framework provides a practical tool for analyzing resource-aware MoE inference systems and for interpreting $I(X;T)$ and $D(R_g)$ as design proxies for efficient expert routing.

</details>


### [63] [Forecasting Green Skill Demand in the Automotive Industry: Evidence from Online Job Postings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05280)
*Sabur Butt,Joshua N. Arrazola E.,Hector G. Ceballos,Patricia Caratozzolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个计算框架，利用墨西哥汽车行业的在线招聘数据识别绿色技能并预测其需求。通过两阶段流程识别出274种绿色技能，发现基于Transformer的时间序列预测模型表现最佳，且当前绿色技能需求集中在运营可持续性实践，而增长最快的技能与可再生能源、回收和氢能技术相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球向可持续经济转型正在重塑劳动力市场，但目前系统化识别和预测绿色技能的方法仍然有限，缺乏有效的数据驱动工具来支持绿色转型中的劳动力规划。

Method: 收集Indeed Mexico、OCC Mundial和LinkedIn三个平台（2024年7月至2025年7月）的招聘广告数据，构建包含204,373条技能记录的数据集。采用两阶段流程：结合多语言嵌入和ESCO验证来识别绿色技能。使用滚动原点评估方法对15种时间序列预测模型进行基准测试。提出基于绝对增长和相对增长的技能分类框架。

Result: 识别出274种独特的绿色技能，共出现8,576次（占所有技能的4.22%）。基于Transformer的模型（特别是FEDformer、Reformer和Informer）表现最佳，MAE约为2.5e-5，相对RMSE低于15%。当前需求集中在运营可持续性实践，而增长最快的技能与可再生能源、回收和氢能技术相关。

Conclusion: 该计算管道能够有效支持绿色转型中的数据驱动型劳动力规划，为识别和预测绿色技能需求提供了系统化的方法论工具，有助于政策制定者和企业更好地应对可持续经济转型带来的人力资源挑战。

Abstract: The global transition toward sustainable economies is reshaping labor markets, yet systematic methods for identifying and forecasting green skills remain limited. This study presents a computational framework to measure and predict green skill demand using online job postings from Mexico's automotive industry, which contributes about 4% of national GDP. We compile a dataset of job advertisements from Indeed Mexico, OCC Mundial, and LinkedIn (July 2024 to July 2025), yielding 204,373 skill records. A two-stage pipeline combining multilingual embeddings and ESCO validation identifies 274 unique green skills across 8,576 occurrences (4.22% of all skills). We benchmark 15 time series forecasting models using a rolling origin evaluation. Transformer-based models, especially FEDformer, Reformer, and Informer, achieve the best performance, with MAE around 2.5e-5 and relative RMSE below 15. We further propose a framework to classify skills by absolute and relative growth, identifying stable, emerging, and high-impact competencies. Results show current demand is concentrated in operational sustainability practices, while the fastest-growing skills relate to renewable energy, recycling, and hydrogen technologies. This pipeline supports data-driven workforce planning in the green transition.

</details>


### [64] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种归因引导的持续微调框架，通过评估参数重要性来调节梯度更新，从而有效缓解大语言模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在持续学习中存在灾难性遗忘问题，现有方法（如数据回放、参数冻结、正则化）缺乏对模型内部知识分布的语义感知，无法有效区分应当保留或更新的参数。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架，估算每个Transformer层中特定任务的逐元素参数重要性分数，并利用这些分数调节梯度。对旧任务重要的参数更新幅度较小，而相关性较低的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准测试中，该方法持续优于基线方法，在保持新任务竞争力的同时，实现了对旧任务更好的保留性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入语义感知的参数重要性评估机制，该框架能够有效缓解灾难性遗忘，为LLM的持续学习提供了一种更精细的参数更新策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [65] [Graph Normalization: Fast Binarizing Dynamics for Differentiable MWIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05330)
*Laurent Guigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了图归一化（GN），一种用于求解最大权独立集（MWIS）问题的可微动态系统，证明了其收敛性并建立了与演化博弈论的联系，在大规模实例上能快速获得高质量解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大权独立集（MWIS）是一个NP难问题，涵盖了最优分配、调度、集合包装和马尔可夫随机场MAP推断等众多组合优化挑战。现有的方法（如置信传播）难以保证收敛到最优解，且缺乏可微性，限制了其在深度学习等端到端架构中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种原理性的图上动态系统——图归一化（GN）。它通过精确的Majorization-Minimization步骤实现快速拟牛顿下降，系统性地改进MWIS松弛目标。理论上证明了GN与非线性演化博弈的复制动力学等价，其中顶点竞争独立集的归属，并遵循费希尔自然选择基本定理。该方法可视为Sinkhorn算法的变体，能自然收敛到硬分配并推广至任意约束图。

Result: 理论证明GN总是收敛到最大独立集的二值指示器；建立了MIS与倾斜单纯形上二次形式局部极小值之间的双射关系（加权Motzkin-Straus定理）。实验上，GN作为最先进的Bregman-Sinkhorn松弛MWIS求解器的二值化引擎，在多达100万条边的真实世界基准测试中，仅需数秒（CPU）即可获得与已知最优结果相差在1%以内的解。

Conclusion: GN框架为需要在约束下做出可微“硬”决策的深度学习架构（如结构化稀疏注意力、动态网络剪枝、专家混合模型）开辟了新途径，并使计算机视觉、计算生物学和资源分配等领域的端到端约束优化学习成为可能。

Abstract: We introduce Graph Normalization (GN), a principled dynamical system on graphs that serves as a differentiable approximation engine for the NP-hard Maximum Weight Independent Set (MWIS) problem. MWIS encompasses many combinatorial challenges, including optimal assignment, scheduling, set packing, and MAP inference in discrete Markov Random Fields. Unlike Belief Propagation, we prove GN always converges to a binary indicator of a Maximum Independent Set. GN realizes a fast quasi-Newton descent through an exact Majorization-Minimization step, systematically improving the MWIS relaxed primal objective. We establish an equivalence between GN and the Replicator Dynamics of a nonlinear evolutionary game, where vertices compete for inclusion in an independent set. While a non-potential game, the GN game follows Fisher's Fundamental Theorem of Natural Selection, where the average fitness equals the MWIS primal objective and strictly increases. This connection leads to a weighted extension of the Motzkin-Straus theorem, showing MISes are in bijection with the local minima of a quadratic form over a tilted simplex. For the Assignment Problem, GN acts as a variant of the Sinkhorn algorithm that naturally converges to a hard assignment while generalizing to arbitrary constraint graphs. We demonstrate GN's performance as a fast binarization engine for the state-of-the-art Bregman-Sinkhorn relaxed MWIS solver. On real-world benchmarks with up to 1M edges, GN identifies solutions within 1% of the best known results in seconds on a CPU. GN opens new avenues for deep learning architectures requiring differentiable, "hard" decisions under constraints, with applications in structured sparse attention, dynamic network pruning, and Mixture-of-Experts. Beyond core AI, the GN framework enables end-to-end learning of constrained optimization in computer vision, computational biology, and resource allocation.

</details>


### [66] [Feature Starvation as Geometric Instability in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05341)
*Faris Chaudhry,Keisuke Yano,Anthea Monod*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应弹性网络稀疏自编码器（AEN-SAEs），通过引入$\ell_2$结构项和自适应$\ell_1$重加权，从根本上解决了标准SAEs的特征缺失和收缩偏差问题，无需启发式方法即可在LLMs上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的$\ell_1$正则化稀疏自编码器存在特征缺失（神经元死亡）和收缩偏差问题，通常需要计算昂贵的启发式重采样和非可微硬掩码方法来缓解。作者认为特征缺失不仅仅是数据多样性不足的经验产物，而是过度完备字典的一种基本优化几何病理：$\ell_1$诱导的稀疏编码映射不稳定，且与浅层摊销编码器根本性错位。

Method: 提出自适应弹性网络稀疏自编码器（AEN-SAEs），这是一种基于经典稀疏回归的完全可微架构。该方法结合了强制强凸性和Lipschitz稳定性的$\ell_2$结构项，以及消除收缩偏差和抑制虚假特征的自适应$\ell_1$重加权，从而联合控制诱导多面体几何的曲率和相互作用结构。

Result: 在理论上证明了AEN-SAEs产生Lipschitz连续的稀疏编码映射，并在温和假设下恢复全局特征支持。在实验上，通过合成设置和LLMs（Pythia 70M、Llama 3.1 8B）验证，AEN-SAEs在没有辅助启发式方法的情况下缓解了特征缺失，同时保持了有竞争力的重构能力。

Conclusion: AEN-SAEs成功解决了标准SAEs的结构不稳定性问题，提供了一种理论基础扎实、完全可微的解决方案，能够在保持重构性能的同时有效避免特征缺失，为LLMs的可解释性研究提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are used to disentangle the dense, polysemantic internal representations of large language models (LLMs) into interpretable, monosemantic concepts. However, standard $\ell_1$-regularized SAEs suffer from feature starvation (dead neurons) and shrinkage bias, often requiring computationally expensive heuristic resampling and nondifferentiable hard-masking methods to bypass these challenges. We argue that feature starvation is not merely an empirical artifact of poor data diversity, but a fundamental optimization-geometric pathology of overcomplete dictionaries: the $\ell_1$-induced sparse coding map is unstable and fundamentally misaligned with shallow, amortized encoders. To address this structural instability, we introduce adaptive elastic net SAEs (AEN-SAEs), a fully differentiable architecture grounded in classical sparse regression. AEN-SAEs combine an $\ell_2$ structural term that enforces strong convexity and Lipschitz stability with adaptive $\ell_1$ reweighting that eliminates shrinkage bias and suppresses spurious features, thereby jointly controlling the curvature and interaction structure of the induced polyhedral geometry. Theoretically, we show that AEN-SAEs yield a Lipschitz-continuous sparse coding map and recover the global feature support under mild assumptions. Empirically, across synthetic settings and LLMs (Pythia 70M, Llama 3.1 8B), AEN-SAEs mitigate feature starvation without auxiliary heuristics while maintaining competitive reconstruction abilities.

</details>


### [67] [A Multi-Head Attention Approach for SLA Compliance Monitoring in Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05354)
*Omanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将数据中心托管合同中的SLA规则编码为结构化JSON对象的框架，通过训练多头Transformer模型提前30分钟预测违约，并为财务、运维和合规角色提供定制化的预测视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的反应式监控只能在违约发生后才检测到问题，限制了补救机会，无法有效避免因功率、温度和湿度阈值违规而产生的经济处罚。

Method: 将SLA规则编码为结构化JSON对象以自动生成训练数据；训练针对特定客户的多头Transformer模型，每个注意力头专门学习一条SLA规则，捕捉违规前30分钟的时间依赖性；推理结果转换为三种特定角色的视图（财务风险视图、运维干预视图、合规审计视图）。

Result: 该框架通过将模型架构与合同义务直接对齐，成功实现了对SLA违约的提前预测，能够暴露信用负债风险、提供风险评分与干预建议，并生成带有不可变遥测签名的合规审计数据。

Conclusion: 该框架使运营商能够预见SLA违约、优先采取纠正措施并最小化经济处罚，实现了从被动监控到主动预防的转变。

Abstract: Service level agreements (SLAs) in data center colocation contracts define precise thresholds for power, temperature, and humidity, with tiered violation penalties expressed as credits against monthly recurring charges. Traditional reactive monitoring detects breaches only after they occur, limiting remediation opportunities. We present a framework that encodes SLA rules as structured JSON objects to generate training data without manual annotation. We train a per-customer multi-head transformer model in which each attention head specializes in one SLA rule, learning temporal dependencies that precede violations by 30 minutes. Post-training, the inference service emits structured prediction events transformed into three role-specific views: finance schemas exposing credit liability, operations schemas surfacing risk scores and recommended interventions, and compliance schemas bundling predictions with immutable telemetry signatures for audit. By aligning model architecture directly with contractual obligations, this framework enables operators to anticipate SLA breaches, prioritize corrective actions, and minimize financial penalties.

</details>


### [68] [Balancing Stability and Plasticity in Sequentially Trained Early-Exiting Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05358)
*Alaa Zniber,Ouassim Karrakchou,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对早退神经网络在顺序训练过程中存在的灾难性遗忘问题，提出了两种基于持续学习思想的方法来保护已有出口的知识，从而提升了顺序训练的准确性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在早退神经网络中，顺序增加新的出口分类器会干扰已训练好的早期出口，导致其性能下降（即灾难性遗忘）。本文旨在解决这一稳定性与可塑性的权衡问题，在允许新出口专门化的同时保留旧有出口的知识。

Method: 提出了两种方法：(1) 基于弹性权重巩固（EWC），约束对已有出口重要的参数，防止其被大幅修改；(2) 基于无遗忘学习（LwF），在训练新出口时通过知识蒸馏保留早期出口的输出分布。

Result: 在标准基准测试上的实验表明，所提方法在低计算预算下实现了显著的速度提升，相比现有的顺序训练方法，各个早期出口均获得了更高的分类准确率。

Conclusion: 通过引入持续学习领域的稳定性-可塑性权衡机制，本文成功缓解了早退网络顺序训练中的性能退化问题，验证了约束关键权重或保留输出分布策略的有效性。

Abstract: Early-exiting neural networks enable adaptive inference by allowing inputs to exit at intermediate classifiers, reducing computation for easy samples while maintaining high accuracy. In practice, exits can be trained sequentially by incrementally adding them to a shared backbone; however, this sequential training can cause newly introduced exits to interfere with previously learned ones, degrading the performance of earlier classifiers. We address this problem by retaining the knowledge embedded in existing exits while allowing new ones to specialize. We propose two alternative approaches that operate at different levels of the model. The first constrains learning by protecting parameters that are important for previously trained exits, while the second preserves the output distributions of earlier exits as the network adapts. These alternatives directly reflect the stability-plasticity trade-off studied in continual learning. Accordingly, we leverage \textit{Elastic Weight Consolidation} to constrain critical weights and \textit{Learning without Forgetting} to preserve output distributions. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that our approaches consistently improve early-exit performance, achieving higher accuracy over existing sequential training methods and significant performance speedups at low computational budgets.

</details>


### [69] [COPYCOP: Ownership Verification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05360)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CopyCop算法，用于检测图神经网络(GNN)的抄袭行为，即识别一个GNN是否通过模仿另一个GNN的节点嵌入并经过变换来掩盖其来源，该方法在模型架构、权重和嵌入维度不同的情况下依然有效，并提供了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的水印和指纹识别方法难以检测经过变换掩盖的GNN模型抄袭行为。当攻击者训练一个GNN专门模仿另一个GNN的嵌入，并对输出嵌入进行变换以模糊两者关系时，如何判断两个GNN是否独立训练成为一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出CopyCop算法，该算法能够在两个GNN具有不同架构、权重和嵌入维度，且攻击者对嵌入进行变换的情况下，识别抄袭型GNN。方法包含理论保证，确保检测的有效性。

Result: 在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上的实验表明，CopyCop在准确性和鲁棒性方面表现优异，能够抵御多种对抗性攻击和变换。

Conclusion: CopyCop为GNN模型所有权验证提供了一种有效、鲁棒且具有理论保证的解决方案，克服了现有方法在检测抄袭型GNN时的局限性。

Abstract: Given two GNNs that output node embeddings, how can we determine if they were trained independently? An adversary could have trained one GNN specifically to mimic the other GNN's embeddings. To obscure this relationship between the GNNs, the adversarial GNN might then transform its output embeddings. The two GNNs could have different architectures, weights, and embedding dimensions, and the adversary can transform the embeddings. Despite these stringent conditions, our algorithm (named CopyCop) can identify such copycat GNNs, unlike existing watermarking and fingerprinting methods. We also provide theoretical guarantees for CopyCop. Finally, experiments on 14 datasets and 5 GNN architectures demonstrate that CopyCop is accurate and robust against a broad class of adversarial attacks and transformations. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CopyCop-Graph-Ownership-Verification-8143/README.md

</details>


### [70] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SPADE算法，用于解决零样本药物发现问题。该算法平均仅需40次测试即可发现10个高质量配体，在样本效率上比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法中位数提高了7%-32%，且评分速度比最接近的竞争对手快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中，仅有不到5%的候选配体能通过早期筛选，且对于缺乏先验数据的新颖蛋白质，需要从头开始迭代选择和测试候选配体。目标是开发一种方法，能在尽可能少的测试次数内，为新颖蛋白质目标找到足够数量的高质量配体。

Method: 提出了一种名为SPADE（Sparse data drug discovery）的新颖算法。该算法专注于从稀疏数据中学习，针对零样本（从零开始）的场景进行配体选择，旨在以极少的测试次数高效发现高质量配体。

Result: SPADE平均仅需40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体。在一对一比较中，SPADE在更多蛋白质目标上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，实现了7%-32%的样本效率中位数提升。此外，SPADE在候选药物评分速度上比最接近的竞争对手快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE算法在针对新颖蛋白质的药物发现任务中表现出卓越的样本效率和计算效率，能够以极低的成本（测试次数少、速度快）发现高质量配体，为解决零样本药物发现难题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [71] [Neural Co-state Policies: Structuring Hidden States in Recurrent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05373)
*David Leeftink,Max Hinne,Marcel van Gerven*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将强化学习中的循环策略的隐状态与庞特里亚金最小值原理（PMP）的协态建立形式化联系，提出PMP协态损失来显式构建网络内部动态，提升了部分可观测连续控制任务的性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境下，基于记忆的强化学习策略虽然能通过隐状态编码历史信息，但其内部动态是不可解释的黑箱，缺乏理论指导与鲁棒性保证。

Method: 从最优控制理论的庞特里亚金最小值原理出发，证明标准循环架构的隐状态表示直接对应PMP的协态（co-states），读出层可解释为哈密顿量最小化；据此引入PMP协态损失，显式约束网络内部动态。

Result: 在部分可观测DMControl任务上，该方法性能匹配或超越基线方法，并对零样本分布外传感器遮蔽具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将循环网络视为由最小值原理支配的动态过程，本文为设计鲁棒的连续控制策略提供了有理论原则的方法，同时提升了模型的可解释性。

Abstract: A key capability of intelligent agents is operating under partial observability: reasoning and acting effectively despite missing or incomplete state observations. While recurrent (memory-based) policies learned via reinforcement learning address this by encoding history into latent state representations, their internal dynamics remain uninterpretable black boxes. This paper establishes a formal link between these hidden states and the Pontryagin minimum principle (PMP) from optimal control. We demonstrate that for standard recurrent architectures, latent representations map directly to PMP co-states, which allows the readout layer to be interpreted as performing Hamiltonian minimization. Because standard reward maximization does not naturally discover this alignment, we introduce a PMP-derived co-state loss to explicitly structure the internal dynamics. Empirically, this approach matches or improves performance on partially observable DMControl tasks, and is robust against zero-shot out-of-distribution sensor masking. By framing recurrent networks as dynamic processes governed by the minimum principle, we provide a principled approach to designing robust continuous control policies.

</details>


### [72] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG
