First, they require careful selection of steering factors on a per-SV basis to balance steering effectiveness and generation quality at inference time. Second, they operate as full-sequence SVs (FSSVs), which can sacrifice generation quality regardless of factor selection due to excessive intervention on the model generation process. To address the first limitation, we propose joint training of steering factors and directions, such that post-hoc factor selection is no longer required. Using neural network scaling theory, we find that moderately large initialization sizes and learning rates for steering factors are essential for stability and efficiency of joint training. To tackle the second limitation, we draw inspiration from representation fine-tuning and introduce Prompt-only SV (PrOSV), an SV that intervenes only on a few prompt tokens. Our empirical results show that PrOSV outperforms traditional FSSVs on AxBench when using our joint training scheme. We also find that PrOSV achieves a better tradeoff between general model utility and adversarial robustness than FSSV.

</details>


### [144] [DiBA: Diagonal and Binary Matrix Approximation for Neural Network Weight Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05994)
*Nobutaka Ono*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiBA，一种用于神经网络权重压缩的紧凑矩阵分解方法，通过将稠密矩阵近似为对角阵和二值矩阵的乘积，显著减少存储和计算成本，并在下游任务中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经网络的许多组件（如线性层、1×1卷积、注意力投影和嵌入层）包含大量稠密矩阵权重，导致模型存储和计算成本高昂。现有的压缩方法在存储效率和近似精度之间存在权衡，且在适应下游任务时往往需要复杂的离散搜索。因此，需要一种既能高效压缩又能方便适应下游任务的矩阵分解方法。

Method: 提出DiBA（Diagonal and Binary Matrix Approximation）方法，将稠密矩阵A∈R^(m×n)近似分解为D₁B₁D₂B₂D₃的形式，其中D₁、D₂、D₃为对角矩阵，B₁、B₂为0/1二值矩阵。该方法：(1) 将矩阵向量乘法分解为三次逐元素缩放和两次二值混合操作，将浮点乘法次数从mn降至m+k+n；(2) 提出DiBA-Greedy交替求解器，结合对角因子的闭式最小二乘更新和二值因子的精确单比特改进测试；(3) 提出DiBARD方法，在下游数据上仅微调对角因子而冻结二值矩阵，避免离散搜索。

Result: 在40个公开预训练模型的稠密权重矩阵上，DiBA-Greedy随理论存储比增加呈现一致的SNR提升。在两个组件替换研究中，DiBARD将DistilBERT/WikiText的掩码token准确率从0.4447提升至0.5210，将Audio Spectrogram Transformer在Speech Commands上的测试准确率从0.7684提升至0.9781。

Conclusion: DiBA提供了一种有效的神经网络权重压缩方案，通过对角矩阵和二值矩阵的组合实现了存储和计算效率的显著提升。DiBA-Greedy求解器能够有效优化分解参数，而DiBARD方法在下游任务适应中展现出优异性能，证明了该方法在实际应用中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose DiBA (Diagonal and Binary Matrix Approximation), a compact matrix factorization for neural network weight compression. Many components of modern networks, including linear layers, $1\times1$ convolutions, attention projections, and embedding layers, have dense matrix weights. DiBA approximates $A\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$ by $\widehat A=D_1B_1D_2B_2D_3$, where $D_1,D_2,D_3$ are diagonal matrices and $B_1,B_2$ are $0/1$ binary matrices. The intermediate dimension $k$ controls the trade-off between theoretical storage and approximation accuracy. For matrix-vector products, DiBA decomposes dense multiplication into three element-wise scaling operations and two binary mixing operations, reducing the floating-point multiplication count from $mn$ to $m+k+n$. For optimization, we introduce DiBA-Greedy, an alternating solver that combines closed-form least-squares updates for the diagonal factors with exact one-bit improvement tests for the binary factors. We also introduce DiBARD (DiBA with Retuning only Diagonal factors), which replaces dense-matrix layers by DiBA factors, freezes the binary matrices, and retunes only the diagonal entries on downstream data. This preserves compact binary mixing without discrete search during adaptation. On 40 dense weight matrices extracted from public pretrained models, DiBA-Greedy yields consistent SNR improvements as the theoretical storage ratio increases. After DiBA replacement in two component-replacement studies, DiBARD improves DistilBERT/WikiText masked-token accuracy from 0.4447 to 0.5210 and Speech Commands test accuracy for an Audio Spectrogram Transformer from 0.7684 to 0.9781 without reoptimizing the binary factors.

</details>


### [145] [A Fine-Grained Understanding of Uniform Convergence for Halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06004)
*Aryeh Kontorovich,Kasper Green Larsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文精细刻画了半空间假设类的均匀收敛行为：非齐次半空间的VC界基本紧确，而二维齐次半空间则展现出显著更优的收敛速率，揭示了维度和结构对收敛性的尖锐阈值效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的VC界给出了最坏情况下的统一收敛保证，但缺乏对具体假设类结构的细致区分。本文旨在突破最坏情况VC界的限制，深入探究半空间假设类在不同维度和结构（齐次vs非齐次）下的细粒度均匀收敛行为。

Method: 针对非齐次半空间构造下界实例，证明标准一阶VC界的紧确性；对于二维齐次半空间，在可实现情形直接分析一致假设的误差，在不可知情形提出关键的“临界楔定位”论证技术，建立分带且无对数项的偏差界，并通过在二进风险带上的并集分析。

Result: 1. 非齐次半空间（$d\geq 2$）：可实现情形下一致假设的总体误差可达$Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$，不可知情形下偏差以$\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$为尺度，标准VC界基本紧确。
2. 二维齐次半空间：可实现情形下，所有一致假设的误差仅为$O(1/n)$；不可知情形下，在每个二进风险带上获得无对数项的偏差界，整体并集仅引入$\ln\ln n$的额外开销，且证明该开销不可避免。

Conclusion: 本文为半空间假设类的均匀收敛提供了近乎完整的精细刻画，揭示了尖锐的维度和结构阈值：非齐次半空间遵循传统VC界，而二维齐次半空间享有显著更优的收敛保证。提出的临界楔定位技术为后续研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We study the fine-grained uniform convergence behavior of halfspaces beyond worst-case VC bounds. For inhomogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $d\ge 2$, we show that standard first-order VC bounds are essentially tight: even consistent hypotheses can incur population error $Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$, and in the agnostic setting the deviation scales as $\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$ at true error $τ$. In contrast, homogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^2$ exhibit a markedly different behavior. In the realizable case, every hypothesis consistent with the sample has error $O(1/n)$. In the agnostic case, we prove a bandwise, log-free deviation bound on each dyadic risk band via a critical-wedge localization argument. Unioning over bands incurs only a $\ln\ln n$ overhead, and we establish a matching lower bound showing this overhead is unavoidable. Together, these results give a fine-grained and nearly complete picture of uniform convergence for halfspaces, revealing sharp dimensional and structural thresholds.

</details>


### [146] [Matrix-Decoupled Concentration for Autoregressive Sequences: Dimension-Free Guarantees for Sparse Long-Context Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06017)
*Pei-Sen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了矩阵解耦集中（MDC）框架，解决了自回归大语言模型中序列级评估的集中界问题，实现了维度无关的O(1)方差代理，为长上下文推理的稳定性提供了数学证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型的序列级评估依赖于高度依赖的token生成过程。现有框架在建立紧密集中界时面临两个基本瓶颈：（1）经典不等式将依赖结构与目标敏感度分离，导致标量坍缩，使得稀疏终端奖励的方差代理膨胀至次优的O(N)；（2）空间方法虽然能获得更紧的界，但缺乏序列生成所需的严格因果过滤，无法应用于自回归场景。

Method: 建立了依赖序列的尖锐McDiarmid型不等式，由因果依赖预解矩阵与目标敏感度向量的精确矩阵-向量乘法严格支配。该矩阵解耦集中（MDC）框架原生恢复马尔可夫链的最优常数，并利用有向d-分离为因果树提供阶最优界。核心是在严格因果框架内精确保持奖励的坐标稀疏性，从而数学上防止标量坍缩。

Result: MDC框架保证维度无关的O(1)方差代理，原生恢复马尔可夫链的最优常数，为因果树提供阶最优界。通过精确保持奖励的坐标稀疏性，数学上防止了标量坍缩问题。

Conclusion: MDC框架成功解决了自回归设置中的集中界问题，为长上下文推理的稳定性提供了严格的数学证明，在理论和应用层面都具有重要意义。

Abstract: Sequence-level evaluations in autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on highly dependent token generation. Establishing tight concentration bounds for these processes remains a challenge due to two fundamental bottlenecks in existing frameworks: (i) classical inequalities typically separate dependency structures from target sensitivities, leading to a scalar collapse that inflates the variance proxy to a suboptimal $\mathcal{O}(N)$ for sparse terminal rewards; (ii) conversely, while certain spatial methods achieve tighter bounds, they lack the strictly causal filtration required by sequential generation, rendering them inapplicable to the autoregressive setting. To resolve both bottlenecks, we establish a sharp McDiarmid-type inequality for dependent sequences, governed strictly by the exact matrix-vector multiplication of the causal dependency resolvent and the target sensitivity vector. This Matrix-Decoupled Concentration (MDC) framework natively recovers optimal constants for Markov chains and exploits directed $d$-separation to yield order-optimal bounds for causal trees. Crucially, by exactly preserving the coordinate-wise sparsity of rewards within a strictly causal framework, MDC mathematically prevents scalar collapse, guaranteeing a dimension-free $\mathcal{O}(1)$ variance proxy and providing a rigorous mathematical justification for the stability of long-context reasoning.

</details>


### [147] [Multi-agent decision making: A Blackwell's informativeness approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06028)
*Zheng Zhang,Cuong C. Nguyen,Kevin Wells,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Blackwell信息性框架分析多LLM决策，证明了贝叶斯池化后验最大化是理论上最优的决策规则，并提出了一种近似池化后验的实用方法，在问答任务上超越了现有的投票和辩论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）的快速发展推动了多智能体系统决策研究，但现有的聚合方法（如投票和辩论）缺乏形式化保证，无法确保决策的信息性，因此需要一种有原则的方法来分析和改进多LLM系统的决策过程。

Method: 基于Blackwell信息性框架，首先理论证明了投票和辩论方法的信息结构不会比所有智能体池化私有信息更具有信息性，确立了贝叶斯池化后验最大化作为Blackwell排序下的理论上界决策规则；然后提出了一种实用方法，通过估计每个智能体的后验概率并使用后验乘积估计器来近似池化后验。

Result: 在六个问答（QA）基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法优于当前最先进的多LLM辩论和投票方法，验证了理论分析的有效性和实际方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 该研究为多LLM决策系统提供了理论指导和实践方法，表明基于信息理论的池化后验方法比传统的投票和辩论更有效，为未来多智能体协作系统的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has motivated research on decision-making in multi-agent systems, where multiple agents collaborate to achieve shared objectives. Existing aggregation approaches, such as voting and debate, are largely ad-hoc and lack formal guarantees regarding the informativeness of the resulting decisions. In this paper, we provide a principled approach to analyse decisions made in the multi-LLM setting using Blackwell's informativeness framework. Within the Blackwell information-structure abstraction, we show that voting and debate induce information structures that are no more informative than the pooled private information of all agents. This result identifies Bayesian pooled posterior maximisation as an information-theoretic upper-bound decision rule under the Blackwell ordering. Motivated by this theoretical analysis, we introduce a practical method for LLM-based question-answering (QA) tasks that estimates each agent's posterior and approximates the pooled posterior using a product-of-posteriors estimator. Extensive experiments on six QA benchmarks demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art multi-LLM debate and voting methods.

</details>


### [148] [Does Synthetic Data Help? Empirical Evidence from Deep Learning Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06032)
*Hugo Cazaux,Eyjólfur Ingi Ásgeirsson,Hlynur Stefánsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验系统性地研究了合成数据在时间序列预测中的作用，发现其效果高度依赖于模型架构：通道混合模型（如TimesNet、iTransformer）通常受益，而通道独立模型（如DLinear、PatchTST）则受损。总体而言，合成数据增强在多数情况下反而降低了性能，仅在低资源场景下对特定架构有显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据已在语言模型训练中取得变革性成果，但其在时间序列预测领域的作用尚不明确。为填补这一认知空白并提供实践指导，作者开展了大规模系统性研究。

Method: 进行了大规模实证研究：设置9个实验组，包含4,218次运行。系统性地评估了合成时间序列增强技术，涵盖5种模型架构、4种合成信号生成方法以及7个数据集。

Result: 效果呈现显著的架构条件性：通道混合模型（TimesNet、iTransformer）在多数试验中受益；通道独立模型（DLinear、PatchTST）一致受损。在特定低资源设置下增益显著（如TimesNet仅用10%数据加增强超越全数据基线），但平均来看，67%的试验中增强导致了性能下降。仅有Seasonal-Trend生成器可靠有效，而硬性课程切换主动有害（MSE恶化24%）。

Conclusion: 研究提供了具体的行动指南：应在通道混合架构下使用合成增强，采用渐进退火调度，并将低资源增强视为依赖架构与数据集的策略。这为合成数据在时序预测中的正确应用指明了方向。

Abstract: Synthetic data has transformed language model training, yet its role in time series forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a large-scale empirical study: nine experiment groups, 4,218 runs systematically evaluating synthetic time series augmentation across five architectures, four synthetic signals and seven datasets. The effect is sharply architecture-conditional: channel-mixing models (TimesNet, iTransformer) benefit in the majority of trials, while channel-independent models (DLinear, PatchTST) are consistently degraded. In selected low-resource settings the gains are striking: TimesNet trained on only 10\% of Weather data with synthetic augmentation surpasses the full-data baseline (4 of 16 sparsity-dataset combinations). Averaged across all architectures, augmentation hurts in 67\% of trials. We further find that only the Seasonal-Trend generator reliably helps across the tested benchmarks, and that hard curriculum switching is actively harmful (+24\% MSE degradation). These results provide concrete, actionable guidelines on how to use synthetic data: use synthetic augmentation with channel-mixing architectures, use gradual annealing schedules, and treat low-resource augmentation as architecture- and dataset-dependent. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/hugoiscracked/synthetic-ts/tree/main}

</details>


### [149] [Optimal Transport for LLM Reward Modeling from Noisy Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06036)
*Licheng Pan,Haochen Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yunsheng Lu,Yongqi Tong,Yinuo Wang,Shijian Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SelectiveRM框架，利用最优传输理论解决RLHF奖励模型训练中的噪声偏好问题，通过联合一致性差异和质量松弛机制自动过滤噪声样本，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型是RLHF的核心组件，但真实数据集不可避免地包含噪声偏好。传统训练目标容易过拟合这些错误，而现有的去噪方法通常依赖同质噪声假设，无法捕捉语言偏好中复杂的异质噪声模式。

Method: 基于最优传输理论提出SelectiveRM框架。首先设计联合一致性差异来对齐模型预测与偏好数据的分布；其次引入质量松弛机制，通过部分传输放宽严格的质量守恒约束，使模型能够自主排除与语义一致性相矛盾的噪声样本。

Result: 理论上证明了SelectiveRM优化了未观测干净风险的更紧上界；实验结果表明该方法在多个基准测试上显著优于现有的最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: SelectiveRM成功解决了奖励模型训练中的噪声偏好问题，通过最优传输和部分传输机制实现了对噪声样本的有效识别和过滤，为RLHF中的鲁棒奖励模型训练提供了新的理论支撑和实践方案。

Abstract: Reward models are fundamental to Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), yet real-world datasets are inevitably corrupted by noisy preference. Conventional training objectives tend to overfit these errors, while existing denoising approaches often rely on homogeneous noise assumptions that fail to capture the complexity of linguistic preferences. To handle these challenges, we propose SelectiveRM, a framework grounded in optimal transport. We first devise a Joint Consistency Discrepancy to align the distribution of model predictions with preference data. Furthermore, to address the limitation of strict mass conservation which compels the model to fit outliers, we incorporate a Mass Relaxation mechanism via partial transport. This enables the autonomous exclusion of samples with noisy preference that contradict semantic consistency. Theoretically, we demonstrate that SelectiveRM optimizes a tighter upper bound on the unobserved clean risk. Extensive experiments validate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [150] [Requests of a Feather Must Flock Together: Batch Size vs. Prefix Homogeneity in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06046)
*Saksham Rathi,Preeti,Mythili Vutukuru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Feather，一种基于强化学习的调度器，通过构建更小但前缀同构的批次来提升LLM解码吞吐量，相比现有方案实现2-10倍性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理调度器在处理前缀共享工作负载时，仅关注通过最大化前缀复用来减少KV缓存内存占用，却忽略了更小且前缀同构的批次能带来更好的空间和时间局部性，从而实现更高的解码吞吐量。此外，现有的基于基数树的前缀检测方法会带来与GPU执行时间相当的显著CPU开销。

Method: 提出Feather调度器，利用强化学习（RL）学习批次大小与前缀同构性之间的最优权衡。引入Chunked Hash Tree（CHT）轻量级数据结构，实现快速前缀检测和高效请求选择，避免了昂贵的树遍历开销。

Result: 将Feather集成到vLLM和SGLang中，在前缀共享工作负载下实现了2-10倍的端到端吞吐量提升；在缺乏足够前缀共享的工作负载下性能不劣于现有方案。Feather通过减少KV缓存访问总次数，超越了具有相同目标的感知前缀注意力内核的性能。

Conclusion: 通过RL调度器和CHT数据结构的结合，Feather成功证明了在前缀共享场景下，优先考虑前缀同构性而非单纯最大化批次大小能显著提升推理效率，为LLM推理优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Auto-regressive token generation in large language models is memory-bound because it requires "attending to" key and value tensors (KV cache) of all previous tokens. Prior work aims to improve the efficiency of this decode process by batching multiple requests together, and maximizing batch size subject to GPU memory constraints. The key observation of our work is that with prefix-sharing workloads, smaller, prefix-homogeneous batches -- where all requests share a common prefix -- can achieve higher decode throughput than larger, heterogeneous batches, due to better spatial and temporal locality during KV cache accesses. However, prefix-aware schedulers in state-of-the-art inference engines maximize prefix reuse within a batch only to reduce KV cache memory footprint, but do not stop batch formation at smaller homogeneous batches that could have performed better. Further, we show that shared prefix detection in existing schedulers relies on radix-tree traversals, incurring substantial CPU overhead that is often comparable to GPU execution time. This paper presents Feather, a prefix-aware scheduler that uses reinforcement learning (RL) to learn the optimal tradeoff between batch size and prefix homogeneity. We also introduce Chunked Hash Tree (CHT), a lightweight data structure that enables fast prefix detection and efficient request selection for the RL scheduler, avoiding expensive tree traversals. We integrate Feather into vLLM and SGLang, and our evaluation shows that Feather achieves 2--10$\times$ higher end-to-end throughput as compared to existing schedulers, while doing no worse than the status quo when the workload does not have enough prefix sharing. Feather achieves these gains by reducing the total number of KV cache accesses, surpassing the performance of prefix-aware attention kernels that have the same goal.

</details>


### [151] [TFM-Retouche: A Lightweight Input-Space Adapter for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06047)
*Duong Nguyen,Mohammed Jawhar,Nicolas Chesneau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TFM-Retouche，一种轻量级、架构无关的表格基础模型输入空间残差适配器，通过在输入空间学习残差校准来适应预训练模型，在TabArena-Lite基准上显著提升性能且位于预测质量与效率的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（TFMs）的归纳偏置在推理时固定，针对特定数据集或任务的适配通常需要计算开销大的全量微调，或需要针对特定架构设计的参数高效微调方法（如LoRA），且权重空间微调对准确率和校准的效果存疑。因此需要一种轻量级、架构无关的适配方案。

Method: 提出TFM-Retouche，一种轻量级的输入空间残差适配器，架构无关且保持TFM主干冻结。该方法在输入空间学习一个小的残差校正，将输入数据与预训练模型的归纳偏置对齐。适配器通过冻结的TFM端到端训练，并配备训练后身份保护机制，当适配在验证集上无效时回退到原始TFM。

Result: 在TabArena-Lite（51个数据集，涵盖二分类、多分类和回归任务）上，TabICLv2-Retouche成为排行榜第一名，相比冻结的TabICLv2基础模型，聚合Elo提升+56，并位于预测质量与训练/推理时间的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: TFM-Retouche为表格基础模型提供了一种高效、通用的适配范式，无需修改模型权重即可实现性能提升，兼具轻量级调优和优异的预测性能，为TFM的实际应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs), such as TabPFN-2.6, TabICLv2, ConTextTab, Mitra, LimiX, and TabDPT, achieve strong zero-shot performance through in-context learning, but their inductive biases remain fixed at inference time. Adapting a pretrained TFM to a specific dataset or task typically requires either full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive, or parameter-efficient tuning methods (PEFT) such as LoRA, which must be tailored to the internal architecture of each TFM. Furthermore, the evidence on whether weight-space fine-tuning improves accuracy or calibration is mixed \citep{tanna_exploring_2026,rubachev_finetuning_2025}. We introduce TFM-Retouche, a lightweight input-space residual adapter that is architecture-agnostic by design with respect to the frozen TFM backbone. TFM-Retouche learns a small residual correction in the input space to align the input data with the inductive biases of the pretrained model. The adapter is trained end-to-end through the frozen TFM, with a post-training identity guard that falls back to the unmodified TFM whenever adaptation does not help on held-out validation. On TabArena-Lite (51 datasets spanning binary classification, multiclass classification, and regression), TabICLv2-Retouche -- the framework instantiated on TabICLv2 -- is the top-ranked method on the leaderboard with light per-task tuning and ensembling, lifting aggregate Elo by +56 over the frozen TabICLv2 base and sitting on the Pareto front of predictive quality versus both training and inference time.

</details>


### [152] [When Brain Networks Travel: Learning Beyond Site](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06050)
*Yingxu Wang,Kunyu Zhang,Yanwu Yang,Thomas Wolfers,Yujie Wu,Siyang Gao,Nan Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CORE框架，通过解耦站点混淆因子、建模瞬态神经动力学和引入先验引导的自适应门控机制，解决了跨站点分布外（OOD）场景下脑网络分析的性能下降问题，在多个真实数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图学习的fMRI脑网络分析方法在跨站点分布外（OOD）场景下性能下降严重。主要原因有两个：一是站点条件化的混淆因子导致非病理性的捷径，二是通过时间平均构建的功能连接掩盖了瞬态神经动力学特性，限制了模型对未见过站点的泛化能力。

Method: 提出CORE统一框架：1) 执行站点感知的混淆因子解耦，缓解站点条件化偏差并提取具有可复现诊断连接边的跨站点群体支架；2) 利用轻量级时间描述符在支架上刻画瞬态通路动力学，并将支架边组织成线图以实现可迁移的通路级建模；3) 引入先验引导的受试者自适应门控机制，在利用支架衍生的群体先验的同时保留受试者特异性的连接变异性。

Result: 在真实世界数据集（ABIDE, REST-meta-MDD, SRPBS和ABCD）上进行的留一站点交叉验证实验表明，CORE持续优于最先进的基线方法，相对提升高达6.7%。此外，CORE对脑图谱划分方案的变化具有鲁棒性，在不同脑分区方案下均能保持性能优势。

Conclusion: CORE框架有效解决了跨站点脑网络分析中的分布偏移问题，通过解耦站点偏差和建模瞬态动力学特性，显著提升了模型在未见站点上的泛化能力和鲁棒性，为跨中心神经影像研究提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Graph-based learning on functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) has shown strong potential for brain network analysis. However, existing methods degrade under cross-site out-of-distribution (OOD) settings because site-conditioned confounders induce non-pathological shortcuts, while functional connectivity constructed by temporal averaging obscures transient neurodynamics, limiting generalization to unseen sites. In this paper, we propose Cross-site OOD Robust brain nEtwork (CORE), a unified framework for brain network learning across unseen sites. CORE first performs site-aware confounder decoupling to mitigate site-conditioned bias and extract a cross-site population scaffold of reproducible diagnostic connectivity edges. It then profiles transient pathway dynamics over this scaffold using lightweight temporal descriptors and organizes scaffold edges into a line graph for transferable pathway-level modeling. Finally, CORE introduces a prior-guided subject-adaptive gating mechanism that leverages scaffold-derived population priors while preserving subject-specific connectivity variability. Extensive experiments under leave-one-site-out evaluation on real-world datasets (ABIDE, REST-meta-MDD, SRPBS, and ABCD) show that CORE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with up to 6.7% relative gain. Furthermore, CORE remains robust to atlas variations, maintaining performance gains across different brain parcellation schemes.

</details>


### [153] [Towards Generation-Efficient Uncertainty Estimation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06053)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Yu Liu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架将LLM不确定性估计视为早停估计问题，提出Logit Magnitude（基于部分生成）和MetaUE（仅输入）两种低成本方法，证明有效的不确定性估计所需生成步数远少于常规假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性估计方法需要一次或多次完整的自回归生成，导致推理成本高昂且延迟评估；而在医疗、金融等高风险应用中，幻觉问题使得用户难以判断输出可信度，亟需低成本、高效的不确定性估计方法。

Method: 1) 建立统一框架，按观测信息量（多生成→部分生成→仅输入）组织不确定性估计器，阐明性能-成本权衡；2) 提出Logit Magnitude：利用top-M logit证据从早期停止的生成前缀估计不确定性；3) 提出MetaUE：将基于生成的不确定性分数蒸馏到轻量级仅输入估计器。

Result: 在通用和领域特定基准上：1) Logit Magnitude实现强性能，部分生成通常足以进行有效不确定性估计；2) MetaUE在多种场景下提供有竞争力的仅输入近似；3) 识别不可靠响应可比常规假设更早完成。

Conclusion: 有效的不确定性估计所需生成量远少于常规认知，这为LLM在高风险领域的部署提供了更低延迟、更低成本的可靠性评估方案，使不可靠响应能被更早识别。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is important for deploying LLMs in high-stakes applications such as healthcare and finance, where hallucinations can appear fluent and plausible while being factually incorrect, making it difficult for users to judge whether an output should be trusted. Existing methods require one or more full autoregressive generations to estimate uncertainty, which introduces substantial inference cost and often delays uncertainty assessment. In this paper, we investigate whether effective uncertainty estimation can be achieved with partial generation or even input-only information. Specifically, we first develop a unified framework that formulates uncertainty estimation as an early estimation problem over the autoregressive generation process of LLMs. This framework organises existing and proposed estimators by the information they observe, ranging from multi-generation to input-only prediction, and clarifies the performance-cost trade-off underlying different uncertainty estimation methods. Building on this view, we study two largely underexplored low-cost settings: estimating uncertainty with part of the generation, and predicting uncertainty from the input prompt. We propose Logit Magnitude, which uses top-M logit evidence to estimate uncertainty from an early-stopped generation prefix, and MetaUE, which distils generation-based uncertainty into a lightweight input-only estimator trained with uncertainty scores. Extensive experiments on general and domain-specific benchmarks show that Logit Magnitude achieves strong performance, and partial generations of LLMs are often sufficient for effective uncertainty estimation. MetaUE further provides a competitive input-only approximation in several settings. These findings suggest that effective uncertainty estimation requires less generation than commonly assumed, enabling unreliable responses to be identified earlier.

</details>


### [154] [Towards Self-Explainable Document Visual Question Answering with Chain-of-Explanation Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06058)
*Kjetil Indrehus,Adrian Duric,Changkyu Choi,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CoExVQA，一个自解释的文档视觉问答框架，通过链式解释设计实现SotA性能，ANLS比现有可解释基线提高12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DocVQA模型将问题相关证据与答案定位纠缠在一起，且主要作为黑盒运行，难以验证预测如何依赖于视觉证据，缺乏可解释性和可验证性。

Method: 提出CoExVQA框架，采用链式解释设计：首先识别问题相关证据，然后显式定位答案区域，最后仅从定位区域解码答案，实现可检查和可验证的推理过程。

Result: 在PFL-DocVQA数据集上，将解码限制在定位证据上的方法达到了可解释DocVQA的SotA性能，ANLS指标比当前可解释基线提高12%。

Conclusion: CoExVQA通过链式解释设计成功实现了透明且可验证的预测，证明了在保持高性能的同时可以实现DocVQA模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Document Visual Question Answering (DocVQA) requires vision-language models to reason not only about what information in a document is relevant to a question, but also where the answer is grounded on the page. Existing DocVQA models entangle question-relevant evidence and answer localization and operate largely as black boxes, offering limited means to verify how predictions depend on visual evidence. We propose CoExVQA, a self-explainable DocVQA framework with a grounded reasoning process through a chain-of-explanation design. CoExVQA first identifies question-relevant evidence, then explicitly localizes the answer region, and finally decodes the answer exclusively from the grounded region. Prediction via CoExVQA's chain-of-explanation enables direct inspection and verification of the reasoning process across modalities. Empirical results show that restricting decoding to grounded evidence achieves SotA explainable DocVQA performance on PFL-DocVQA, improving ANLS by 12% over the current explainable baselines while providing transparent and verifiable predictions.

</details>


### [155] [Geometry-Aware Simplicial Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06061)
*Elena Xinyi Wang,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何单纯Weisfeiler-Lehman（GSWL）测试，将顶点坐标引入颜色细化过程以区分具有相同拓扑但不同几何嵌入的网格，并建立了几何感知单纯消息传递方案表达能力的理论上界与匹配条件，结合Euler特征变换形成了完整的几何表达性刻画框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Weisfeiler-Lehman（WL）测试及其单纯扩展（SWL）仅能刻画组合层面的表达能力，对几何信息不敏感——即具有相同连通性但不同嵌入的网格无法被区分。这限制了对几何感知消息传递网络表达能力的理解和设计。

Method: 引入几何单纯Weisfeiler-Lehman（GSWL）测试，将顶点坐标融入几何单纯复形的颜色细化过程；证明几何感知单纯消息传递方案的表达能力以GSWL为上界，且存在参数使其判别能力在任意固定有限几何单纯复形族上与GSWL匹配；结合Euler特征变换（ECT）——几何单纯复形的完全不变量，构建几何表达性刻画与近似框架。

Result: （1）建立了几何感知单纯消息传递方案表达能力的理论上界（GSWL）；（2）证明了存在参数使这些方案在任意固定有限几何单纯复形族上达到GSWL的判别能力；（3）结合ECT形成了几何表达性刻画框架；（4）在合成数据与网格数据上的实验验证了理论，揭示了从组合模型到几何感知模型的清晰表达能力层次结构。

Conclusion: GSWL为几何感知单纯消息传递网络的表达能力提供了理论基础与刻画工具，填补了组合表达性与几何感知之间的空白，为设计更强大的几何深度学习架构提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The Weisfeiler--Lehman (WL) test and its simplicial extension (SWL) characterize the combinatorial expressivity of message passing networks, but they are blind to geometry, i.e., meshes with identical connectivity but different embeddings are indistinguishable. We introduce the Geometric Simplicial Weisfeiler--Lehman (GSWL) test, which incorporates vertex coordinates into color refinement for geometric simplicial complexes. In addition, we show that (i) the expressivity of geometry-aware simplicial message passing schemes is bounded above by GSWL, and (ii) that there exist parameters such that the discriminating power of GSWL is matched by these schemes on any fixed finite family of geometric simplicial complexes. Combined with the Euler Characteristic Transform (ECT), a complete invariant for geometric simplicial complexes, this yields a geometric expressivity characterization together with an approximation framework. Experiments on synthetic and mesh datasets serve to validate our theory, showing a clear hierarchy from combinatorial to geometry-aware models.

</details>


### [156] [Causal Reinforcement Learning for Complex Card Games: A Magic The Gathering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06066)
*Cristiano da Costa Cunha,Ajmal Mian,Tim French,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了MTG-Causal-RL基准，这是首个结合序列决策、部分可观测性、大掩码动作空间和显式因果结构的因果强化学习基准，并提出了CGFA-PPO方法，展示了因果视角能揭示传统标量胜率无法捕获的诊断性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果强化学习领域缺乏能够同时处理序列决策、隐藏信息、大掩码动作空间和显式因果结构的复杂系统基准。当前基准无法共同支持掩码动作空间下的因果信度分配、跨原型结构迁移以及基于结构因果模型的政策可审计性研究。

Method: 基于《万智牌》构建Gymnasium基准环境，包含3077维部分观测、478维掩码离散动作空间、5种竞技标准原型、3种奖励方案，并手工指定战略变量的结构因果模型(SCM)。提出因果图分解优势PPO(CGFA-PPO)方法，利用胜率的SCM父节点作为因子对齐的评论家目标，配合干预校准损失。采用配对种子、配对bootstrap置信区间和Holm-Bonferroni校正进行严格统计比较。

Result: 掩码PPO和CGFA-PPO均达到竞争性的分布内胜率并超越随机基线。更重要的是，通过每因子校准轨迹和留一法迁移差距，揭示了仅靠标量胜率无法暴露的诊断性结构信息，证明了因果方法在分析和理解策略方面的独特价值。

Conclusion: MTG-Causal-RL为因果强化学习、世界模型和LLM智能体研究提供了共享测试平台，使研究人员能够探讨当前基准无法同时提出的核心问题：掩码动作空间下的因果信度分配、跨原型的结构迁移以及基于SCM的政策可审计性。基准、参考基线结果和完整评估协议已开源发布。

Abstract: Causal reinforcement learning (RL) lacks benchmarks for complex systems that combine sequential decision making, hidden information, large masked action spaces, and explicit causal structure. We introduce MTG-Causal-RL, a Gymnasium benchmark built on Magic: The Gathering with a 3,077-dimensional partial observation, a 478-action masked discrete action space, five competitive Standard archetypes, three reward schemes, and a hand-specified Structural Causal Model (SCM) over strategic variables. Every episode exposes causal variables, SCM-predicted intervention effects, and per-factor credit traces, making causal credit assignment, leave-one-out cross-archetype transfer, and policy auditability first-class metrics. We adapt a panel of reference baselines: random, heuristic, masked PPO, a causal-world-model PPO variant, and an architecture-matched scalar control. We propose Causal Graph-Factored Advantage PPO (CGFA-PPO) as a reference causal agent that uses SCM parents of win probability as factor-aligned critic targets with an intervention-calibration loss. All comparisons use paired seeds, paired-bootstrap confidence intervals, and Holm-Bonferroni correction within pre-registered families. Masked PPO and CGFA-PPO reach competitive in-distribution win rates and exceed the random baseline; per-factor calibration trajectories and leave-one-out transfer gaps expose diagnostic structure that scalar win rate alone cannot. We release the benchmark, reference-baseline results, and full evaluation protocol openly. By coupling a strategically rich, partially observed domain with an explicit causal interface and statistical protocol, MTG-Causal-RL gives causal-RL, world-model, and LLM-agent research a shared testbed for questions current benchmarks cannot pose together: causal credit assignment under masked action spaces, structural transfer across archetypes, and SCM-grounded policy auditability.

</details>


### [157] [Normalized Architectures are Natively 4-Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06067)
*Maxim Fishman,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出nGPT架构，通过将权重和隐藏表示约束在单位超球面上，实现了在4-bit精度下的稳定训练，无需额外的量化干预措施，且信噪比更高、损失平面更平滑，在大规模模型上优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在4-bit精度下训练对效率至关重要，但现有方法需引入随机Hadamard变换和张量尺度计算等干预手段以保持模型质量，增加了复杂性和开销。本文旨在探索一种无需额外干预即可在低精度下稳定训练的架构。

Method: 提出nGPT架构，将权重和隐藏表示约束在单位超球面上。通过理论分析发现，该约束使得元素级乘积间形成弱正相关，从而在隐藏维度上实现信号的建设性累积，而噪声则相互抵消。在1.2B密集模型和最大3B/30B参数的混合（Mamba-Transformer）MoE模型上验证该方法，支持端到端NVFP4训练。

Result: nGPT在4-bit精度下训练时无需随机Hadamard变换或张量尺度计算等干预措施即可保持模型质量；量化噪声在标准架构和nGPT中均基本不相关，但nGPT的超球约束提升了信噪比，并产生更平坦的损失平面；随着隐藏维度增加，该效应增强，表明在更大规模模型上具有更大优势。

Conclusion: nGPT通过对权重和隐藏表示施加超球约束，本质上对低精度算术更鲁棒，实现了无需额外干预的稳定4-bit训练。该方法为大规模LLM的高效训练提供了新思路，且优势随规模扩大而增强。

Abstract: Training large language models at 4-bit precision is critical for efficiency. We show that nGPT, an architecture that constrains weights and hidden representations to the unit hypersphere, is inherently more robust to low-precision arithmetic. This removes the need for interventions-such as applying random Hadamard transforms and performing per-tensor scaling calculations-to preserve model quality, and it enables stable end-to-end NVFP4 training. We validate this approach on both a 1.2B dense model and hybrid (Mamba-Transformer) MoE models of up to 3B/30B parameters. We trace this robustness to the dot product: while quantization noise remains largely uncorrelated in both standard and normalized architectures, the signal behaves differently. In nGPT, the hypersphere constraint enhances weak positive correlations among the element-wise products, leading to a constructive accumulation of the signal across the hidden dimension while the noise continues to average out. This yields a higher effective signal-to-noise ratio and a flatter loss landscape, with the effect strengthening as the hidden dimension grows, suggesting increasing advantages at scale. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/anonymous452026/ngpt-nvfp4

</details>


### [158] [PRISM: Iterative Cross-Modal Posterior Refinement for Dynamic Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06073)
*Trimble Chang,Yihang Liu,Mingjing Han,Han Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，通过语义-行为模态划分和迭代后验优化机制，有效解决了动态文本属性图谱表示学习中模态融合不充分的问题，在时序链路预测等任务上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态方法在处理动态文本属性图谱(DyTAGs)时存在明显局限：(1)依赖刚性的载体级模态划分，无法捕捉节点语义与交互行为间的内在联系；(2)采用一次性融合策略，难以建模语义与行为间演化的依赖关系。这限制了模型对节点语义和时间依赖交互的耦合建模能力。

Method: 提出PRISM（迭代跨模态后验优化框架）：1)将DyTAG信息重构为语义模态和行为模态，提供更本质的模态划分方式；2)摒弃单步融合，设计迭代优化轨迹，通过跨模态交互逐步将语义先验转化为行为条件的后验状态；3)在优化过程中动态融合行为证据，实现语义与行为的深度耦合建模。

Result: 在DTGB基准数据集上进行全面实验：(1)时序链路预测任务上取得优异性能；(2)目的地节点检索任务上表现突出；(3)消融实验验证了语义-行为建模和迭代后验优化各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: PRISM框架成功实现了DyTAG表示学习的创新突破，证明了语义-行为模态划分和迭代后验优化策略的有效性。该方法为动态图谱的多模态学习提供了新范式，具有广泛的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Dynamic text-attributed graphs (DyTAGs) provide a powerful framework for modeling evolving systems in which node semantics and time-dependent interactions are tightly coupled. Recently, multimodal learning has emerged as a promising yet underexplored direction for enhancing DyTAG representation learning. However, existing methods typically rely on rigid modality partitions and one-shot fusion strategies, which limit their ability to capture the intrinsic and evolving dependencies between node semantics and interaction behaviors. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, an iterative cross-modal posterior refinement framework for DyTAG representation learning. PRISM organizes DyTAG information into semantic and behavioral modalities, providing a more intrinsic alternative to carrier-level modality partitions. Instead of fusing the two modalities in a single step, PRISM learns a refinement trajectory that progressively transforms semantic priors into behavior-conditioned posterior states through cross-modal interaction with behavioral evidence. Extensive experiments on DTGB benchmark datasets show that PRISM achieves strong performance on temporal link prediction and destination node retrieval tasks. Further ablation studies validate the effectiveness of semantic--behavioral modeling and iterative posterior refinement.

</details>


### [159] [Understanding diffusion models requires rethinking (again) generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06077)
*Pierre Marion,Yu-Han Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出扩散模型泛化需要超越经典统计学习理论和良性过拟合范式的新理论框架，主张研究重点应从“为何不记忆”转向“预记忆阶段学到了什么”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与监督学习存在本质不同：完全记忆训练数据的模型会生成副本而非新样本，记忆与泛化互不兼容。现有基于容量限制、隐式正则化或架构归纳偏好的理论解释相互作用尚不清晰，因此需要新的理论框架。

Method: 提出研究视角的转变：从解释扩散模型为何不记忆训练数据，转向研究模型在预记忆阶段实际学习到的内容。通过在CIFAR-10数据集上训练扩散模型进行实证研究，总结发现并提出具体的开放性问题。

Result: 实证研究揭示了扩散模型在预记忆阶段的学习特性，提炼出了若干关键开放性问题，这些问题对理解扩散模型泛化机制至关重要。

Conclusion: 理解扩散模型泛化需要根本性的新理论框架，研究焦点应转向预记忆阶段的学习过程，而非仅关注避免记忆的机制。该方向将推动扩散模型泛化理论的深入理解。

Abstract: This position paper argues that understanding generalization in diffusion models requires fundamentally new theoretical frameworks that go beyond both classical statistical learning theory and the benign overfitting paradigm developed for supervised learning. In diffusion models, unlike in supervised learning, memorization of training data and generalization to novel samples are incompatible: a model that has fully memorized its training set generates copies rather than novel data. Several theoretical explanations for why practical diffusion models nevertheless generalize have been proposed, based on capacity limitations, implicit regularization from optimization, or architectural inductive biases, but their interactions remain unclear. We argue that the field should pivot from explaining why the diffusion models do not memorize to investigating what the model actually learns during pre-memorization phase. To highlight our stance, we conduct empirical study of diffusion models trained on CIFAR-10, and we distill the findings into concrete open questions that we believe are key to improve understanding of generalization in diffusion models.

</details>


### [160] [Fast Gauss-Newton for Multiclass Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06081)
*Mikalai Korbit,Mario Zanon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了快速高斯-牛顿法（FGN），通过将多分类GGN曲率精确分解为"一对其余"项和竞争者内部协方差项，并舍弃后者，实现了对多分类softmax交叉熵曲率的高效正半定下界近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多分类softmax交叉熵中，完整的广义高斯-牛顿（GGN）曲率通过softmax协方差耦合所有输出逻辑值，随着类别数量增长，曲率-向量乘积的计算扩展性变差。

Method: 推导出多分类GGN可精确分解为"一对其余"项和半正定的竞争者内部协方差项，保留第一项得到FGN；利用FGN结构将阻尼更新转化为等效的白化行空间系统，使用共轭梯度法无矩阵求解。

Result: 实验表明：当竞争者质量集中或阻尼较大时，FGN最接近完整的softmax GGN；随着舍弃的竞争者内部协方差增大，FGN与完整GGN的偏差增加。

Conclusion: FGN提供了一种可扩展的多分类曲率近似方法，在二分类时精确成立，为大规模多分类问题提供了理论与实践支持。

Abstract: In multiclass softmax cross-entropy, the full generalized Gauss-Newton (GGN) curvature couples all output logits through the softmax covariance, making curvature-vector products harder to scale as the number of classes grows. We show that the standard multiclass GGN can be decomposed exactly into a true-vs-rest term and a positive semidefinite within-competitor covariance term. Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN) retains the first term and drops the second, yielding a positive semidefinite under-approximation of the multiclass GGN that is exact for binary classification. The derivation uses an exact true-vs-rest scalar-margin representation of softmax cross-entropy: the loss and gradient are unchanged, and the approximation enters only at the curvature level. Exploiting the FGN curvature structure, the damped update can be written as an equivalent whitened row-space system with one row per mini-batch example. We solve this system matrix-free by conjugate gradient using Jacobian-vector and vector-Jacobian products of the scalar margin map. Targeted mechanism experiments and an evaluation on a fixed-feature multiclass head support the predictions from the decomposition: FGN stays closest to the full softmax GGN when competitor mass is concentrated or damping is large, and deviates as the dropped within-competitor covariance grows.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Autoregressive RTG: Conditioning via Injection Outside Sequential Modeling in Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06104)
*Yongyi Wang,Hanyu Liu,Lingfeng Li,Bozhou Chen,Ang Li,Qirui Zheng,Xionghui Yang,Chucai Wang,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SlimDT，通过将回报预测（RTG）注入状态表示而非作为独立token，将序列长度缩短1/3，在提升推理效率的同时超越了标准Decision Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Decision Transformer (DT) 将RTG作为独立token处理，但RTG作为标量信息量远少于状态或动作向量，却占用相同计算预算；且Transformer的自注意力成本随序列长度二次增长，RTG作为独立token引入了不必要的计算开销。

Method: 提出SlimDT架构，将RTG从自回归序列中移除，在序列建模前将RTG信息注入到状态表示中，使Transformer仅处理紧凑的（状态，动作）序列，从而将序列长度减少三分之一。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试中，SlimDT在各种任务上超越标准DT，并达到与现有最先进方法相当的性能，同时显著提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: 将稀疏条件信号从信息丰富的序列中解耦，既能获得计算收益，又能提高任务性能，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Decision Transformer (DT) formulates offline reinforcement learning as autoregressive sequence modeling, achieving promising results by predicting actions from a sequence of Return-to-Go (RTG), state, and action tokens. However, RTG is a scalar that summarizes future rewards, containing far less information than typical state or action vectors, yet it consumes the same computational budget per token. Worse, the self-attention cost of Transformers grows quadratically with sequence length, so including RTG as a separate token adds unnecessary overhead. We propose SlimDT, which removes RTG from the autoregressive sequence. Instead, we inject RTG information into the state representations before the sequential modeling step, allowing the Transformer to process only a compact (state, action) sequence. This reduces the sequence length by one-third, directly improving inference efficiency. On the D4RL benchmark, SlimDT surpasses standard DT across various tasks and achieves performance comparable to existing state-of-the-art methods. Decoupling a sparse conditioning signal from an information-rich sequence thus yields both computational gains and higher task performance.

</details>


### [162] [BoostLLM: Boosting-inspired LLM Fine-tuning for Few-shot Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06117)
*Yi-Siang Wang,Kuan-Yu Chen,Yu-Chen Den,Darby Tien-Hao Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BoostLLM框架，将传统用于树模型的Boosting思想应用于大语言模型微调，通过训练序列化的PEFT适配器作为弱学习器，实现了在表格数据低样本场景下性能超越XGBoost和GPT-4o方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过序列化结构特征进行表格预测时，在低数据场景下的性能仍逊于梯度提升决策树（GBDT）。作者旨在探索Boosting范式是否能作为一种通用的LLM微调训练原则，以提升其在结构化数据低样本 regimes 的表现。

Method: 提出BoostLLM框架：1）将参数高效微调（PEFT）转化为多轮残差优化过程，训练序列化的PEFT适配器作为弱学习器；2）引入表格归纳偏置，将决策树路径作为第二输入视图与原始特征并行输入；分析表明路径视图在训练早期充当结构化教师，随后模型转向特征驱动的表示。

Result: 在多个LLM骨干网络和数据集上，BoostLLM相比标准微调取得一致提升；在广泛的样本数量范围内匹配或超越XGBoost，并以4B参数模型优于基于GPT-4o的方法；框架具有良好的扩展性，配合更强的树模型和延长Boosting轮次可获得额外增益。

Conclusion: Boosting可以作为LLM微调的通用训练原则，特别是在结构化数据的低样本场景中有效，成功将传统树模型的优势融入大语言模型训练范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been adapted to tabular prediction by serializing structured features into natural language, but their performance in low-data regimes remains limited compared to gradient-boosted decision trees (GBDTs). In this work, we revisit the boosting paradigm, traditionally associated with tree ensembles, and ask whether it can be applied as a general training principle for LLM fine-tuning. We propose BoostLLM, a framework that transforms parameter-efficient fine-tuning into a multi-round residual optimization process by training sequential PEFT adapters as weak learners. To incorporate tabular inductive bias, BoostLLM integrates decision-tree paths as a second input view alongside raw features; analysis reveals that the path view acts as a structured teacher in early training steps before the model shifts toward feature-driven representations. Empirically, BoostLLM achieves consistent improvements over standard fine-tuning across multiple LLM backbones and datasets, matching or surpassing XGBoost across a wide range of shot counts and outperforming GPT-4o-based methods with a 4B model. We further show that the framework scales: pairing with stronger tree models and extended boosting horizons yields additional gains under appropriate stabilization. These results suggest that boosting can serve as a general training principle for LLM fine-tuning, particularly in low-data regimes for structured data.

</details>


### [163] [Listwise Policy Optimization: Group-based RLVR as Target-Projection on the LLM Response Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06139)
*Yun Qu,Qi Wang,Yixiu Mao,Heming Zou,Yuhang Jiang,Yingyue Li,Wutong Xu,Lizhou Cai,Weijie Liu,Clive Bai,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有RLVR方法共享隐式目标分布投影的几何结构，提出了列表策略优化（LPO）框架，通过显式的目标投影和精确散度最小化，在多种推理任务上稳定地提升了LLM的训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于群组的策略梯度方法虽然被广泛采用，但其内在的优化机制尚不清晰。本文旨在揭示这些方法背后共享的几何结构，即它们都隐式地定义了一个目标分布并通过一阶近似向其投影，从而为算法设计提供更清晰的理论指导。

Method: 作者提出了列表策略优化（LPO）方法。该方法首先在响应单纯形上限制近端RL目标，显式定义目标分布；然后通过精确散度最小化将策略投影向该目标，解耦了投影步骤，允许灵活选择具有不同结构特性的散度函数。

Result: LPO提供了列表目标的单调改进，其投影梯度具有有界、零和及自校正特性。在多种推理任务和LLM骨干网络上的实验表明，在目标设置相同时，LPO持续优于典型的策略梯度基线，并内在地保持了优化稳定性和响应多样性。

Conclusion: LPO框架通过显式的目标投影揭示并改进了现有方法的隐式机制，实现了理论性质与实证性能的双重提升，为LLM推理能力的强化学习训练提供了更优的范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard approach for large language models (LLMs) post-training to incentivize reasoning capacity. Among existing recipes, group-based policy gradient is prevalent, which samples a group of responses per prompt and updates the policy via group-relative advantage signals. This work reveals that these optimization strategies share a common geometric structure: each implicitly defines a target distribution on the response simplex and projects toward it via first-order approximation. Building on this insight, we propose Listwise Policy Optimization (LPO) to explicitly conduct the target-projection, which demystifies the implicit target by restricting the proximal RL objective to the response simplex, and then projects the policy via exact divergence minimization. This framework provides (i) monotonic improvement on the listwise objective with bounded, zero-sum, and self-correcting projection gradients, and (ii) flexibility in divergence selection with distinct structural properties through the decoupled projection step. On diverse reasoning tasks and LLM backbones, LPO consistently improves training performance over typical policy gradient baselines under matched targets, while intrinsically preserving optimization stability and response diversity.

</details>


### [164] [SymDrift: One-Shot Generative Modeling under Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06140)
*Samir Darouich,Vinh Tong,Lluís Pastor-Pérez,Tanja Bien,Loay Mualem,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SymDrift框架，通过坐标空间对称化漂移和G不变嵌入两种策略，解决了单步生成模型在处理物理系统对称性时的挑战，在保持生成质量的同时将计算效率提升了40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在模拟分子等物理系统时需要满足旋转等全局对称性。现有的等变扩散和流匹配模型虽能处理对称性，但依赖昂贵的多步采样。新兴的单步生成（drifting）模型虽高效，但在对称性处理上存在特定挑战：等变生成器无法产生与对称化目标分布相同的漂移场，而直接对称化经验分布代价高昂。

Method: 提出SymDrift框架，通过使漂移场本身具备对称性感知能力来解决问题。引入两种互补策略：(i) 基于最优对齐的坐标空间对称化漂移；(ii) 通过构造消除对称性歧义的G不变嵌入。这两种方法避免了昂贵的经验分布对称化过程。

Result: 在标准构象生成和过渡态生成基准测试中，SymDrift优于现有单步生成方法，并与昂贵的多步方法性能相当。相比现有基线方法，计算开销降低最多40倍。

Conclusion: SymDrift成功实现了兼顾对称性的单步推理，在生成质量与计算效率间取得平衡，为虚拟药物筛选、大规模反应网络探索等高通量应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative modeling of physical systems, such as molecules, requires learning distributions that are invariant under global symmetries, such as rotations in three-dimensional space. Equivariant diffusion and flow matching models can incorporate such invariances effectively, even when trained on a non-invariant empirical distribution, but they typically rely on costly multi-step sampling. Recently, drifting models have emerged as an efficient alternative, enabling single-step generation and achieving state-of-the-art performance in generative modeling tasks. However, we show that drifting models face a symmetry-specific challenge, since an equivariant generator does not generally produce the same drifting field as the one obtained from the symmetrized target distribution. Addressing this issue would require expensive symmetrization of the empirical distribution. To avoid this cost, we propose SymDrift, a framework that makes the drifting field itself symmetry-aware. We introduce two complementary strategies: (i) a symmetrized drift in coordinate space based on optimal alignment, and (ii) a $G$-invariant embedding that removes symmetry ambiguity by construction. Empirically, SymDrift outperforms existing one-shot methods on standard benchmarks for conformer and transition state generation, while remaining competitive with significantly more expensive multi-step approaches. By enabling one-shot inference, SymDrift reduces computational overhead by up to 40$\times$ compared to existing baselines, making it promising for high-throughput applications such as virtual drug screening and large-scale reaction network exploration.

</details>


### [165] [Matrix-Valued Optimism is Matrix-Valued Augmentation: Additive Hybrid Designs for Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06141)
*Jiayi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了增广拉格朗日方法与乐观原始-对偶方法在矩阵值校正参数下的等价性，提出了可加性原理，并据此设计了一种混合算法，在约束雅可比矩阵条件数为温和到中等程度时优于单一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增广拉格朗日方法与乐观原始-对偶方法通过不同机制稳定等式约束优化：前者增加约束相关的原始曲率，后者增加对偶记忆。近期研究表明这两种机制在标量参数下等价，作者希望将此等价性扩展到矩阵值校正参数，并探索由此带来的设计自由度。

Method: 作者首先证明了对称矩阵参数的可加性原理：理想的原始轨迹仅依赖于校正矩阵之和，而与其在增广和乐观通道间的分配方式无关。基于此，作者将问题形式化为步长受限的设计问题，并推导出一个闭式混合规则：该规则选择矩阵校正，在两个通道间分配校正，并使用局部谱权重选择原始和对偶步长。

Result: 在具有可控约束雅可比矩阵条件数的非线性等式约束问题上的实验表明：混合设计优于纯增广和纯乐观端点，紧密跟踪网格搜索混合预言机，并在温和到中等条件不良情况下与一阶原始-对偶基线具有竞争力。实验也发现了预期的限制：随着约束雅可比矩阵变得病态，精确抵消需要越来越大的矩阵校正。

Conclusion: 矩阵值校正参数下增广拉格朗日方法与乐观原始-对偶方法的等价性揭示了新的设计自由度，基于此设计的混合算法能够有效平衡两种机制，但在约束雅可比矩阵严重病态时面临校正量过大的挑战。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian and optimistic primal--dual methods stabilize equality-constrained optimization through seemingly different mechanisms: the former adds constraint-dependent primal curvature, while the latter adds dual memory. Recent work has shown that these mechanisms are equivalent for scalar parameters. We extend this equivalence to matrix-valued correction. We prove an additivity principle: for symmetric matrix parameters, the ideal primal trajectory depends only on the summed correction matrix, not on how it is split between augmented and optimistic channels. This exposes a design freedom: algebraically equivalent decompositions can have different finite-step feasibility because augmented correction affects primal curvature, whereas optimistic correction affects the scale of the dual memory correction. We formulate the resulting step-size-limited design problem and derive a closed-form hybrid rule that selects a matrix correction, splits it between the two channels, and chooses primal and dual steps using local spectral weights. Experiments on nonlinear equality-constrained problems with controlled constraint-Jacobian conditioning show that the hybrid design improves over pure augmented and pure optimistic endpoints, closely tracks a grid-search hybrid oracle, and is competitive with first-order primal--dual baselines under mild-to-moderate ill-conditioning. The experiments also identify the expected limitation: exact cancellation requires increasingly large matrix corrections as the constraint Jacobian becomes ill-conditioned.

</details>


### [166] [Unifying Goal-Conditioned RL and Unsupervised Skill Learning via Control-Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06145)
*Alireza Modirshanechi,Benjamin Eysenbach,Peter Dayan,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了无监督技能预训练与下游目标条件强化学习之间的理论联系。研究发现三种目标条件强化学习范式本质不等价，但均要求策略对未来轨迹的“目标敏感性”高。通过证明互信息技能学习目标受下游目标敏感性的上界约束，论文揭示了预训练技能多样性与下游任务性能之间的精确对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互信息技能学习等无监督预训练方法在目标条件强化学习中取得了显著的经验成功，但其理论基础尚不清晰。核心问题在于：为何通过最大化行为多样性学习到的技能能够支持下游的目标达成任务？由于不同GCRL任务采用不同的性能度量标准，不同MISL方法优化不同的多样性概念，这使得建立统一的理论框架具有挑战性。

Method: 作者将GCRL和MISL统一为控制最大化的实例进行分析。首先识别三种典型的GCRL形式化定义，证明它们本质不等价（即使在同一环境中也可能诱导出不兼容的最优策略）。然后提出统一解释：高性能的目标条件策略是指其未来轨迹对指令目标高度敏感的策略，不同GCRL形式定义了不同的敏感性概念。最后，通过将MISL目标理解为类似目标敏感性的技能敏感性度量，证明MISL目标受特定形式的下游目标敏感性上界约束。

Result: 证明了三种典型的GCRL形式化定义（可能指概率目标可达性、目标条件价值函数等）在理论上是不等价的，可能产生相互冲突的最优策略。关键发现是：MISL目标与下游GCRL任务的目标敏感性之间存在精确的界限关系，即MISL目标被特定GCRL形式下的下游目标敏感性所上界约束。这建立了MISL方法与GCRL任务之间的对应关系：对于每个GCRL形式，存在一个匹配的MISL目标，使得更多样化的技能能带来更高的下游目标敏感性。

Conclusion: 本研究为强化学习预训练奠定了理论基础，表明MISL预训练之所以有效，是因为其优化的技能多样性与下游任务的目标敏感性需求相匹配。这具有重要的实践意义：当用户关注特定类别的下游任务时，可以根据理论指导选择合适的预训练目标，而非盲目使用通用的技能学习方法。研究揭示的无监督预训练与下游任务性能之间的精确对应关系，为设计更有效的预训练算法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Unsupervised pretraining has driven empirical advances in goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In particular, an influential class of methods, mutual information skill learning (MISL), discovers behaviorally diverse skills that can later be used for downstream goal-reaching. However, it remains a theoretical mystery why skills learned through MISL should support goal-reaching. A subtle challenge is that both GCRL and MISL are umbrella terms: different GCRL tasks use distinct criteria for measuring goal-reaching performance, while different MISL methods optimize distinct notions of behavioral diversity. We address this challenge and unify GCRL and MISL as instances of control maximization. We identify three canonical GCRL formulations and prove that they are fundamentally inequivalent: they can induce incompatible optimal policies even in the same environment. Nevertheless, they all share a common interpretation: a well-performing goal-conditioned policy is one whose future trajectory is highly sensitive to the commanded goal, with the precise notion of sensitivity determined by the GCRL formulation. Noting that MISL objectives can be understood as measures of skill-sensitivity akin to goal-sensitivity, we show that MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities. These bounds establish a precise correspondence between MISL methods and downstream GCRL tasks: for every GCRL formulation, there exists a matching MISL objective for which more diverse skills afford greater downstream goal sensitivity. Our results thus lay a theoretical foundation for RL pretraining and have important practical implications, such as suggesting which pretraining objectives to use when a user cares about a specific class of downstream tasks.

</details>


### [167] [AdaGamma: State-Dependent Discounting for Temporal Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06149)
*Yaomin Wang,Jianting Pan,Ran Tian,Xiaoyang Li,Yu Zhang,Hengle Qin,Tianshu YU*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AdaGamma方法，通过引入return-consistency目标正则化，实现了深度强化学习中状态相关折扣因子的有效学习，解决了传统方法的不稳定性问题，并在基准测试和实际业务中取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度强化学习方法通常对所有状态使用单一固定的折扣因子，这限制了有效规划视野和引导强度的灵活调整。虽然状态相关折扣因子在概念上具有吸引力，但朴素的深度actor-critic实现往往不稳定，容易退化为TD误差崩塌，因此亟需一种实用的解决方案。

Method: 提出AdaGamma方法，这是一种实用的深度actor-critic算法，能够学习状态相关的折扣函数，并引入return-consistency目标来正则化诱导的备份结构。在理论层面，分析了状态相关折扣因子诱导的Bellman算子，并在适当条件下建立了其基本适定性。该方法可集成到SAC和PPO算法中。

Result: 在连续控制基准测试中，AdaGamma集成到SAC和PPO后均取得了一致的性能提升；在京东物流平台的在线A/B测试中获得了统计上显著的收益。实验结果表明，结合return-consistency目标后，状态相关折扣因子能够有效防止退化性目标操纵。

Conclusion: 状态相关折扣因子在深度强化学习中可以通过return-consistency目标有效防止退化性目标操纵，从而成为一种实用的技术手段。这为深度强化学习算法提供了更灵活的规划视野和引导强度调整能力。

Abstract: The discount factor in reinforcement learning controls both the effective planning horizon and the strength of bootstrapping, yet most deep RL methods use a single fixed value across all states. While state-dependent discounting is conceptually appealing, naive deep actor--critic implementations can become unstable and degenerate toward TD-error collapse. We propose AdaGamma, a practical deep actor--critic method for state-dependent discounting that learns a state-dependent discount function together with a return-consistency objective to regularize the induced backup structure. On the theory side, we analyze the Bellman operator induced by state-dependent discounting and establish its basic well-posedness properties under suitable conditions. Empirically, AdaGamma integrates into both SAC and PPO, yielding consistent improvements on continuous-control benchmarks, and achieves statistically significant gains in an online A/B test on the JD Logistics platform. These results suggest that state-dependent discounting can be made effective in deep RL when coupled with a return-consistency objective that prevents degenerate target manipulation.

</details>


### [168] [Grokking or Glitching? How Low-Precision Drives Slingshot Loss Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06152)
*Liu Hanqing,Jianjun Cao,Yuanze Li,Zijian Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度神经网络训练中"Slingshot Mechanism"现象的根本原因是浮点数精度限制，提出了数值特征膨胀（NFI）机制来解释损失尖峰和参数异常增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将深度神经网络长期训练中出现的周期性损失尖峰（Slingshot Mechanism）归因于内在优化动力学，但其触发机制尚不明确。本文旨在从数值计算角度揭示该现象的真实成因。

Method: 通过理论证明，分析浮点运算精度限制对训练过程的影响。研究当训练进入高置信度阶段时，正确类别logit与其他类别logit的差值超过吸收误差阈值的情况，以及反向传播中梯度的舍入行为。

Result: 证明正确类别梯度被舍入为零而错误类别梯度非零，破坏了梯度零和约束，在分类器层引入系统性漂移。该漂移与特征形成正反馈循环，导致全局分类器均值和全局特征均值指数增长，形成数值特征膨胀（NFI）机制。NFI解释了Slingshot尖峰前的快速范数增长、梯度重现和损失尖峰。进一步证明NFI不等同于可见的损失尖峰，在实际任务中部分吸收可能不产生可见尖峰但仍会破坏零和约束并驱动参数范数快速增长。

Conclusion: 重新解释Slingshot现象为有限精度训练的数值动力学，为后期训练中的异常参数增长和logit发散提供了可测试的解释，揭示了浮点精度限制对深度学习训练过程的深层影响。

Abstract: Deep neural networks exhibit periodic loss spikes during unregularized long-term training, a phenomenon known as the "Slingshot Mechanism." Existing work usually attributes this to intrinsic optimization dynamics, but its triggering mechanism remains unclear. This paper proves that this phenomenon is a result of floating-point arithmetic precision limits. As training enters a high-confidence stage, the difference between the correct-class logit and the other logits may exceed the absorption-error threshold. Then during backpropagation, the gradient of the correct class is rounded exactly to zero, while the gradients of the incorrect classes remain nonzero. This breaks the zero-sum constraint of gradients across classes and introduces a systematic drift in the parameter update of the classifier layer. We prove that this drift forms a positive feedback loop with the feature, causing the global classifier mean and the global feature mean to grow exponentially. We call this mechanism Numerical Feature Inflation (NFI). This mechanism explains the rapid norm growth before a Slingshot spike, the subsequent reappearance of gradients, and the resulting loss spike. We further show that NFI is not equivalent to an observed loss spike: in more practical tasks, partial absorption may not produce visible spikes, but it can still break the zero-sum constraint and drive rapid growth of parameter norms. Our results reinterpret Slingshot as a numerical dynamic of finite-precision training, and provide a testable explanation for abnormal parameter growth and logit divergence in late-stage training.

</details>


### [169] [Entropy-Regularized Adjoint Matching for Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06156)
*Abdelghani Ghanem,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出最大熵伴随匹配（ME-AM）框架，通过镜像下降熵最大化和混合行为先验机制，解决离线强化学习中流匹配模型存在的流行度偏差和支持约束问题，实现分布外高奖励区域的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流匹配模型的离线强化学习方法（如Q-learning with Adjoint Matching）受限于固定行为分布，存在两个关键问题：（1）流行度偏差会抑制低密度区域的高奖励动作；（2）支持约束限制了分布外探索。现有解决方案（如添加残差高斯策略）会重新引入单峰分布的表达性瓶颈。

Method: 提出Maximum Entropy Adjoint Matching (ME-AM)统一框架，包含两个核心机制：（1）镜像下降熵最大化目标，缓解流行度偏差以促进从离线数据集中提取最优策略；（2）混合行为先验，在数学上扩展几何支持以包含分布外高奖励区域，在扩展几何中寻找鲁棒动作，同时保持生成向量场的绝对连续性。

Result: 在多样化的稀疏奖励连续控制环境基准测试中，ME-AM相比现有最先进方法展现出具有竞争力或更优越的性能表现。

Conclusion: ME-AM成功解决了流匹配模型在离线强化学习中的表达性限制问题，通过熵最大化和混合行为先验的协同作用，既保持了生成模型的表达能力，又实现了有效的分布外探索，为离线强化学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Integrating expressive generative policies, such as flow-matching models, into offline reinforcement learning (RL) allows agents to capture complex, multi-modal behaviors. While Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) stabilizes policy optimization via the continuous adjoint method, it remains inherently bound to the fixed behavior distribution. This dependence induces a \textit{popularity bias} that can suppress high-reward actions in low-density regions, and creates a \textit{support binding} that restricts off-manifold exploration. Existing workarounds, such as appending \textit{residual} Gaussian policies, often re-introduce the expressivity bottlenecks associated with unimodal distributions. In this work, we propose \textit{Maximum Entropy Adjoint Matching} (ME-AM), a unified framework that addresses these limitations within the continuous flow formulation. ME-AM incorporates two mechanisms: (1) a Mirror Descent entropy maximization objective that mitigates the popularity bias to facilitate the extraction of optimal policies from offline datasets, and (2) a \textit{Mixture Behavior Prior} that mathematically broadens the geometric support to encompass out-of-distribution high-reward regions. By exploring this extended geometry, ME-AM identifies robust actions while preserving the absolute continuity of the generative vector field. Empirically, ME-AM demonstrates competitive or superior performance compared to prior state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across a diverse suite of sparse-reward continuous control environments.

</details>


### [170] [One Algorithm, Two Goals: Dual Scoring for Parameter and Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06166)
*Xinrui Chen,Liu Yang,Ou Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DualSFT，一种利用共享梯度统计信息同时选择参数和数据的一步法算法，解决了传统分离式选择带来的冗余开销和协调困难问题，在3B-9B LLM上实现了比顺序混合基线更优的联合约束权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型微调中，参数选择和数据选择通常采用独立的评分机制。当参数掩码和数据子集共同决定受限微调时，这种分离会导致冗余开销，并使得协调选择变得困难。因此，需要一种统一的方法来同时进行参数和数据选择。

Method: 将参数和数据选择建模为共同验证目标下的两个双层选择问题，推导出共享的局部响应代理评分规则。在一阶和二阶验证改进近似下，参数重要性和数据效用表现为单一梯度交互矩阵的列聚合和行聚合，从而形成用于共同提取两种信号的闭通行列对应关系。基于此结构，提出DualSFT一步法双评分算法，从共享梯度统计信息中生成参数掩码和数据子集。

Result: 在3B-9B LLM上的实验表明，单轴DualSFT变体在各自的对比组中增强了目标任务性能和稳定性-可塑性权衡；完整DualSFT在匹配预算下比顺序混合基线产生更优的联合约束权衡。

Conclusion: 通过建立参数重要性和数据效用之间的统一理论联系，DualSFT成功实现了参数和数据的同时选择，不仅消除了分离式方法的开销，还在性能和权衡方面展现出显著优势，为大语言模型的高效微调提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning, parameter and data selection are common strategies for reducing fine-tuning cost, yet they are typically driven by separate scoring mechanisms. When a parameter mask and data subset jointly determine restricted fine-tuning, this separation incurs redundant overhead and makes coordinated selection difficult. We cast parameter and data selection as two bilevel selection problems under a common validation objective and derive a shared local response-surrogate scoring rule. Under first- and second-order validation-improvement approximations, parameter importance and data utility emerge as column-wise and row-wise aggregations of a single gradient interaction matrix, yielding a closed-form row-column correspondence for co-extracting both signals. Building on this structure, we propose DualSFT (Dual-Selection Fine-Tuning), a one-shot dual-scoring algorithm that produces a parameter mask and data subset from shared gradient statistics. On 3B-9B LLMs, single-axis DualSFT variants strengthen target-task performance and stability-plasticity trade-offs within their comparison groups, while full DualSFT yields a more favorable joint-constrained trade-off than sequential hybrid baselines under matched budgets.

</details>


### [171] [Mean Mode Screaming: Mean--Variance Split Residuals for 1000-Layer Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06169)
*Pengqi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了深度扩散Transformer在扩展至数百层时会出现均值主导的崩溃现象，并提出了MV-Split残差连接方法来有效解决这一问题，成功训练了1000层的DiT模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当扩散Transformer扩展到数百层深度时，会出现一种隐蔽的结构性脆弱：网络可能进入均值主导的崩溃状态，导致token表示同质化并抑制中心化变化。即使训练表面看似稳定，这种崩溃仍可能发生，严重限制了模型向更大深度扩展的能力。

Method: 通过机制审计识别出崩溃的触发事件为均值模式尖叫(MMS)，并提出均值-方差分离残差(MV-Split)方法。该方法将中心化残差更新与泄漏的主干均值替换相结合，通过精确分解梯度为均值一致分量和中心化分量来应对崩溃问题。

Result: 在400层单流DiT上，MV-Split成功阻止了未稳定基线的崩溃，在完整训练过程中表现优于LayerScale等token各向同性门控方法，并保持了接近基线崩溃前的轨迹。在1000层DiT上验证了该架构在极限深度下仍能保持稳定训练。

Conclusion: MV-Split残差连接有效解决了深度扩散Transformer的均值主导崩溃问题，使得架构能够稳定训练至1000层，证明了该方法在极限规模下的可扩展性和有效性。

Abstract: Scaling Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) to hundreds of layers introduces a structural vulnerability: networks can enter a silent, mean-dominated collapse state that homogenizes token representations and suppresses centered variation. Through mechanistic auditing, we isolate the trigger event of this collapse as Mean Mode Screaming (MMS). MMS can occur even when training appears stable, with a mean-coherent backward shock on residual writers that opens deep residual branches and drives the network into a mean-dominated state. We show this behavior is driven by an exact decomposition of these gradients into mean-coherent and centered components, compounded by the structural suppression of attention-logit gradients through the null space of the Softmax Jacobian once values homogenize.
  To address this, we propose Mean-Variance Split (MV-Split) Residuals, which combine a separately gained centered residual update with a leaky trunk-mean replacement. On a 400-layer single-stream DiT, MV-Split prevents the divergent collapse that crashes the un-stabilized baseline; it tracks close to the baseline's pre-crash trajectory while remaining substantially better than token-isotropic gating methods such as LayerScale across the full schedule. Finally, we present a 1000-layer DiT as a scale-validation run at boundary scales, establishing that the architecture remains stably trainable at extreme depth.

</details>


### [172] [In-Context Black-Box Optimization with Unreliable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06187)
*Nicolas Samuel Blumer,Julien Martinelli,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FICBO方法，将上下文黑盒优化与外部辅助反馈信息相结合。通过引入结构化反馈先验来建模反馈源的质量，预训练的Transformer模型能够动态评估反馈源的可靠性，在有效利用有价值信息的同时对噪声和误导性反馈保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和工程中的黑盒优化问题通常伴随着专家意见、模拟器或启发式方法等辅助反馈信息。现有方法存在局限性：反馈感知的贝叶斯优化方法通常一次只处理单个任务，泛化能力有限；而现有的上下文优化器则假设优化历史是测试时唯一可用的信号，忽略了丰富的外部反馈信息。因此，需要一种能够同时利用历史数据和多种辅助反馈源进行跨任务自适应优化的方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为FICBO（反馈知情的上下文黑盒优化）的方法。核心创新包括：1）引入结构化反馈先验，显式建模不同反馈源在访问权限、相关性及失真度方面的特性；2）基于此先验预训练一个反馈感知的Transformer模型；3）在测试时，模型通过比较已观测的目标函数值与辅助反馈信号，在上下文中动态估计各反馈源的可靠性，从而改进查询选择策略。

Result: 在合成任务和真实世界任务上的实验表明，FICBO能够有效利用有信息量的反馈来加速优化过程，同时对弱反馈或误导性反馈源保持鲁棒性。该方法在多个基准测试中优于其他对比基线。进一步的实证分析展示了模型如何感知测试时的反馈源，验证了其可解释性和决策过程的合理性。

Conclusion: FICBO成功桥接了反馈感知优化与上下文优化之间的空白。通过显式建模反馈源特性和动态可靠性估计，该方法能够自适应地整合多种异质反馈源，在复杂优化场景中实现了性能提升，为后续研究提供了可解释性的视角。

Abstract: Black-box optimization in science and engineering often comes with side information: experts, simulators, pretrained predictors, or heuristics can suggest which candidates look promising. This information can accelerate search, but it can also be biased, input-dependent, or misleading. Feedback-aware BO methods typically handle one task at a time, limiting their ability to generalize over multiple sources of feedback. In-context optimizers address cross-task adaptation, but usually assume that optimization history is the only available signal at test time. We study feedback-informed in-context black-box optimization (FICBO), where a pretrained optimizer conditions on both the observed history and cheap auxiliary feedback for the current candidate set. We introduce a structured feedback prior that models how feedback sources vary in their access, relevance, and distortion relative to the true objective, and use it to pretrain a feedback-aware transformer. At test time, the model estimates source reliability in context by comparing observed objective values with auxiliary signals, improving query selection. On synthetic and real-world tasks, FICBO effectively exploits informative feedback while remaining robust to weak or misleading sources, improving over other baselines. Empirical investigations further illustrate how the model perceives test-time sources, offering insights into its interpretability and decision-making process.

</details>


### [173] [Constrained Contextual Bandits with Adversarial Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06190)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于在线回归预言机的模块化框架，将预算约束的上下文赌博问题转化为标准的无约束问题，实现了在对抗性上下文场景下对后悔和预算违约的双重控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注随机上下文场景，而实际应用中常面临对抗性上下文。在持续设置（预算耗尽后算法继续运行）下，需要同时控制后悔和预算违约，这对算法设计提出了挑战。

Method: 基于Foster等人提出的SquareCB框架，构建了一个简单且模块化的框架。该框架利用在线回归预言机，通过自适应定义代理奖励函数，将预算约束问题转化为标准的无约束上下文赌博问题。

Result: 该框架在更广泛的对抗性上下文设定下获得了改进的理论保证，并提供了一个高效算法，其分析简洁且透明。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为预算约束的上下文赌博问题提供了一种有效的解决方案，在对抗性上下文下超越了现有工作的性能，并保持了算法的简洁性和分析的可解释性。

Abstract: We study budget-constrained contextual bandits with adversarial contexts, where each action yields a random reward and incurs a random cost. We adopt the standard realizability assumption: conditioned on the observed context, rewards and costs are drawn independently from fixed distributions whose expectations belong to known function classes. We focus on the continuing setting, in which the algorithm operates over the entire horizon even after the budget for cumulative cost is exhausted. In this setting, the objective is to simultaneously control regret and the violation of the budget constraint. Building on the seminal $\mathsf{SquareCB}$ framework of Foster et al. [2018], we propose a simple and modular framework that leverages online regression oracles to reduce the constrained problem to a standard unconstrained contextual bandit problem with adaptively defined surrogate reward functions. In contrast to prior works, which focus on stochastic contexts, our reduction yields improved guarantees for more general adversarial contexts, together with an efficient algorithm with a compact and transparent analysis.

</details>


### [174] [Bandit Learning in General Open Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06202)
*Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对开放系统中的强盗学习问题，提出了统一框架和认证的全局UCB学习方法，引入预训练程度、稳定性等新概念，获得了与预训练程度线性相关、在稳定环境下受持久最优臂识别时间和智能体模式影响的遗憾上界，并证明了下界的紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于开放系统（智能体随时间到达和离开）的强盗学习研究强加了一些在实际中经常被违反的结构假设。针对一般开放系统的学习范式面临新到达智能体引起的内生非平稳性、智能体模式决定信息积累速度以及新智能体使遗憾随时间范围进一步缩放等新挑战。

Method: 本文构建了包含异质奖励和一般智能体模式的统一开放系统强盗问题。引入三个新概念：新智能体的预训练程度（pre-training degree）量化其进入时携带的信息量；稳定性（stability）度量新智能体对系统的影响；全局动态遗憾（global dynamic regret）比较所有活跃智能体的累积期望奖励与变化的最优臂的累积期望奖励。在此基础上，开发了具有可证明保证的认证全局UCB（certified global-UCB）学习方法。

Result: 遗憾上界揭示：进入不确定性通过预训练程度线性进入遗憾；在稳定机制下，遗憾受识别持久最优臂所需时间以及智能体模式支配。同时在困难实例上证明了下界，表明这些依赖关系是紧的。

Conclusion: 本文为一般开放系统提供了统一的强盗学习框架，所提出的方法具有可证明的保证，遗憾界限清晰地揭示了系统关键因素的影响，为开放系统中的序贯决策提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Recent developments in digital platforms have highlighted the prevalence of open systems, where agents can arrive and depart over time. While bandit learning in open systems has recently received initial attention, existing work imposes structural assumptions that are frequently violated in practice. A learning paradigm for general open systems creates fresh challenges: newly arriving agents induce endogenous non-stationarity; agent patterns determine how quickly information accumulates; and new agents make regret scale further with the time horizon. To this end, we formulate a unified open-system bandit problem with general dynamics, including heterogeneous rewards and general agent patterns. We introduce new concepts to capture the inherent complexities: the \emph{pre-training degree} of new agents quantifies how much information an agent carries upon entry, \emph{stability} measures the impact of new agents on the system, and \emph{global dynamic regret} compares the cumulative expected reward of all active agents with that of the varying optimal arms. We develop certified global-UCB learning methodologies with provable guarantees. Our regret bounds reveal that entry uncertainty enters linearly via the pre-training degree, while in stable regimes, regret is governed by the time needed to identify a persistent optimal arm, as well as by the agent patterns. We further show that these dependencies are tight via lower bounds in hard instances.

</details>


### [175] [Federation of Experts: Communication Efficient Distributed Inference for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06206)
*Muhammad Shahir Abdurrahman,Chun Deng,Azalia Mirhoseini,Philip Levis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了专家联合（FoE）架构，通过将MoE块重构为多个MoE集群并限制all-to-all通信范围，在保持生成质量的同时显著降低了分布式大语言模型推理的通信开销和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家（MoE）模型虽然能提高大语言模型的计算效率，但在分布式环境中，专家之间的token嵌入通信成为主要瓶颈，限制了模型的推理性能和扩展能力。

Method: 提出专家联合（FoE）架构，将transformer层的MoE块重构为多个MoE集群。每个集群仅负责一个KV头，在集群内的专家之间应用专家并行，集群间通过求和同步注意力后残差来驱动下一个MoE块的路由和分发。该设计在单节点场景下完全消除all-to-all通信，在多节点场景下将all-to-all通信限制在节点内部。

Result: 在LongBench基准测试中，FoE显著提升了单节点和多节点场景下的推理吞吐量和延迟性能：端到端前向传播延迟降低5.2倍，TTFT（首个token时间）降低3.62倍，TBT（token间时间）降低1.95倍，同时保持与同等规模MoE模型相当的生成质量。

Conclusion: FoE架构成功解决了分布式MoE模型的通信瓶颈问题，通过重新设计MoE块结构和通信模式，在不牺牲模型性能的前提下大幅提升了推理效率，为大规模MoE模型的部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture of experts has emerged as the primary mechanism for making Large Language Models (LLMs) computationally efficient. However, in distributed settings, communicating token embeddings between experts is a significant bottleneck.
  We present the novel Federation of Experts (FoE) architecture. FoE restructures the MoE block of a transformer layer into multiple MoE clusters. Each cluster is responsible for only one of the KV heads and expert parallelism is applied between those experts. Between clusters, a sum synchronizes the post-attention residuals, which then drives routing and dispatch for the next MoE block. In a single-node setting, FoE completely eliminates all-to-all communication as all experts within a group are contained on the same GPU. In multi-node settings, FoE confines all-to-all communication to the intra-node fabric, thus significantly reducing communication overhead.
  An implementation of FoE finds that on LongBench, FoE significantly improves inference throughput and latency in both single-node and multi-node settings, reducing the end-to-end forward-pass latency by up to 5.2x, TTFT by 3.62x, and TBT by 1.95x. It does so while achieving comparable generation quality to a mixture of experts model of the same size and training configuration.

</details>


### [176] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了对比式极限识别与生成问题，在无噪声环境下给出了精确刻画和紧样本复杂度，并证明对比式生成与文本识别互不可比；而在有限对抗腐败下，对比式学习反而比正例学习更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极限学习模型聚焦于正例或全标注数据，但现实中的监督信号往往是关系性的（对比对），而非单点标签。为了弥补这一空白，作者引入对比式极限识别与生成框架，研究学习器在仅知两样本标签不同、但不知具体正负的情况下能否最终恢复目标假设或生成新正例。

Method: 形式化对比式数据流为无序对\{x,y\}满足h(x)≠h(y)，引入公共交叉图作为统一技术对象，编码成对歧义、族级生成障碍与腐败缺陷。无噪声部分通过几何细化Angluin的tell-tale条件刻画可识别类，定义对比闭包维数并给出紧样本复杂度；有限腐败部分设计预算无关算法并证明鲁棒性反转。

Result: 无噪声下：1）精确刻画对比可识别类（Angluin条件的几何细化）；2）提出对比闭包维数，紧致刻画均匀对比生成样本复杂度；3）证明对比生成与文本识别形成严格层次且互不可比。有限对抗腐败下：存在类在任意有限腐败预算下可由单一算法对比识别，但即使在仅一次腐败下也无法从正例识别，形成鲜明反转。

Conclusion: 对比式极限学习不仅理论可行，而且在有限腐败下比传统正例学习更鲁棒；公共交叉图为分析成对歧义、生成障碍与腐败缺陷提供了统一视角，为关系型监督信号的学习理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [177] [Playing the network backward: A Game Theoretic Attribution Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06212)
*Jakob Paul Zimmermann,Jim Berend,Georg Loho,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种博弈论框架，将梯度与α-β-LRP族等反向归因方法统一为扩展网络图上的双人博弈轨迹积分，并基于此推导出新的归因方法，在ViT-B/16上显著优于现有Transformer专用反向方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反向归因方法（包括梯度、LRP及Transformer专用规则）缺乏统一的比较框架，导致无法系统性地理解其底层计算逻辑及设计更好的归因方法。

Method: 将反向归因重构为扩展网络图上的双人博弈，基于Gaubert和Vlassopoulos的ReLU Net Game；梯度与α-β-LRP族在特定均衡下表现为博弈轨迹的积分，归因图因此成为轨迹分布的投影。通过博弈论概念（策略正则化、风险规避、扩展行动集）直接导出新的反向规则变体。

Result: 在ViT-B/16模型上，所选择的α-β-LRP改编版本在所有考虑的定位指标上均优于先前的Transformer专用反向归因方法。

Conclusion: 该博弈论框架不仅统一了多种反向归因方法的理论基础，还提供了通过博弈论概念系统性设计具有特定属性（如局部化聚焦、噪声鲁棒性、稳定注意力路由）的新归因方法的途径。

Abstract: Attribution methods explain which input features drive a model's prediction, making them central to model debugging and mechanistic interpretability. Yet backward attribution methods, including gradients, LRP, and transformer-specific rules, lack a shared framework in which to compare the underlying backward calculations. We introduce such a framework by recasting backward attribution as a two-player game on an extended network graph, building on Gaubert and Vlassopoulos' ReLU Net Game. Gradients and the full alpha-beta-LRP family arise as integrals over game trajectories under specific equilibria, so attribution maps become projections of trajectory distributions rather than the primary object. Desired explanation properties, such as localisation focus, robustness to input noise, or stable attention routing, can be specified as game-theoretic concepts, including policy regularization, risk aversion, and extended action sets, and translate directly into novel adaptations of the well-known backward rules. On ViT-B/16, one such selected adaptation of alpha-beta-LRP outperforms prior transformer-specific backward methods across all considered localisation metrics.

</details>


### [178] [AffineLens: Capturing the Continuous Piecewise Affine Functions of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06218)
*Yi Wei,Xuan Qi,Furao shen,Jian Zhao,Vittorio Murino,Cigdem Beyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AffineLens框架，能够直接计算和可视化分段仿射神经网络（PANNs）的仿射区域划分，克服了现有方法依赖间接代理的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于PANNs可解释性和表达性的分析多依赖间接代理（如激活统计或理论上界），缺乏在实际架构和有界输入域下枚举和可视化区域划分的实用工具。

Method: 提出AffineLens框架：给定有界输入多面体，识别与输入域相交的神经元诱导超平面，逐层枚举仿射子区域，返回可证明非空的最大连续分段仿射（CPA）区域及其内部代表点；利用CPA网络在固定激活模式下的仿射限制性质，支持批归一化、池化、残差连接、MLP和卷积层等现代组件。

Result: AffineLens提供了区域划分和决策边界的可视化功能，支持定性检查和定量区域计数；能够系统性地比较不同网络架构的区域复杂度指标。

Conclusion: 通过AffineLens进行的实证研究揭示了架构设计选择如何影响学习函数的几何结构，为理解神经网络表达性提供了新的几何视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Piecewise affine neural networks (PANNs) provide a principled geometric perspective on neural network expressivity by characterizing the input--output map as a continuous piecewise affine (CPA) function whose complexity is governed by the number, arrangement, and shapes of its affine regions. However, existing interpretability and expressivity analyses often rely on indirect proxies (e.g., activation statistics or theoretical upper bounds) and rarely offer practical, accurate tools for enumerating and visualizing the induced region partition under realistic architectures and bounded input domains. In this work, we present AffineLens, a unified framework for computing the hyperplane arrangements and polyhedral structures underlying PANNs.
