Method: 作者建立了一个理论框架，分析了两类神经GED估计器（图相似性预测器和基于对齐的方法）中编码器几何性质的作用。理论证明：在固定图集合上，图级别双Lipschitz编码器能产生可控的GED代理并提升排序稳定性；对于匹配型估计器，节点级别双Lipschitz几何性质能传播到编码器诱导的对齐成本中。基于此，作者采用FSW-GNN（一种双Lipschitz WL等价编码器）作为代表性架构的即插即用替代品。

Result: 在代表性基线和基准数据集上，这种几何感知的变体在GED预测和排序指标上均有显著提升。未训练编码器的忠实性案例研究、消融实验和迁移实验均支持这一观点：性能增益源于改进的表示几何性质。

Conclusion: 编码器几何性质应作为神经图匹配设计的重要原则。通过采用双Lipschitz编码器，可以在不改变整体架构的前提下，有效提升GED估计的准确性和稳定性。

Abstract: Graph Edit Distance (GED) is a fundamental, albeit NP-hard, metric for structural graph similarity. Recent neural graph matching architectures approximate GED by first encoding graphs with a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and then applying either a graph-level regression head or a matching-based alignment module. Despite substantial architectural progress, the role of encoder geometry in neural GED estimation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that connects encoder geometry to GED estimation quality for two broad classes of neural GED estimators: graph similarity predictors and alignment-based methods. On fixed graph collections, where the doubly-stochastic metric $d_{\mathrm{DS}}$ is comparable to GED, we show that graph-level bi-Lipschitz encoders yield controlled GED surrogates and improved ranking stability; for matching-based estimators, node-level bi-Lipschitz geometry propagates to encoder-induced alignment costs and the resulting optimized alignment objective. We instantiate this perspective using FSW-GNN, a bi-Lipschitz WL-equivalent encoder, as a drop-in replacement in representative neural GED architectures. Across representative baselines and benchmark datasets, the resulting geometry-aware variants significantly improve GED prediction and ranking metrics. A faithfulness case study of untrained encoders, together with ablations and transfer experiments, supports the view that these gains arise from improved representation geometry, positioning encoder geometry as a useful design principle for neural graph matching.

</details>


### [252] [BRICKS: Compositional Neural Markov Kernels for Zero-Shot Radiation-Matter Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06591)
*Richard Hildebrandt,Evangelos Kourlitis,Baran Hashemi,Manuel Bünstorf,Thierry Meyer,Nikola Boskov,Michael Kagan,Dan Rosenbaum,Sanmay Ganguly,Lukas Heinrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合离散-连续变换器和黎曼流匹配的组合式神经代理模型，用于模拟粒子辐射与物质相互作用。该模型支持零样本泛化到未见的大规模材料分布，并在GPU上实现了显著的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射-物质相互作用模拟是粒子物理、核工程、空间工程和医学物理等领域的关键任务。传统的机械模拟器通常计算成本高、不可微分且难以提供可处理的似然，限制了其在下游应用中的灵活性和效率。因此，急需一种既能保持物理准确性，又具备可微分性和高效计算能力的代理模型。

Method: 利用粒子相互作用的局部性和马尔可夫性质，构建“下一粒子预测”内核。采用基于乘积流形上黎曼流匹配的混合离散-连续变换器模型，能够生成变长的、带有类型的粒子集合及辐射副作用。该模型设计为可微分，并提供可处理的似然，从而支持下游应用。

Result: 模型在内核层级进行了评估，展示了在多轮自回归推演中的预测稳定性。相比基于CPU的传统机械模拟，该模型在GPU上执行单内核时有显著的计算加速。同时，发布了包含2000万事件的辐射-物质相互作用数据集以供研究。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为辐射-物质相互作用提供了一种高效、可微分且具有组合能力的神经代理，能够在零样本情况下泛化到未见的大规模材料分布，具备良好的预测稳定性和计算效率。发布的大规模数据集有助于推动该方向的进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce a new strategy for compositional neural surrogates for radiation-matter interactions, a key task spanning domains from particle physics through nuclear and space engineering to medical physics. Exploiting the locality and the Markov nature of particle interactions, we create a \emph{next-particle prediction} kernel using hybrid discrete-continuous transformer models based on Riemannian Flow Matching on product manifolds. The model generates variable-sized typed sets of particles and radiation side effects that are the result of the interaction of an incident particle with a material volume. The resulting kernel can be composed to simulate unseen large-scale material distributions in a zero-shot manner. Unlike mechanistic simulators, our model is designed to be differentiable, provides tractable likelihoods for future downstream applications. A significant computational speed-up on GPU compared to CPU-bound mechanistic simulation is observed for single-kernel execution. We evaluate the model at the kernel level and demonstrate predictive stability over multi-round autoregressive rollouts. We additionally release a novel 20M-event radiation-matter interaction dataset for further research.

</details>


### [253] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从功能分析角度严格刻画了带L2正则化的Transformer目标函数，证明了其满足Villani准则，推导出了显式的log-Sobolev和Poincaré常数，为权值衰减提供了理论基础，并在GPT-Neo-125M上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权值衰减在大语言模型中被广泛用作正则化方法，但其对Transformer损失地景的精确作用机制在理论上仍未被充分探索，缺乏严格的理论分析。

Method: 通过证明带L2正则化的交叉熵损失函数满足Villani强制能量函数准则（无限可微、至少二次增长、高斯可积尾部、满足微分增长条件），推导显式的log-Sobolev和Poincaré常数，并引入可扩展的Villani诊断函数Ψ_s，使用Hutchinson迹探测进行高效估计。

Result: 证明了正则化损失F满足Villani准则，推导出log-Sobolev和Poincaré常数C_LS ≤ λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}，建立了正则化强度λ和模型维度d与有限时间收敛保证的联系。在GPT-Neo-125M上的实验验证了Ψ_s的二次增长预测、Hessian谱膨胀现象以及与log-Sobolev分析一致的指数收敛行为。

Conclusion: 权值衰减不仅从经验上改善泛化性能，还建立了深度学习中快速Langevin混合和理论基础的曲率感知优化所需的数学条件，为大语言模型的正则化提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [254] [How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06605)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DAPRO框架，首次实现了多轮LLM交互中事件发生时间的动态预算分配，在有限计算资源下提供了更紧的理论保证和更准确的风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估多轮对话中大语言模型的关键事件（如越狱攻击或智能体任务成功）计算成本极高，这些稀有事件往往在多次交互后才出现，现有共形生存框架采用静态预算分配效率低下，需要动态分配方案来优化计算资源使用。

Method: 提出动态分配投影优化方法（DAPRO），这是首个用于多轮LLM交互事件时间界限的理论有效动态预算分配框架。核心创新包括：1）证明满足预算约束且提供分布无关的有限样本覆盖保证，无需先前方法所需的审查时间与事件时间条件独立性假设；2）提出新的覆盖界限，按平均审查权重的平方根而非最坏情况权重缩放，从而获得更紧的理论保证。

Result: 在Llama 3.1和Qwen 2.5等LLM上，针对智能体任务成功、对抗性越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉等场景进行综合实验，DAPRO在满足预算约束的同时，相比静态基线方法，一致实现了更接近名义水平的覆盖率和更低的方差，并能获得群体级评估指标（如越狱率）的无偏、低方差估计。

Conclusion: DAPRO成功解决了多轮LLM交互中关键事件评估的计算效率问题，在理论上提供了更紧的覆盖保证，在实践中实现了更准确的性能预测，为有限计算资源下的LLM风险评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved.
  Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions.
  We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches.
  A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources.
  Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.

</details>


### [255] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上揭示了Softmax注意力机制Transformer如何实现上下文学习：通过构造一个能执行上下文逻辑回归的多层Transformer，证明其每一层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降，并展示了该模型可通过训练单层自注意力层并循环应用来获得，同时提供了训练收敛和分布外泛化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer强大的上下文学习（ICL）能力普遍被认为源于其能在上下文中隐式执行某些算法。然而，关于Softmax注意力机制的Transformer具体如何实现ICL的理论理解尚不充分，本文旨在深入探究这一问题。

Method: 1. 构造一类多层Transformer，使其能在上下文中执行逻辑回归，证明每一层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降。
2. 展示该构造可通过以下方式获得：监督训练单层自注意力层以执行一步梯度下降，随后循环应用该训练层得到循环模型。
3. 对单层自注意力层提供训练收敛保证，对循环模型提供分布外泛化保证。

Result: 成功构造了能精确执行上下文逻辑回归的多层Transformer模型，证明了其与归一化梯度下降的等价性；提供了单层训练的收敛性证明和循环模型的分布外泛化理论保证，从理论上阐明了Softmax Transformer如何作为有效的上下文学习器。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了对ICL机制的理论理解，通过具体构造和理论分析，清晰地展示了Softmax Transformer能够有效地模拟梯度下降算法，从而实现上下文学习，为理解Transformer的ICL能力提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [256] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SoftSAE，一种具有动态Top-K选择机制的稀疏自编码器，能够根据输入复杂度自适应调整激活特征数量，解决了传统固定稀疏度方法在处理不同复杂度数据时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TopK SAEs采用固定的稀疏度水平，对所有输入强制使用相同数量的激活特征（K），忽略了现实世界数据复杂度的变化。自然数据通常位于具有不同局部内在维度的流形上，简单输入可能只需少量特征，而复杂输入需要更多特征。固定的K值会在简单情况下引入噪声，或在复杂情况下遗漏重要结构。

Method: 提出SoftSAE，采用可微分的Soft Top-K算子来学习依赖于输入的稀疏度水平k。该动态Top-K选择机制使模型能够根据每个输入的复杂度调整激活特征的数量，从而使表示更好地匹配数据结构，解释长度反映输入中的信息量。

Result: 实验结果证实，SoftSAE不仅能够发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择正确数量的特征，验证了动态稀疏度调整的有效性。

Conclusion: SoftSAE通过动态调整激活特征数量，成功解决了固定稀疏度方法在处理不同复杂度数据时的不足，为机械可解释性研究提供了更灵活和准确的工具。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [257] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从机理上揭示了LLM中“注意力汇”现象的成因：自注意力中的数值聚合过程引发方差差异，并被FFN层中超级神经元的激活大幅放大，从而迫使模型将注意力汇作为结构锚点。基于此，作者提出了头级RMSNorm架构改进，通过恢复位置间的统计均衡显著加速了训练收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管“注意力汇”现象在大型语言模型中普遍存在——即初始token不成比例地占据了注意力分数，但其结构性起源尚不明确。本文旨在为这一现象提供一个机理层面的解释。

Method: 首先，追溯该现象的根源至自注意力固有的数值聚合过程，该过程会引发系统性的方差差异。进一步证明，前馈网络（FFN）层中超级神经元的激活会急剧放大这一差异。具体而言，通道稀疏的下投影触发了首token表示的维度差异，从而有必要形成注意力汇作为结构锚点。随后，通过两组受控干预验证此因果链：（i）通过修改注意力掩码隔离聚合效应；（ii）放大目标token表示的方差。这两项干预均可在任意位置复现注意力汇。

Result: 提出的机理理解系统控制汇的形成奠定了基础。作为概念验证，提出了头级RMSNorm这一架构修改，在预训练中稳定数值聚合输出。实验表明，恢复跨位置的统计均衡显著加速了收敛。

Conclusion: 本文成功揭示了注意力汇现象的结构性成因，并通过实验验证了其因果机制。提出的头级RMSNorm架构修改证明了该理论的实际应用价值，为未来系统性地控制和优化注意力汇的形成提供了理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [258] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为BRPC的在线贝叶斯校准框架，用于解决流式数据下模型失配和非平稳性（含渐变与突变）的问题。该方法分离了校准参数和差异函数的更新以保持可辨识性，并结合重启机制处理突变，在合成数据与工厂仿真基准测试中表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯校准存在参数与差异函数混杂问题，且通常为静态数据的离线过程，难以适应现代数字孪生应用中系统随时间演变（渐进漂移和突变）的场景。现有数据同化方法虽支持序贯更新，但未显式建模系统偏差，在突变情况下效果有限。

Method: 提出贝叶斯递归投影校准（BRPC），将投影校准扩展至在线场景。核心在于分离校准参数的无差异粒子更新与差异函数的条件高斯过程更新，以保持可辨识性并适应渐进演变；引入重启机制以检测和处理突变，实现偏差感知的自适应校准。

Result: 理论分析证明了BRPC在渐进演变下的跟踪性能以及重启机制在误报率和检测行为方面的保证。在合成数据和工厂仿真基准测试中，BRPC在渐进变化下校准精度更高；结合重启机制的BRPC在突变情况下显著提升了鲁棒性和预测性能。

Conclusion: BRPC为非平稳环境下的数字孪生与计算机实验提供了一种有效的在线贝叶斯校准解决方案，成功解决了传统方法参数混杂与离线限制的问题，通过理论保证与实证研究验证了其在渐进漂移和突变场景下的优越性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [259] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入ℓ1范数平稳性、ℓ∞平滑性和可分离噪声模型，证明了基于符号的优化算法（SignSGD和Muon）在稀疏噪声环境下相比SGD能将复杂度降低d倍（d为问题维度），并在GPT-2预训练中验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SignSGD和Muon等基于符号的优化算法在训练大型基础模型时表现出色，但在标准平滑性和有限方差条件下，SGD已被证明在寻找ℓ2范数平稳点方面具有极小极大最优性，这从根本上排除了符号方法在标准设置中获得复杂度优势的可能性。因此需要建立新的理论框架来理解符号方法的优势条件。

Method: 提出基于ℓ1范数平稳性、ℓ∞平滑性和可分离噪声模型的理论框架，该框架能更好地捕捉符号更新的坐标特性。在此框架下推导SignSGD的匹配上界和下界，并将框架扩展到矩阵域以分析Muon优化器。通过比较SignSGD的上界与SGD的下界来量化性能差异。

Result: 在稀疏噪声环境下，SignSGD相比SGD能有效将复杂度降低d倍（d为问题维度）。Muon优化器在矩阵域中保持了这种最优的维度扩展性。在124M参数GPT-2模型预训练实验中，SignSGD的更快收敛速度与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 通过改变问题几何（从ℓ2到ℓ1范数，从标准平滑性到ℓ∞平滑性）和噪声模型，成功建立了基于符号优化算法的理论优势。证明了SignSGD和Muon在特定问题类别（稀疏噪声环境）中确实优于SGD，并通过实验验证了理论预测的准确性，为实践中观察到的符号优化算法优越性能提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [260] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical GANs for the Generation of Adversarial Network Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06629)
*Prateek Paudel,Nitin Jha,Abhishek Parakh,Mahadevan Subramaniam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合量子-经典生成对抗网络（QC-GAN）框架，利用变分量子生成器生成能够绕过入侵检测系统（IDS）的对抗性网络流量，证明了量子机器学习在网络攻击和IDS压力测试中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典生成对抗网络（GANs）在生成对抗性网络流量攻击入侵检测系统时存在诸多缺陷：需要大量高维数据集、模式崩溃问题以及高计算开销。为解决这些问题，本文探索利用量子计算的优势来改进生成过程。

Method: 提出混合量子-经典GAN（QC-GAN）框架，采用变分量子生成器替代传统生成器。将潜在向量编码为量子态而非经典噪声向量，以获得更具表达力的潜在表示并降低计算开销。经典判别器在真实数据集（UNSW-NB15）和QC-GAN生成的虚假网络流上进行训练。攻击模型假设攻击者为具有有限量子计算能力的国家行为体，而判别器对大多数终端用户和组织保持经典形式。

Result: 使用经典入侵检测系统模型（随机森林分类器和基于卷积神经网络的分类器）测试生成的流量流，评估其绕过检测过程的能力。进一步评估了基于硬件的噪声如何影响这些攻击，为IDS提供了新的视角。

Conclusion: 本工作突出了量子机器学习作为生成高级攻击流和压力测试经典IDS的可能性，强调了构建量子弹性防御系统的必要性，为未来网络安全防御提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Classical generative adversarial networks (GANs) have been applied to generate adversarial network traffic capable of attacking intrusion detection systems, but they suffer from shortcomings such as the need for large amounts of high-dimensional datasets, mode collapse, and high computational overhead. In this work, we propose a hybrid quantum-classical GAN (QC-GAN) framework where a variational quantum generator is used to generate synthetic network traffic flows mimicking malicious traffic using latent representations. Instead of sampling classical noise vectors, we encode the latent vector (the hidden features) as a quantum state, which is the basis for claiming more expressive latent representations and reducing computational overhead. A classical discriminator will be trained on real-world datasets (UNSW-NB15) and the proposed QC-GAN-generated fake network flows. In this configuration, the generator aims to minimize the discriminator's ability to distinguish real from fake traffic, while the discriminator aims to maximize its classification accuracy, in an iterative manner. In our attack model, we assume that the attacker is a state actor with access to limited quantum computing power, whereas the discriminator is chosen to be classical, as will likely be the case for most end users and organizations. We test the generated flows using classical intrusion detection system (IDS) models, such as a random forest classifier and a convolutional neural network-based classifier, for their ability to bypass the detection process. This work aims to highlight the possibilities of quantum machine learning as a means of generating advanced attack flows and stress testing classical IDS. Lastly, we further evaluate how hardware-based noise affects these attacks to offer a new perspective on IDS, highlighting the need for a quantum resilient defense system.

</details>


### [261] [Crafting Reversible SFT Behaviors in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06632)
*Yuping Lin,Pengfei He,Yue Xing,Yingqian Cui,Jiayuan Ding,Subhabrata Mukherjee,Hui Liu,Zhen Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LCDD方法将SFT诱导的行为压缩到稀疏的“载体”子网络中，并配合SFT-Eraser提示实现推理时可逆的行为控制，证明结构约束是实现因果必要性与选择性抑制的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督微调（SFT）会在大模型中引入新行为，但缺乏对这些行为分布的结构约束；现有的事后归因方法仅识别相关子网络，无法保证因果必要性，限制了推理时对这些行为的选择性控制。

Method: 提出两种方法：(1) 损失约束双重下降（LCDD），在效用预算约束下联合优化路由掩码和模型权重，将SFT行为压缩到稀疏的机制必要子网络（载体）中；(2) SFT-Eraser，通过对提取的载体通道进行激活匹配优化软提示，用于逆转SFT诱导的行为。

Result: 在多个模型族的安全、固定响应和风格行为任务上，LCDD生成的稀疏载体能够保持目标行为，同时由SFT-Eraser触发时实现强逆转；消融实验表明相同触发优化在标准SFT模型上失败，证实稀疏结构而非触发器设计是实现逆转的关键前置条件。

Conclusion: 稀疏载体对SFT诱导行为具有因果必要性，为部署模型中系统性定位和选择性抑制SFT诱导行为提供了新方向。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) induces new behaviors in large language models, yet imposes no structural constraint on how these behaviors are distributed within the model. Existing behavior interpretation methods, such as circuit attribution approaches, identify sparse subnetworks correlated with SFT-induced behaviors post-hoc. However, such correlations do not imply *causal necessity*, limiting the ability to selectively control SFT-induced behaviors at inference time. We pursue an alternative by asking: can an SFT-induced behavior be deliberately compressed into a sparse, mechanistically necessary subnetwork, termed a *carrier*, while remaining controllable at inference time without weight modification? We propose (a) **Loss-Constrained Dual Descent (LCDD)**, which constructs such carriers by jointly optimizing routing masks and model weights under an explicit utility budget, and (b) **SFT-Eraser**, a soft prompt optimized via activation matching on extracted carrier channels, to reverse the SFT-induced behavior. Across safety, fixed-response, and style behaviors on multiple model families, LCDD yields sparse carriers that preserve target behaviors while enabling strong reversion when triggered by SFT-Eraser. Ablations further establish that the sparse structure is the key precondition for reversal: the same trigger optimization fails on standard SFT models, confirming that structure rather than trigger design is the operative factor. These results provide direct evidence that the learned carriers are causally necessary for the behaviors, pointing to a new direction for systematically localizing and selectively suppressing SFT-induced behaviors in deployed models.

</details>


### [262] [Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06640)
*Ronaldo Canizales,Divya Gopinath,Corina Păsăreanu,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于概念的溯因和对比解释方法，能够捕捉因果相关的最小高层概念集合，并通过概念擦除程序建立因果关系，实现对单个图像和图像集合的模型预测解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于概念的解释方法要么无法建立概念与模型预测之间的因果关系，要么表达能力有限，只能推断涉及单个概念的因果解释；而形式化溯因和对比解释工作虽然能计算因果相关的最小输入特征集，但仅考虑像素等低层特征。这两个研究方向存在互补性，需要融合两者的优势。

Method: 提出基于概念的溯因和对比解释概念，捕捉因果相关的最小高层概念集合；设计一系列算法枚举所有最小解释，并利用概念擦除程序建立因果关系；通过适当聚合这些解释，实现对单个图像和具有用户指定共同行为的图像集合的模型预测理解。

Result: 在多个模型、数据集和行为上进行评估，证明了该方法在计算有帮助且用户友好的解释方面的有效性，能够理解模型在单个图像和图像集合上的预测行为。

Conclusion: 成功将基于概念的解释与形式化因果解释相结合，提出了一种新的解释方法，既具有高层概念的可理解性，又具备因果关系的严谨性，为深度神经网络的可解释性研究提供了新的思路和工具。

Abstract: *Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.

</details>


### [263] [Edge-specific signal propagation on mature chromophore-region 3D mechanism graphs for fluorescent protein quantum-yield prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06644)
*Yuchen Xiong,Swee Keong Yeap,Steven Aw Yoong Kit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于发色团中心机制图的荧光蛋白量子产额预测方法，在531个蛋白基准测试中优于现有模型基线，并能解释不同波段蛋白的亮度机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光蛋白量子产额由成熟发色团及其三维微环境决定，而现有蛋白语言模型和发射带平均值仅能捕捉全局趋势，无法建模局部物理信号如何作用于特定发色团区域。

Method: 将PDB结构转化为带类型的3D残基图，注册到成熟CRO状态，划分为酚盐、桥连和咪唑啉酮区域，通过通道-信号-区域传播进行变换，生成121个富集特征，使用52个非身份特征进行波段特定的ExtraTrees回归。

Result: 在531个蛋白基准测试中，随机交叉验证性能优于模型基线（R=0.772±0.008, MAE=0.131±0.002），超过Band均值（R=0.632）、ESM-C（R=0.734）和SaProt（R=0.731）；在同源性控制下优势明显，在远程bucket（<50%相似性）中R=0.697。筛选出的特征恢复了波段特定机制：GFP类蛋白的芳香堆积和钳夹不对称、红蛋白的电荷/钳夹平衡、远红蛋白的柔性风险/大体积接触特征。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅实现了准确的量子产额预测，还提供了内在可解释性，揭示了不同波段荧光蛋白的亮度决定机制，为荧光蛋白工程提供了新工具。

Abstract: Fluorescent protein quantum yield (QY) is governed by the mature chromophore and its three-dimensional microenvironment rather than sequence identity alone. Protein language models and emission-band averages capture global trends, but do not model how local physical signals act on specific chromophore regions.
  We present a chromophore-centred mechanism graph algorithm for QY prediction. Each PDB structure is converted into a typed 3D residue graph, registered to a mature-CRO state, partitioned into phenolate, bridge and imidazolinone regions, and transformed by channel-signal-region propagation. The representation contains 121 enrichment features; after removing identity shortcuts, 52 non-identity features are used for band-specific ExtraTrees regression. Because each feature encodes a contact channel, seed signal and target CRO region, interpretation is intrinsic rather than post hoc. On a 531-protein benchmark, the method achieved the best random-CV performance among model-based baselines (R = 0.772 +/- 0.008, MAE = 0.131 +/- 0.002), exceeding Band mean (R = 0.632), ESM-C (R = 0.734) and SaProt (R = 0.731), and ranked first in bright screening (Bright P@5 = 0.704). Under homology control, the advantage was clearest in the remote bucket (<50% similarity; R = 0.697 versus 0.633, 0.575 and 0.408), with the strongest overall bright/dark Top-K screening. Stable selected features recovered band-specific mechanisms: aromatic packing and clamp asymmetry in GFP-like proteins, charge/clamp balance in Red proteins, and flexibility-risk/bulky-contact features in Far-red proteins.
  Source code, feature tables and evaluation scripts are available from the first author upon request. Contact: yuchenak05@gmail.com

</details>


### [264] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将仅适用于二分类的Venn-Abers预测器推广至无界回归场景，并结合共形预测方法，验证了其在较大训练集上能提升预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers预测器虽具有良好的有效性性质，但其主要局限在于仅适用于二分类任务（近期虽有针对有界回归的扩展），尚未解决无界回归这一更普遍场景的应用问题。

Method: 提出了一种将Venn-Abers预测器推广至无界回归情形的方法，该过程需要引入共形预测（conformal prediction）的元素来实现扩展。

Result: 通过模拟研究和实证分析，考察了从Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器的预测效率。研究结果表明，对于较大的训练集，该方法在一定程度上提升了标准回归器的预测效率。

Conclusion: 成功实现了Venn-Abers预测器在无界回归任务中的泛化，为在更广泛的回归场景中利用该预测器的有效性优势提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [265] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种无基准比较的安全评分方法，用于在缺乏标注数据的情况下比较语言模型的安全性，并通过SimpleAudit工具在挪威安全包上验证了方法的有效性，最后在挪威公共部门采购案例中展示了实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多部署场景需要在缺乏相关语言、行业或监管体系的标注基准数据时比较候选语言模型的安全性，现有方法无法满足这一需求。

Method: 将问题形式化为无基准比较安全评分，建立工具效度链（包括对安全vs消融对比的响应性、目标驱动方差的主导性、跨运行稳定性），并实现SimpleAudit本地优先评分工具进行验证。

Result: 在挪威安全包上验证：安全与消融目标的AUROC值在0.89-1.00之间分离，目标身份是主要方差成分(η²≈0.52)，严重性配置在10次运行后稳定。应用于Petri工具同样有效。挪威公共部门采购案例显示更安全的模型取决于场景类别和风险度量。

Conclusion: 评分必须与匹配增量、临界率、不确定性以及使用的审计员和评判器一起报告，而不能简单地折叠成单一排名。关键差异源于上游的声明-契约执行和部署适配。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [266] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在SFT阶段使用与预训练相同的优化器能实现更优的学习-遗忘权衡，表现优于其他优化器和LoRA；Muon在推理任务微调中表现不佳，可能源于其过度依赖死记硬背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化器在LLM预训练和微调中发挥关键作用，但优化器选择对微调性能和知识保留的影响尚未得到充分研究。作者观察到使用相同优化器进行全量微调能实现更好的学习-遗忘权衡，由此提出“优化器-模型一致性”现象，并深入探究其内在机制。

Method: 通过受控实验和理论分析，研究优化器对模型的影响机制：1）分析优化器如何通过激活正则化效应塑造模型，形成预训练检查点周围不同的优化景观；2）理论推导在SFT中权重更新应遵循何种结构以降低预训练知识的遗忘；3）对比Muon和AdamW在预训练和SFT全流程中的表现，并通过合成语言建模实验深入分析Muon在推理任务微调中表现不佳的原因。

Result: 发现优化器-模型一致性现象：使用相同优化器进行全量微调能获得更好的学习-遗忘权衡，遗忘更少且性能等同或优于其他优化器和LoRA。Muon在推理任务微调中表现较差，合成实验表明这源于Muon强烈的死记硬背倾向，在小数据量（如SFT）场景下会损害模式获取能力。

Conclusion: 优化器选择对SFT性能和知识保留具有重要影响。使用与预训练相同的优化器进行微调能保持优化器塑造的模型结构和知识表示，从而降低遗忘。Muon因死记硬背倾向不适合推理任务的SFT微调。研究为优化器选择提供了理论和实证依据，强调了优化器-模型一致性的重要性。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [267] [Why Global LLM Leaderboards Are Misleading: Small Portfolios for Heterogeneous Supervised ML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06656)
*Jai Moondra,Ayela Chughtai,Bhargavi Lanka,Swati Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 全球大语言模型（LLM）排行榜存在严重误导性：研究发现基于全局排名的方法掩盖了用户偏好的显著异质性。通过引入(λ,ν)-投资组合框架，仅需5个不同的排名即可覆盖96%的投票，而全局排名仅覆盖21%。语言是造成这种异质性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于成对人类反馈的LLM排行榜（如Arena）采用全局Bradley-Terry（BT）排名方法。然而，这种方法假设用户偏好是同质的，忽略了不同语言、任务和时间段可能存在的偏好差异。研究旨在揭示全局排名的局限性，并提出能够处理异质性的新框架。

Method: 1. 分析Arena平台约89K条跨116种语言、涉及52个LLM的比较数据；2. 评估全局BT排名的统计显著性；3. 分析异质性来源（语言、任务、时间）；4. 提出(λ,ν)-投资组合框架，将其建模为集合覆盖问题的变体；5. 利用底层集合系统的VC维提供理论保证；6. 在Arena数据和COMPAS数据集上验证方法。

Result: 1. 全局BT排名具有误导性：近2/3的决定性选票相互抵消，前50名模型的成对获胜概率最高仅0.53（统计上无法区分）；2. 语言是关键异质性来源：按语言分组后，ELO分数的离散度提高两个数量级；3. 算法恢复出5个BT排名即可覆盖96%的投票（λ较小），而全局排名仅覆盖21%；4. 6个LLM的投资组合覆盖的投票数是全局排名前6名的两倍；5. 在COMPAS数据集上成功检测数据盲点。

Conclusion: 看似全局噪声的数据实际上是多个一致但相互冲突的子群体的混合。全局排名掩盖了这种异质性，导致排名不可靠。提出的(λ,ν)-投资组合框架能有效识别并覆盖不同的偏好群体，为LLM评估提供了更准确、更有意义的方法，该方法也可应用于公平性问题的检测。

Abstract: Ranking LLMs via pairwise human feedback underpins current leaderboards for open-ended tasks, such as creative writing and problem-solving. We analyze ~89K comparisons in 116 languages from 52 LLMs from Arena, and show that the best-fit global Bradley-Terry (BT) ranking is misleading. Nearly 2/3 of the decisive votes cancel out, and even the top 50 models according to the global BT ranking are statistically indistinguishable (pairwise win probabilities are at most 0.53 within the top 50 models). We trace this failure to strong, structured heterogeneity of opinions across language, task, and time. Moreover, we find an important characteristic - *language* plays a key role. Grouping by language (and families) increases the agreement of votes massively, resulting in two orders of magnitude higher spread in the ELO scores (i.e., very consistent rankings). What appears as global noise is in fact a mixture of coherent but conflicting subpopulations.
  To address such heterogeneity in supervised machine learning, we introduce the framework of $(λ, ν)$-portfolios, which are small sets of models that achieve a prediction error at most $λ$, "covering" at least a $ν$ fraction of users. We formulate this as a variant of the set cover problem and provide guarantees using the VC dimension of the underlying set system. On the Arena data, our algorithms recover just 5 distinct BT rankings that cover over 96% of votes at a modest $λ$, compared to the 21% coverage by the global ranking. We also provide a portfolio of 6 LLMs that cover twice as many votes as the top-6 LLMs from a global ranking. We further construct portfolios for a classification problem on the COMPAS dataset using an ensemble of fairness-regularized classification models and show that these portfolios can be used to detect blind spots in the data, which might be of independent interest to policymakers.

</details>


### [268] [Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06660)
*Yuhang Lai,Jiazhan Feng,Yee Whye Teh,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了VHG框架，通过在传统的出题者-求解者对弈中引入独立的验证者，有效解决了大模型生成高质量数学问题时的无效性和奖励黑客问题，在数学任务上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在生成有效、高难度且新颖的科学数学问题方面存在不足，而这对模型训练和自主科学研究至关重要；现有方法依赖昂贵的人工专家或存在奖励黑客导致生成无效问题的缺陷。

Method: 提出VHG（Verifier-Enhanced Hard Problem Generation）框架，在传统setter-solver二元对弈基础上引入第三方独立验证者，将出题者的奖励设计为问题有效性（由验证者评估）和问题难度（由求解者评估）的联合函数。实现了两种验证者变体：硬符号验证者和软LLM验证者。

Result: 在不定积分任务和通用数学推理任务上的实验表明，VHG在各项指标上大幅优于所有基线方法，能够生成更有效且更具挑战性的问题。

Conclusion: 通过引入独立验证者构建三方自博弈机制，VHG框架有效平衡了问题的有效性和难度，为大模型训练数据生成和自主科学研究提供了可靠的问题生成解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.

</details>


### [269] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UniPool架构，通过共享专家池打破传统MoE每层独立专家集的刚性分配规则，在减少专家参数预算的同时提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代MoE架构采用每层独立专家集的刚性分配规则，导致深度扩展与专家参数线性增长耦合。作者发现深层路由器的随机替换仅导致1.0-1.6点精度下降，揭示了现有分配存在冗余。

Method: 提出UniPool架构，用单一共享专家池替代每层独立专家所有权，通过独立分层路由器访问。引入池级辅助损失实现全局专家利用平衡，采用NormRouter提供稀疏且尺度稳定的路由。

Result: 在五个LLaMA架构规模（182M-978M参数）上，UniPool持续降低验证损失（最多0.0386）。使用仅41.6%-66.7%专家参数预算的UniPool变体匹配或超越基线MoE性能，证明专家参数可亚线性增长。

Conclusion: 共享池设计下专家参数无需随深度线性增长，可实现亚线性增长并保持更优效率和效果。UniPool优势可与细粒度专家分解技术组合使用。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了标注员策略模型，仅从标注行为中学习标注员的内部安全策略，无需额外询问即可揭示标注分歧的来源（操作失误、策略歧义或价值多元主义），并展示了其在发现策略歧义和价值差异方面的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全策略指导AI输出的标注与开发，但标注分歧普遍存在。分歧来源包括操作失误、策略歧义和价值多元主义，区分这些来源至关重要（因对应措施不同），但直接询问标注员推理过程成本高、负担重且不可靠。

Method: 引入标注员策略模型，这是一种可解释模型，仅利用标注行为数据学习标注员的内部安全策略，使标注员推理过程可视化且可比较，无需额外的标注工作。

Result: 验证表明APMs能准确建模标注员安全策略（准确率超80%），忠实地预测对反事实编辑的响应，并在受控环境中恢复已知策略差异。应用于LLM和人类标注时，成功揭示了策略歧义和不同群体间的安全优先级系统差异。

Conclusion: APMs支持更精准、透明和包容的安全策略设计，能够通过揭示标注员对安全指令的不同理解和群体间的价值差异，帮助制定针对性的改进措施。

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [271] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ZAYA1-8B，一个专注于推理的混合专家模型，仅用不到10亿活跃参数即在数学和编程基准测试中达到或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528的性能；结合提出的Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，在AIME'25和HMMT'25上分别达到91.9%和89.6%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个高效的小规模推理模型，证明在少量活跃参数下通过优化的训练策略和测试时计算方法，可以实现与大模型相媲美的推理能力，同时展示在AMD全栈计算平台上进行端到端模型训练的可行性。

Method: 基于Zyphra的MoE++架构从头训练；采用保留答案的裁剪方案从预训练开始注入推理数据；后训练实施四阶段强化学习级联（推理预热、400任务RLVE-Gym课程、数学与代码RL、行为RL）；引入Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，递归聚合并行推理轨迹并仅传递有限长度的推理尾部。

Result: ZAYA1-8B（700M活跃参数/8B总参数）在多个数学和编程基准测试中匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528，与更大规模的开源推理模型保持竞争力；应用Markovian RSA后，在AIME'25达到91.9%、在HMMT'25达到89.6%，缩小了与Gemini-2.5 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2和GPT-5-High等大型推理模型的差距。

Conclusion: 成功证明通过MoE架构、全流程推理数据融合、多阶段强化学习级联以及创新的测试时计算方法，可以在极低的活跃参数量下实现卓越的推理性能，为构建高效推理模型提供了新的技术路径，并验证了AMD全栈平台的训练能力。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-8B, a reasoning-focused mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 700M active and 8B total parameters, built on Zyphra's MoE++ architecture. ZAYA1-8B's core pretraining, midtraining, and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) were performed on a full-stack AMD compute, networking, and software platform. With under 1B active parameters, ZAYA1-8B matches or exceeds DeepSeek-R1-0528 on several challenging mathematics and coding benchmarks, and remains competitive with substantially larger open-weight reasoning models. ZAYA1-8B was trained from scratch for reasoning, with reasoning data included from pretraining onward using an answer-preserving trimming scheme. Post-training uses a four-stage RL cascade: reasoning warmup on math and puzzles; a 400-task RLVE-Gym curriculum; math and code RL with test-time compute traces and synthetic code environments built from competitive-programming references; and behavioral RL for chat and instruction following. We also introduce Markovian RSA, a test-time compute method that recursively aggregates parallel reasoning traces while carrying forward only bounded-length reasoning tails between rounds. In TTC evaluation, Markovian RSA raises ZAYA1-8B to 91.9\% on AIME'25 and 89.6\% on HMMT'25 while carrying forward only a 4K-token tail, narrowing the gap to much larger reasoning models including Gemini-2.5 Pro, DeepSeek-V3.2, and GPT-5-High.

</details>


### [272] [Partial Evidence Bench: Benchmarking Authorization-Limited Evidence in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05379)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Partial Evidence Bench，一个用于评估企业智能体在授权边界受限环境下“不安全完整性”故障模式的确定性基准，包含72个任务，涵盖尽职调查、合规审计和安全事件响应三类场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体在受限检索系统和策略约束环境中运行时，虽然访问控制可以正确执行，但系统可能产生看似完整的答案，而实质证据却位于调用者的授权边界之外。这一故障模式此前缺乏有效的测量方法。

Method: 设计了Partial Evidence Bench基准，包含三个场景家族（尽职调查、合规审计、安全事件响应），共72个任务，配备ACL分区的语料库、预言机完整答案、授权视图答案、完整性判断和结构化缺口报告预言机。系统从四个维度评估：答案正确性、完整性意识、缺口报告质量和不安全完整性行为。

Result: 基线测试表明，静默过滤在所有场景中都具有灾难性的不安全性，而显式失败并报告行为能够消除不安全完整性，同时不会使任务陷入简单的弃权。初步真实模型运行显示，系统在过度声明完整性、保守低估或以企业可用形式报告不完整性方面存在模型依赖和场景敏感的差异。

Conclusion: 该基准的核心贡献在于使治理关键的企业智能体故障模式能够在无需人工评判或易受污染的静态语料库的情况下变得可测量，为企业智能体的安全部署提供了重要的评估工具。

Abstract: Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside scoped retrieval systems, delegated workflows, and policy-constrained evidence environments. In these settings, access control can be enforced correctly while the system still produces an answer that appears complete even though material evidence lies outside the caller's authorization boundary. This paper introduces Partial Evidence Bench, a deterministic benchmark for measuring that failure mode. The benchmark ships three scenario families -- due diligence, compliance audit, and security incident response -- with 72 tasks total, ACL-partitioned corpora, oracle complete answers, oracle authorized-view answers, oracle completeness judgments, and structured gap-report oracles. It evaluates systems along four surfaces: answer correctness, completeness awareness, gap-report quality, and unsafe completeness behavior. Checked-in baselines show that silent filtering is catastrophically unsafe across all shipped families, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without collapsing the task into trivial abstention. Preliminary real-model runs show model-dependent and scenario-sensitive differences in whether systems overclaim completeness, conservatively underclaim, or report incompleteness in an enterprise-usable form. The benchmark's broader contribution is to make a governance-critical agent failure measurable without human judges or contamination-prone static corpora.

</details>


### [273] [BALAR : A Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05386)
*Aymen Echarghaoui,Dongxia Wu,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了BALAR，一种任务无关、无需微调的贝叶斯主动推理外循环算法，通过维护潜在状态的结构化信念并最大化期望互信息来选择澄清问题，在三个基准测试上准确率显著提升14.6%-38.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在交互式多轮对话场景中普遍采用被动反应式处理，缺乏推理缺失信息及决策下一步提问的原则性机制，导致在需要主动信息获取的任务中表现不佳。

Method: 提出BALAR（Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning）算法：1) 维护潜在状态的结构化信念表示；2) 通过最大化期望互信息选择最优澄清问题；3) 当当前状态表示不足时动态扩展状态空间。该方法为任务无关的外循环算法，无需模型微调即可实现结构化的多轮交互。

Result: 在三个多样化基准测试上显著优于所有基线方法：AR-Bench-DC（侦探案件）准确率提升14.6%，AR-Bench-SP（思维谜题）提升38.5%，iCraft-MD（临床诊断）提升30.5%。

Conclusion: BALAR成功实现了大语言模型的主动推理能力，证明了贝叶斯框架与信息论原则在构建结构化多轮交互系统中的有效性，为无需微调的任务无关主动对话系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate in interactive settings where solving a task requires multiple rounds of information exchange with a user. However, most current systems treat dialogue reactively and lack a principled mechanism to reason about what information is missing and which question should be asked next. We propose BALAR (Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning), a task-agnostic outer-loop algorithm that requires no fine-tuning and enables structured multi-turn interaction between an LLM agent and a user. BALAR maintains a structured belief over latent states, selects clarifying questions by maximizing expected mutual information, and dynamically expands its state representation when the current one proves insufficient. We evaluate BALAR on three diverse benchmarks: AR-Bench-DC (detective cases), AR-Bench-SP (thinking puzzles), and iCraft-MD (clinical diagnosis). BALAR significantly outperforms all baselines across all three benchmarks, with $14.6\%$ higher accuracy on AR-Bench-DC, $38.5\%$ on AR-Bench-SP, and $30.5\%$ on iCraft-MD.

</details>


### [274] [Intelligent CCTV for Urban Design: AI-Based Analysis of Soft Infrastructure at Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05402)
*Vinit Katariya,Seungjin Kim,Curtis Craig,Nichole Morris,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种利用现有CCTV基础设施的AI分析框架，评估临时行人安全岛和路缘延伸等软性干预措施对车速和安全的影响，发现车速最高降低20%，证明软性基础设施的交通稳静化效果及AI方法在交通政策评估中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能和计算机视觉正在变革交通数据收集方式，亟需开发低成本、快速的方法来评估软性交通干预措施（如临时行人安全岛和路缘延伸）对车辆速度和安全的影响，以支持循证的交通政策制定。

Method: 利用深度学习和基于透视的车速估算技术，结合现有的CCTV基础设施，在明尼阿波利斯市对干预措施实施前后的驾驶行为进行评估，并在安装后的第1周和第2周进行重复监测。

Result: 在无信号控制交叉口，平均速度和第85百分位速度分别最高下降18.75%和16.56%，过境交通流量最高减少12.2%；在信号控制交叉口（除一处外），平均速度和第85百分位速度分别最高下降20.0%和17.19%。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了软性基础设施具有显著的交通稳静化效果，同时突显了AI驱动方法在快速、低成本、基于证据的交通政策评估方面的实用价值。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and computer vision are transforming transportation data collection. This study introduces an AI-enabled analytics framework leveraging existing CCTV infrastructure to evaluate the impact of soft interventions, such as temporary pedestrian refuges and curb extensions, on vehicle speed and safety. Using deep learning and perspective-based speed estimation, we evaluated driver behavior before and after interventions, with repeated post-installation monitoring in Week 1 and Week 2, in Minneapolis. Findings reveal that at unsignalized intersections, mean and 85th-percentile speeds fell by up to 18.75% and 16.56%, respectively, while pass-through traffic decreased by as much as 12.2%. Signalized intersections showed comparable reductions except one location, with mean and 85th-percentile speeds dropping by up to 20.0% and 17.19%. These results demonstrate the traffic-calming effectiveness of soft infrastructure and underscore the utility of AI-powered methods for rapid, low-cost, and evidence-based transport policy evaluation.

</details>


### [275] [When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
*Jiechen Li,Catherine A. Barry,Rishika Randev,Janet Chen,Ella Jorgensen,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新定义LLM中的奉承行为（sycophancy）为社会对齐与认知完整性之间的边界失效，提出了三条件识别框架和分类体系，并倡导采用边界感知的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅通过外在行为（如同意错误信念、立场反转等）来操作化奉承行为，这种 formulation 仅捕捉了显性表现，却未能充分界定涉及认知完整性与社会对齐之间更微妙的边界失效问题。作者认为奉承不应仅被理解为简单的“同意”，而是一种取代独立认知判断的对齐行为。

Method: 提出了一个识别奉承行为的三条件框架：1）用户发出信念、偏好或自我概念的线索；2）模型通过对齐行为向该线索偏移；3）这种偏移损害了认知准确性、独立推理或适当的纠正。此外，还构建了一个包含对齐目标、机制和严重程度的分类体系。

Result: 建立了一套更精细的理论框架来区分奉承行为，明确了其作为社会对齐与认知完整性边界失效的本质，并提供了结构化的分类方法来识别和评估不同类型的奉承行为。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了对对齐评估的影响，主张采用边界感知评估、结构化评分标准和缓解策略，并将这些提议与其他奉承行为观点并列讨论，为后续的模型评估与对齐研究提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This position paper argues that sycophancy in LLMs is a boundary failure between social alignment and epistemic integrity. Existing work often operationalizes sycophancy through external behavior such as agreement with incorrect user beliefs, position reversals, or deviation from an objective standard of correctness. These formulations capture only overt forms of the phenomenon and leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified. We argue that sycophancy should not be understood as agreement alone, but as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment. To clarify this boundary, we propose a three-condition framework for sycophancy. First, the user expresses a cue in the form of a belief, preference, or self-concept. Second, the model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior. Third, this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. We also introduce a taxonomy for classifying sycophancy, consisting of alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity. The paper concludes by discussing implications for alignment evaluation and argues for boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies, while situating these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

</details>


### [276] [PRISM: Perception Reasoning Interleaved for Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05407)
*Mohamed Salim Aissi,Clemence Grislain,Clement Romac,Laure Soulier,Mohamed Chetouani,Olivier Sigaud,Nicolas Thome*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRISM框架，通过动态问答管道紧密耦合视觉语言模型（VLM）和大语言模型（LLM），有效弥合了具身智能体在多模态场景中的感知-推理-决策差距，在ALFWorld和R2R基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基于大语言模型（LLM）的具身智能体从纯文本环境扩展到复杂的多模态设置仍面临重大挑战。现有独立的视觉语言模型（VLM）存在感知-推理-决策差距，经常忽略任务关键信息，导致决策质量下降。

Method: 提出PRISM框架，通过动态问答（DQA）管道紧密耦合感知模块（VLM）和决策模块（LLM）。LLM不再被动接受VLM的描述，而是对其进行批判，提出面向目标的问题，并综合生成紧凑的图像描述。这种闭环交互实现了对场景的精准、任务驱动的理解，且完全自动化，无需人工设计问答对。

Result: 在ALFWorld和Room-to-Room（R2R）基准测试上的实验表明：(1) PRISM显著优于最先进的基于图像的模型；(2) 交互式面向目标的感知管道带来了系统性和实质性的性能提升。

Conclusion: PRISM通过闭环的感知-决策交互机制，有效解决了VLM独立使用时忽略任务关键信息的问题，实现了场景的深度任务驱动理解，为构建高性能的多模态具身智能体提供了一种高效且全自动的解决方案。

Abstract: Scaling LLM-based embodied agents from text-only environments to complex multimodal settings remains a major challenge. Recent work identifies a perception-reasoning-decision gap in standalone Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which often overlook task-critical information. In this paper, we introduce PRISM, a framework that tightly couples perception (VLM) and decision (LLM) through a dynamic question-answer (DQA) pipeline. Instead of passively accepting the VLM's description, the LLM critiques it, probes the VLM with goal-oriented questions, and synthesizes a compact image description. This closed-loop interaction yields a sharp, task-driven understanding of the scene. We evaluate PRISM on the ALFWorld and Room-to-Room (R2R) benchmarks. We show that: (1) PRISM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art image-based models, (2) our Interactive goal-oriented perception pipeline yields systematic and substantial gains, and (3) PRISM is fully automatic, eliminating the need for handcrafted questions or answers.

</details>


### [277] [FinRAG-12B: A Production-Validated Recipe for Grounded Question Answering in Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05482)
*Denys Katerenchuk,Pablo Duboue,Keelan Evanini,David Gondek,Nithin Govindugari,Olivier Allauzen,Joshua Baptiste,David J More,Joshua Schechter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向银行领域的统一、数据高效的大语言模型训练框架，通过数据生成流水线、校准拒答机制及端到端部署方法，在仅使用143M token的条件下，实现了优于GPT-4.1的答案质量和引用准确率，并在40多家金融机构部署，显著提升了查询解决率，同时大幅降低了响应延迟和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在多领域快速应用，但在银行业因对高准确性、监管合规性及可验证、有依据回复的要求，推广受阻。需要一种能在实际部署约束下，优化答案质量、引用依据和校准拒答能力的领域专用LLM训练框架。

Method: 提出一个统一、数据高效的框架：1）数据生成流水线，结合LLM-as-a-Judge过滤、引用标注和课程学习，仅用143M token；2）校准拒答机制，通过在22%不可回答样本上训练，实现合理的“我不知道”率；3）端到端方法论，涵盖从数据整理到量化服务的全过程。

Result: 训练得到的12B参数模型在答案质量上超越GPT-4.1，引用准确率优于GPT-4.1，相比未调优基线有适度的引用折中；拒答率为12%，优于基线不安全的4.3%，避免GPT-4.1的过度拒答（20.2%）。系统已在40多家金融机构部署，查询解决率提升7.1个百分点（p < 0.001），响应速度提升3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍。

Conclusion: 该框架在数据效率、答案质量、引用依据和拒答校准方面取得显著成效，成功满足了银行业的高标准要求，并在实际部署中证明了其有效性和经济性，为领域专用LLM的开发与部署提供了可行范例。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly being adopted across various domains. However, their adoption in banking industry faces resistance due to demands for high accuracy, regulatory compliance, and the need for verifiable and grounded responses. We present a unified, data-efficient framework for training grounded domain-specific LLMs that optimizes answer quality, citation grounding, and calibrated refusal under real-world deployment constraints. First, we describe a data generation pipeline that combines LLM-as-a-Judge filtering, citation annotation, and curriculum learning with only 143M tokens. The resulting 12B model achieves high answer quality outperforming GPT-4.1 on citation grounding, with a modest citation tradeoff versus the untuned base. Second, we propose a calibrated refusal mechanism: training on 22% unanswerable examples yield a 12% "I don't know" rate, substantially improving over the base model's unsafe 4.3% rate while avoiding GPT-4.1's over-refusal (20.2%). Third, we present an end-to-end methodology spanning from data curation to quantized serving. The system is deployed at 40+ financial institutions, achieving a 7.1 percentage point improvement in query resolution (p < 0.001). Additionally, the model delivers 3-5x faster responses at 20-50x lower cost compared to GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [278] [Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Financial Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05409)
*Yang Shu,Yingmin Liu,Zequn Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FinAgent-RAG框架，通过迭代检索-推理循环、对比检索器、程序化思维模块和自适应策略路由，解决了金融文档问答中的多步数值推理难题，在三个基准数据集上准确率提升5.62-9.32个百分点，同时降低41.3%的API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成（RAG）方法采用单次检索-生成范式，难以应对金融文档问答中普遍存在的组合式推理链和跨异构证据（表格、文本、脚注）的复杂多步数值推理需求。

Method: 提出FinAgent-RAG智能体RAG框架，包含三个核心创新：(1)对比金融检索器，通过困难负样本挖掘区分语义相似但数值不同的段落；(2)思维程序推理模块，生成可执行Python代码进行精确算术运算；(3)自适应策略路由器，根据问题复杂度动态分配计算资源。框架采用迭代检索-推理循环与自验证机制。

Result: 在FinQA、ConvFinQA和TAT-QA三个基准数据集上分别达到76.81%、78.46%和74.96%的执行准确率，相比最强基线提升5.62-9.32个百分点。自适应策略路由在FinQA上降低41.3%的API成本且保持准确率。消融实验、跨骨干LLM评估和部署成本分析验证了框架的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FinAgent-RAG框架有效解决了金融文档数值推理的精度和成本问题，具备良好的鲁棒性和实际部署价值，为金融机构提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Financial document question answering (QA) demands complex multi-step numerical reasoning over heterogeneous evidence--structured tables, textual narratives, and footnotes--scattered across corporate filings. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approaches adopt a single-pass retrieve-then-generate paradigm that struggles with the compositional reasoning chains prevalent in financial analysis. We propose FinAgent-RAG, an agentic RAG framework that orchestrates iterative retrieval-reasoning loops with self-verification, specifically engineered for the precision requirements of financial numerical reasoning. The framework integrates three domain-specific innovations: (1) a Contrastive Financial Retriever trained with hard negative mining to distinguish semantically similar but numerically distinct financial passages, (2) a Program-of-Thought reasoning module that generates executable Python code for precise arithmetic rather than relying on error-prone LLM-based mental computation, and (3) an Adaptive Strategy Router that dynamically allocates computational resources based on question complexity, reducing API costs by 41.3% on FinQA while preserving accuracy. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets--FinQA, ConvFinQA, and TAT-QA--demonstrate that FinAgent-RAG achieves 76.81%, 78.46%, and 74.96% execution accuracy respectively, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.62--9.32 percentage points. Ablation studies, cross-backbone evaluation with four LLMs, and deployment cost analysis confirm the framework's robustness and practical viability for financial institutions.

</details>


### [279] [Retrieval-Conditioned Topology Selection with Provable Budget Conservation for Multi-Agent Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05657)
*Abhijit Talluri,Pujith Anne,Bhagavan Choudary Pendiyala,Raghavendra Chilukuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RGAO架构，通过提取代码结构复杂度向量来动态选择多智能体LLM系统的编排拓扑，结合复杂度条件路由与形式资源代数，实现了预算可证明守恒的动态拓扑选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM代码生成系统面临根本性的路由问题：最优编排拓扑依赖于被修改代码的结构复杂度，但现有系统在选择拓扑时并未考虑代码库本身，导致路由决策缺乏针对性。

Method: 提出检索引导自适应编排(RGAO)架构：从分层代码索引中提取结构复杂度向量，基于此选择编排拓扑；在Code-Agent多智能体框架中实现，子智能体由六维预算向量的形式契约管理；结合复杂度条件LLM路由和形式资源代数两个独立研究方向。

Result: 复杂度条件拓扑路由器将代理测量的错误路由率从30.1%降低至8.2%；提出预算代数及结构归纳守恒定理；实现亚毫秒级DAG构建和线性树索引可扩展性。

Conclusion: 成功将复杂度条件路由与形式资源代数相结合，获得了单一方法无法实现的特性：在检索条件动态拓扑选择下的可证明预算守恒，为多智能体LLM系统提供了理论基础和实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems for code generation face a fundamental routing problem: the optimal orchestration topology depends on the structural complexity of the code under modification, yet existing systems select topologies without consulting the codebase. We present Retrieval-Guided Adaptive Orchestration (RGAO), an architecture that closes this loop by extracting a structural complexity vector from a hierarchical code index before selecting the orchestration topology. RGAO operates within Code-Agent, a multi-agent framework whose sub-agents are governed by formal contracts with six-dimensional budget vectors. Our headline contribution is the composition of two previously separate lines of work -- complexity-conditioned LLM routing and formal resource algebras -- yielding a property neither admits alone: provable budget conservation under retrieval-conditioned dynamic topology selection. Concretely we contribute: (1) a complexity-conditioned topology router that reduces proxy-measured misrouting from 30.1% to 8.2%; (2) a budget algebra with a structural-induction conservation theorem; and (3) a hierarchical code retrieval engine. Empirical evaluation demonstrates sub-millisecond DAG construction and linear tree-index scalability.

</details>


### [280] [LaTA: A Drop-in, FERPA-Compliant Local-LLM Autograder for Upper-Division STEM Coursework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05410)
*Jesse A. Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LaTA，一个完全在本地硬件上运行的开源自动评分系统，利用大语言模型对LaTeX格式作业进行评分，在约200名学生课程中实现了0.02-0.04%的评分错误率，学生考试成绩提升8-11%，学习信心显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的自动评分系统通常依赖第三方API，违反FERPA隐私法规并使机构面临数据风险，同时需要对作业进行大量修改。教育机构迫切需要一个可本地部署、保护学生隐私、且能无缝融入现有LaTeX工作流程的自动评分解决方案。

Method: LaTA采用四阶段流水线（摄入、分段、评分、报告），在本地商用硬件上运行开源权重的思维链LLM评分器（gpt-oss:120b）。系统将学生作业与教师编写的参考解答进行对比，应用YAML格式的评分细则进行二元逐项评分。整个系统为开源（AGPLv3），可作为现有课程的直接替代方案。

Result: 在Oregon State University的ME 373课程中部署，约200名学生的每周作业在单台Mac Studio上以零边际成本完成评分，每份作业耗时1-3分钟。评分错误率稳定在0.02-0.04%每评分项。与传统评分的对照组相比，LaTA评分组期中考试成绩提高约11%，期末考试提高约8%。学生在所有学习目标上的自信心显著提升（N=159，Δ≥+1.49 Likert分，p<10^-27）。

Conclusion: LaTA成功证明了大语言模型自动评分系统可以在完全本地化的条件下高效运行，兼顾隐私保护与评分质量。该系统不仅大幅降低了评分负担，还通过允许作业重交和扩展答疑时间，显著提升了学生的学习成绩和自信心，为STEM课程的自动评分提供了可行的开源解决方案。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) graders promise to relieve the grading burden of upper-division STEM courses, but most deployments to date send student work to third-party APIs, violating FERPA and exposing institutions to data risk while requiring substantial assignment modification. We present $\textbf{LaTA}\ (\textit{LaTeX Teaching Assistant})$, a drop-in, open-source autograder that runs entirely on commodity on-premises hardware and assumes a LaTeX-native workflow already adopted by many engineering and physics courses. LaTA implements a four-stage pipeline (ingest, segment, grade, report) using a locally hosted open-weight chain-of-thought LLM grader (gpt-oss:120b) that compares student work to an instructor-authored reference solution and applies a YAML rubric with binary per-item scoring. We deployed LaTA in Winter~2026 in ME 373 (Mechanical Engineering Methods) at Oregon State University, grading every weekly assignment for approximately 200 students on a single Mac Studio at \$0 marginal cost per assignment and 1--3 minutes of wall-clock time per submission, enabling regrading of corrected assignments and greatly expanded TA office hour offerings. The instructor-confirmed grading-error rate held at roughly $0.02$--$0.04\%$ per rubric line item across the term. Relative to the same instructor's previous traditionally-graded cohort, the LaTA-graded cohort outperformed by approximately $11\%$ on the midterm exam and $8\%$ on the final exam, and reported large gains in self-assessed confidence on every stated learning objective ($N = 159$ survey responses, $Δ\geq +1.49$ Likert points, $p < 10^{-27}$ on every comparison). We release the code under AGPLv3.

</details>


### [281] [BioResearcher: Scenario-Guided Multi-Agent for Translational Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05985)
*Remigiusz Kinas,Joanna Krawczyk,Rafał Powalski,Przemysław Pietrzak,Agnieszka Kowalewska,Krzysztof Kolmus,Maciej Sypetkowski,Łukasz Smoliński,Tomasz Jetka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ingenix BioResearcher，一种面向转化医学的场景引导多智能体系统，通过版本化研究流程、工具协作和多模型 reconciliation 机制，在单元级能力、开放式推理和端到端临床发现任务上均取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转化医学需要整合文献、临床试验、专利和多组学数据，同时保持标识符、不确定性和可追溯性。现有的通用基础模型和工具增强/多智能体系统无法满足这一需求，它们倾向于给出单次答案或开放性执行，缺乏可审计的、场景特定的工作流程，难以处理异构的生物医学数据源。

Method: 提出Ingenix BioResearcher系统，采用场景引导的多智能体架构：将查询映射到版本化的研究流程（playbooks），调度30+工具和机器学习端点的专业子智能体，混合结构化数据库访问与沙盒代码进行基因组规模分析，在编辑整合前应用claim级别的多模型调和（reconciliation）机制。

Result: 在109项单步测试中达到83.49%通过率和0.892平均分；在BixBench-Verified-50上达到89.33%，在BaisBench Scientific Discovery上获得最高0.758平均分；在30查询的端到端临床基准测试中达到最高的阳性命中率（74.7%±3.3%）和阴性清除率（96.8%±0.2%），在单元级、开放式和端到端临床评估中均表现优异。

Conclusion: Ingenix BioResearcher通过场景引导的多智能体架构，成功解决了转化医学中异构数据整合的挑战，在多个评估维度上展现出广泛且具有竞争力的性能，为生物医学发现提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Translational medicine turns underspecified development goals into evidence synthesis that must combine literature, trials, patents, and quantitative multi-omics analysis while preserving identifiers, uncertainty, and retrievable provenance. General-purpose foundation models and off-the-shelf tool-augmented or multi-agent systems are not built for this: they tend to produce single-shot answers or run open-endedly, and fall short on the auditable, scenario-specific workflows that heterogeneous biomedical sources demand.
  This paper introduces Ingenix BioResearcher, a scenario-guided multi-agent system that maps queries to versioned research playbooks, delegates to specialized subagents over 30+ tools and machine-learning endpoints, mixes structured database access with sandboxed code for genome-scale analyses, and applies claim-level multi-model reconciliation before editorial assembly.
  We evaluate BioResearcher across unit-level capabilities, open-ended biomedical reasoning, and end-to-end clinical discovery. It leads evaluated baselines on 109 single-step tests (83.49% pass rate; 0.892 average score), achieves strong biomedical benchmark performance (89.33% on BixBench-Verified-50 and the top 0.758 mean score on BaisBench Scientific Discovery), and leads on a 30-query clinical end-to-end benchmark with the highest positive hit rate (74.7% $\pm$ 3.3%) and negative clear rate (96.8% $\pm$ 0.2%). These results show broad, competitive performance across unit-level, open-ended, and end-to-end clinical evaluations.

</details>


### [282] [From History to State: Constant-Context Skill Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05413)
*Haoyang Xie,Xinyuan Wang,Yancheng Wang,Puda Zhao,Feng Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种常量上下文技能学习框架，通过将可复用的程序性知识从提示中转移到轻量级任务模块权重中，在保持性能的同时显著降低了推理成本并保护了隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体面临隐私-成本-能力的权衡困境：云端模型能力强但会暴露敏感的中间上下文；本地模型保护隐私但可靠性不足。此外，两种设置都需要为冗长的技能提示和不断增长的历史记录重复支付高昂的token成本。

Method: 提出constant-context skill learning框架：将可复用的代理工作流学习到轻量级任务族模块中，推理时仅依赖当前观测和紧凑状态块。确定性跟踪器从任务进度生成状态块并提供对齐的子目标奖励，每个模块可通过步级SFT训练并通过在线RL精炼。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld基准测试中，基于Qwen3-4B/8B和Llama-3.1-8B的智能体表现优异。其中Qwen3-8B模型在ALFWorld达到89.6%未见任务成功率，WebShop达76.8%成功率，SciWorld达66.4%未见任务成功率，性能匹配或超越已发表的强基线，同时将每轮提示token减少2-7倍。

Conclusion: 证明了程序性上下文可以从提示转移到权重中，在保持强性能的同时有效降低了推理成本，为构建高效且隐私友好的个人助手智能体提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to operate browsers, files, code and tools, making personal assistants a natural deployment target. Yet personal agents face a privacy-cost-capability tension: cloud models execute multi-step workflows well but expose sensitive intermediate context to external APIs, while local models preserve privacy but remain less reliable. Both settings also pay repeatedly for long skill prompts and growing histories. We propose constant-context skill learning, a context-to-weights framework for recurring agent workflows: reusable procedures are learned in lightweight task-family modules, while inference conditions only on the current observation and a compact state block. A deterministic tracker renders this state block from task progress and supplies aligned subgoal rewards, so each module can be trained with step-level SFT and refined through online RL. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, our agents achieve strong performance across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B. With Qwen3-8B, SFT+RL reaches 89.6\% unseen success on ALFWorld, 76.8\% success on WebShop, and 66.4\% unseen success on SciWorld. They match or exceed strong published agent-training results while reducing prompt tokens per turn by 2--7$\times$ relative to controlled ReAct prompting baselines, showing that procedural context can be moved from prompts into weights.

</details>


### [283] [SANEmerg: An Emergent Communication Framework for Semantic-aware Agentic AI Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05861)
*Yong Xiao,Haoran Zhou,Yujie Zhou,Marwan Krunz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SANEmerg框架，一种面向语义感知AgentNet系统的多智能体涌现通信方法。通过带宽自适应重要性过滤器和基于MDL的复杂度正则化器，在严格带宽约束下实现协作智能体间高效通信协议的涌现，显著提升任务准确性并降低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来网络系统将演变为原生AI智能体生态系统，大量异构专用AI智能体需要实时协作完成复杂用户需求。然而，传统网络范式将通信与计算刚性解耦，导致大规模智能体网络(AgentNet)系统效率低下。涌现通信为解决这一问题提供了新思路，但如何在带宽受限环境下使计算受限的智能体间涌现出高效通信协议仍是挑战。

Method: 提出SANEmerg框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)带宽自适应重要性过滤器，动态优先传输贡献度更高的消息维度，确保带宽受限环境下的鲁棒性能；(2)基于最小描述长度(MDL)原则的复杂度正则化器，促进计算受限信令协议的涌现。框架能够自动检测、推断用户语义意图，并将其关联到分配给智能体集合的子任务集。

Result: 通过AgentNet原型和大量实验验证，SANEmerg相比最先进方案取得显著性能提升：实现了更优的任务准确性，同时大幅降低带宽和计算开销。框架成功在物理限制约束下实现了协作任务完成的通信涌现。

Conclusion: SANEmerg框架有效解决了AgentNet系统中通信与计算解耦导致的效率问题，证明了涌现通信在语义感知网络系统中的可行性和优越性。该方法为构建高效、可扩展的AI原生网络生态系统提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Future networking systems are envisioned to become part of an agentic AI-native ecosystem in which a vast number of heterogeneous and specialized AI agents cooperate seamlessly to fulfill complex user requirements in real time. However, traditional networking paradigms are characterized by a rigid decoupling of communication and computation, which often leads to significant inefficiencies in large-scale agentic AI networking (AgentNet) systems. Emergent communication offers a novel solution by enabling autonomous agents that support task-specific signaling protocols for information exchange and collaborative coordination. In this paper, we consider a multi-agent emergent communication framework, tailored for semantic-aware AgentNet systems in which the user's semantic intent can be automatically detected, inferred, and linked to a set of sub-tasks to be assigned to a set of agents. We investigate how communication and signaling protocols can emerge among collaborative agents with computationally bounded intelligence under stringent bandwidth constraints. Our proposed framework, called SANEmerg, is designed to facilitate the emergence of communication for collaborative task fulfillment while adhering to the physical limits of AgentNet. SANEmerg incorporates a bandwidth-adaptable importance-filter that dynamically prioritizes the transmission of higher-contribution message dimensions, ensuring robust performance in bandwidth-limited environments. Furthermore, SANEmerg integrates a complexity-regularizer grounded in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle to facilitate the emergence of computationally bounded signaling. Evaluated via an AgentNet prototype and extensive experimentation, SANEmerg demonstrates significant performance improvements over state-of-the-art solutions, achieving superior task accuracy while significantly reducing bandwidth and computational overhead.

</details>


### [284] [From Agent Loops to Deterministic Graphs: Execution Lineage for Reproducible AI-Native Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06365)
*Josh Rosen,Seth Rosen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“执行谱系”这一基于DAG的执行模型，用于解决大语言模型智能体工作流中隐式状态导致的维护难题，在文档更新任务中实现了变更隔离与状态一致性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体工作流严重依赖隐式对话状态，导致难以保留稳定的工作产物、隔离无关更新或通过中间产物传播变更，缺乏可维护性。

Method: 引入执行谱系模型，将AI原生工作表示为具有显式依赖关系、稳定中间边界和基于身份重放的有向无环图（DAG），使AI生成的工作在变更下可维护。

Result: 在无关分支更新任务中，DAG重放在所有运行中完整保留最终备忘录，零变动且零污染；在中间产物编辑中，仅DAG重放实现了上游保留、下游传播、未受影响产物保留及跨产物一致性。

Conclusion: 最终答案质量与维护状态质量是两个不同维度，循环基线在单次任务上可能表现良好但掩盖状态不一致，执行谱系为变更隔离、稳定保留与演进提供了更强保证。

Abstract: Large language model systems are increasingly deployed as agentic workflows that interleave reasoning, tool use, memory, and iterative refinement. These systems are effective at producing answers, but they often rely on implicit conversational state, making it difficult to preserve stable work products, isolate irrelevant updates, or propagate changes through intermediate artifacts.
  We introduce execution lineage: an execution model in which AI-native work is represented as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of artifact-producing computations with explicit dependencies, stable intermediate boundaries, and identity-based replay. The goal is not to make the model a better one-shot writer, but to make evolving AI-generated work maintainable under change.
  We compare execution-lineage replay against loop-centric update baselines on two controlled policy-memo update tasks. In an unrelated-branch update, DAG replay preserved the final memo exactly in all runs, with zero churn and zero unrelated-branch contamination, while loop baselines regenerated the memo and frequently imported unrelated context. In an intermediate-artifact edit, all systems reflected the new constraint in the final memo, but only DAG replay achieved perfect upstream preservation, downstream propagation, unaffected-artifact preservation, and cross-artifact consistency.
  These results show that final answer quality and maintained-state quality are distinct. Strong loop baselines can remain competitive at producing polished final outputs when the task is a bounded synthesis/update problem and all current sources fit in context, but immediate task success can mask partial state inconsistency that may compound over future revisions.
